So...

Chung Myung’s eyes were dazed in an unbelievable situation.

‘I have to get up again...’

He slowly lowered his head and looked at his body. No, he tried to look. But his head didn’t move at all, as if it was stuck to the bed.

This was because the rope that tied his head was tight.

Peng.

The band, which was so tight that almost no blood could flow, was putting pressure on his head.

“...”

Chung Myung’s head moved slightly, very slightly to the side. Yoon Jong, who was standing next to the bed, met his earnest eyes, flinched, and nodded slightly.

“Ahem.”

Yoon Jong cleared his throat slightly and opened his mouth cautiously.

“That... Soso.”

“Uh?”

“... I don’t think it is necessary to go this far.”

“What?”

“... No, I just...”

A bluish-black vein stood out on Tang Soso’s forehead.

“Do these people really think the human body is something simple? Do you think if you roll around and then replace some wheels, the body will run fine again? What? You want me to change something?”

“... no. That was never my intention. I wasn’t thinking straight.”

Yoon Jong quickly took a step back due to the killing intent of Tang Soso.

Usually, Tang Soso looked very pleasing to the eyes with her bright smile. Still, when she smiled at the patients, she had a killing intent, as if she wanted to kill someone with just her eyes.

‘No, if one thought about it, shouldn’t it be the other way around? Patients are to be cared for with laughter and compassion...’

At that time, Tang Soso turned her head and glared at Chung Myung. She gritted her teeth as she looked at Chung Myung, tied up with thick rope and pinned to the bed.

“If your body turns to rags, you will receive treatment quietly! Can you not stand anymore and crawl out? Are you still human? Uh?”

“I am your sahyung...”

“I know that too!”

Yes.

He was glad that she knew it.

“No! How on earth did this person end up like this? You go looking for alcohol the moment you open your eyes, even though you have wounds that could have killed you ten times over as if they didn’t belong to you!”

“I didn’t go looking for alcohol...”

“Shut up!”

“... Yes.”

Chung Myung looked at the ceiling with blank eyes.

For some reason, it reminded him of the old times.

-No, if you are hurt, you get treatment, you moron! Come and see!

-Get away from me, you quack.

-Quack! What does this bastard think of a Sichuan Tang family elder? If I had started a physician’s place, I would have made a lot of money! I told you to stop speaking nonsense and come here. No, don’t drink it! I brought that to disinfect the wound!

‘Tang Bo...’

He still lives like this.

Why did his family always treat him like this...

Usually, even if a so-called warrior was stabbed and bled profusely, they would just apply something and say it would get better in time. But these Tang Family bastards were the exception; they used such evil measures to kill people who had mere scratches on their bodies, too.

In the past, these people were annoying patients to death, but now they continue to do...

At that time, the door burst open, and a person walked in like salvation.

“Hmm?”

It was Tang Gunak. He frowned, looking at Chung Myung tied to the bed.

“Oh my. Family lord!”

When Chung Myung shouted as if asking to be rescued, Tang Gunak’s eyes darkened.

“Who did this?”

Tang Soso, pricked by his cold voice, looked at Tang Gunak. She knew well how much her father cared for Chung Myung.

Tang Gunak was a person who had never shown the same kindness he showed to Chung Myung, even to his kids. In his eyes, the sight of Tang Soso, the sajae of Chung Myung, playfully tying up Chung Myung couldn’t have looked good, even if it was by his favorite daughter.

“U-Uhm...”

“Uh?”

“... Yes.”

Tang Soso shrugged her shoulders.

Tang Gunak, who was staring at her with cold eyes, scolded her.

“Did I teach you this?”

“Sorry. Sahyung was being disobedient.”

“You tied up Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon with rope! What will the world say when they see this?”

Tang Gunak clicked his tongue and held out something he was holding in the back of his hand.

“How can something like this happen?”

“...”

What he held in his hand was an iron rope. For a moment, Tang Soso’s face turned blank.

“It is a specially made iron rope, so it should be able to subdue the injured. Tie it back properly.”

“...”

“Hurry up.”

“Yes.”

Tang Soso quickly accepted the iron rope held out by Tang Gunak. Chung Myung watched the incredible scene with shocked eyes.

Tang Gunak clicked his tongue and said.

“Well, a warrior... they need to know that the body needs some time to recover!”

Tears flowed down the eyes of Chung Myung.

‘Damn these bastards.’

There was the same uproar from generation to generation. From generation to generation...

No, did this family have some motto? Why were they saying the same thing after 100 years too?

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak approached Chung Myung and looked around.

“It is difficult to pinpoint internal bleeding with needles alone. Let us use this medicine along with it.”

“He doesn’t eat it well.”

“... Just force it down.”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung forcibly turned his head and looked at Baek Cheon.

“Dong-Ryong.”

“What?”

“Go and get the sect leader. The Tang Family is persecuting Mount Hua disciples...”

“I think he will applaud?”

The tears that had stopped flowed down Chung Myung’s temples again.

‘There is no one on my side. There is no one on my side.’

Sect leader sahyung.

He missed the old days...

-It was the same way back in the day, you bastard. You were the same then, too.

“...”

Ah, he was lonely, so lonely.

At that time, Tang Gunak sighed and asked.

“How are you feeling?”

“Can you not see?”

“It looks good.”

“... but this guy?”

Chung Myung’s body was shaking.

“Any inconveniences?”

“You think there aren’t any!”

“I don’t think there are any.”

“... can you not hear me?”

Chung Myung stared at Tang Gunak with a face full of anger, then sighed.

“... it is so uncomfortable to eat like this, so please relax a little just when I am going to eat food.”

“Well, that can be done.”

Tang Gunak looked at Tang Soso and said,

“What about meals?”

“I’m feeding him from time to time.”

“It means he is eating rice. Change it to porridge. Eating rice and fried food isn’t good for patients. Make sure to only give food three meals a day.”

“...”

Chung Myung looked up at the ceiling with a sad heart.

Sect leader sahyung.

... He was so lonely.

-hehehe.

Yah, you bastard!

Do not laugh!

Rumors traveled faster than a storm.

“Server! Here, bring me another bottle.”

“Yes! I am bringing it now!”

It was time for those sitting around the guesthouse to pour the last drink remaining in the bottle.

The door burst open with a sweaty man rushing in.

“Here. Here! Why are you coming in such a hurry?”

“It looks like you were scared that we would drink all the alcohol you ordered.”

There was loud laughter. The man, who had been looking around at the sound of people calling, ran over and shouted loudly.

“D-did you hear this?”

“What?”

“The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families signed a non-aggression pact with the Evil Faction!”

Those who heard it burst into laughter.

“Stop talking nonsense and sit down. I ordered a new bottle.”

“It isn’t a joke, I am telling you!”

“Right. Right.”

“It is chaos outside! The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families declared that they wouldn’t set foot in the Yangtze River for the next three years!”

The faces of those shouting went pale as they heard it, but soon, someone clicked their tongue.

“Tsk, tsk. Just some jokes. Why on earth would the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families sign a treaty with those bastards?”

“W-well, it isn’t just the Evil Faction. The Shenzhou Five Clans had come together and created a new Four Precepts Alliance!’

“Four Precepts Alliance?”

“Right! It is said that the Shaolin and Wudang who went to the Yangtze River were unable to escape the attack of the Four Precepts Alliance and made peace!”

“... The Evil Faction bastards have united as one?”

“Right!”

“Ah, that is ridiculous. No matter how many of the Evil Faction bastards gather in one place, how can you say Shaolin and Wudang are at peace with them! We are going to be in big trouble!”

The man pounded his chest as if he were frustrated.

“If you don’t believe me, just go out and see! It is chaos outside! The entire world is in an uproar now!”

“... what nonsense...”

Everyone went speechless at that.

Looking at that desperate face, he didn’t think of it as a lie, but no none could believe it either.

How could the Justice Faction and Evil Faction make peace?

They had never heard such a story before. Wasn’t love and hate separated because they couldn’t mix like water and oil? But how can those two?

“W-wait. Did you say non-aggression?”

“Yes!”

“The Nine Sects and Five Families said they would no longer set foot on the Yangtze River? Then what about the Yangtze River clans already situated there?”

“Everyone is leaving the Yangtze River.”

“The Nine Sects and Five Families have turned the Yangtze River into an infested area and are retreating, leaving behind their enemies? Well, then, what happens to the civilians there?”

“Who knows?”

“Ha... haha... ha. Is it even possible for something like this to happen?”

Finally, a roar erupted from the mouths of those who had been holding back.

“If that’s the case, we might as well lose it. They attacked out of curiosity and then just ran with their tails curled? As if that wasn’t enough, they signed a humiliating agreement to not step foot in the Yangtze River for the next three years!”

The faces of those in the room were filled with despair. They were talking so loudly that it was impossible not to listen.

“Are you saying this because you don’t know how those pirates will act? The pirates have done this so many times that the people are going insane. So, who will handle all that? How can the so-called Justice Faction do such a ridiculous thing?”

“I cannot believe it! The Shaolin I know and the Wudang I know aren’t such places!”

There were violent reactions here and there, but that didn’t stop the people from erupting.

“It doesn’t matter whether you believe it or not. I heard that the people around the Yangtze River are already packing their bags. Now that there are no Nine Great Sects or Five Great Families, there is no one who can stop the Evil Faction. Many would rather abandon their hometown than die there.”

“Ha... haha... ha.”

It was unbelievable news.

But there was no way not to believe it. Even as they were talking, some people turned thoughtful and burst into the room, discussing the same news.

“Oh my god... who would have thought that Shaolin, Wudang, and the Namgung family would abandon the civilians and find their own way... so what was the deal they had been clamoring for so far? And yet they pride themselves on being the Justice Faction?”

“Why have we supported them until now! If this is the case, aren’t they no different from the thieves?”

“Those guys are fucking scammers, scammers! In the first place, they were just people who were only interested in money and gains! We got fooled. Entirely fooled!”

Anger began to spread.

People naturally felt more anger when they were betrayed by those they trusted than when wronged by those known for evil. As great as the trust placed in the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families was, the disappointment and anger over their choices were enormous.

Once the rumor spread from the Yangtze River to each province, it quickly engulfed the entire central plain like wildfire.

More than a hundred years after the war against the Demonic Sect, the most shocking news shook the quiet Central Plains like an earthquake.

The abbot remained silent for a long time, his face expressionless.

Byeop Gye couldn’t bear to meet his gaze and lowered his head.

“... Is everything you said true?”

“Abbot...”

Byeop Gye could only groan in response, unable to find words. Even with ten mouths, there was nothing to say as the expression went.

“Huh...”

The abbot laughed incredulously.

“You signed an agreement with the Evil Faction? A unilateral agreement that prevents only one side from advancing into the Southlands, including the Yangtze River?”

“...”

“How...”

He couldn’t even finish the sentence and fell silent.

‘It was my mistake.’

It wasn’t just about sending Byeop Gye. If he was dispatching most of the Shaolin disciples, he should have gone himself. At best, it was a matter of dealing with numerous pirates, and it wasn’t something Byeop Gye should have been sent for.

“Oh, this foolish person. How are we going to fix this... Amitabha. Amitabha.”

The abbot mumbled, shaking his head repeatedly. Byeop Gye watched with the feeling of swallowing a stone.

“Abbot... If something goes wrong, please remove me.”

“Do you mean those words?”

“Yes. Wouldn’t it be better to say I agreed without the abbot’s permission, who had different intentions? It might be difficult for me to show my face, but Shaolin is...”

“Such foolish words!”

The abbot shouted.

“That would only tarnish Shaolin’s name. How would the world view Shaolin if we did something like this just to survive? From now on, words not directly from my mouth would become worthless and reversible? That would undermine Shaolin’s status.”

“...”

“It is an external affair. I understand your feelings, but...”

If it hadn’t been such an unconventional offer, there was no way Jang Ilso would have obediently let go of those who entered his territory.

But...

“How on earth should I resolve this matter?”

If it had been just a year ago, it wouldn’t have been such a big deal. No matter how much they despised the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, if there was no other option, the people would inevitably rely on them again.

But not now.

“I never would have thought that ignoring the establishment of the Heavenly Friends Alliance would lead to this...”

The abbot had supported the Heavenly Friends Alliance despite opposition from other sects. At the time, he did so for various interests. Still, he couldn’t have imagined that his actions would plunge their position into the abyss.

“How could things turn out like this?”

If only he hadn’t tolerated the formation of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

If only Jang Ilso hadn’t organized the Evil Faction into an alliance and set a trap in the Yangtze River.

If only Venerable Heo Do hadn’t made the extreme choice of sacrificing his reputation to save his disciples.

And if only the Four Precepts Alliance hadn’t made such an extreme decision!

Even if just one of these events hadn’t occurred, it might have been something that could have been resolved somehow. But these are all meaningless assumptions. All of this happened in the end, and all that was left was to restore the status of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who were being driven into the ground at this very moment.

“What was the reaction?”

“The sects that didn’t come to the Yangtze River are requesting an explanation. The rumor hasn’t spread widely yet, so public sentiment against us isn’t boiling, but it will soon spread...”

“Right... it has to be. Amitabha. Amitabha...”

He continued to chant, his mind racing with frustration.

Public sentiment was a frightening thing.

The common people usually endured no matter how much persecution they received. Still, the moment they crossed a certain line, they had the strength to rush into the Imperial Palace with simple farming tools.

The moment they became beyond angry at the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, the downfall would begin quickly.

“What about the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“... Uh?”

“What about Mount Hua? What are they up to?”

Byeop Gye looked at the abbot with a face that showed he had no clear understanding and then answered hesitantly.

“We got out of the Yangtze River first and...”

The eyes of the abbot widened in shock.

“You’re saying you didn’t even check what Mount Hua was doing?”

“The situation was....”

“...”

The abbot closed his eyes tightly and sighed inwardly.

‘Ahh... why did this happen...’

If Byeop Gye had enough time to stand back and observe the situation, he would have been keenly aware of the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s movements. It wasn’t Byeop Gye who couldn’t understand the fact that the people who would benefit the most when the Nine Great Sects fell would be Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

But....

‘There is a difference between watching from a distance and recognizing the situation in the middle of a storm.’

The same went for Byeop Gye and Heo Do.

It was true that it was difficult to make a cool-headed decision in a rapidly changing situation. Still, he didn’t expect that he would panic so much as to make such an absurd choice.

A time when there was no war or conflict.

Those who had developed their strength through it might have gotten stronger, but they haven’t matured. Even the abbot didn’t know that he was no different from a strong child.

“Were there any signs of leaving the Yangtze River?”

“... Looking back, we tried to leave quickly, but they didn’t show any signs of preparing.”

“Right... right, it must be. There is no way Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon would miss this chance.”

The abbot laughed in frustration.

“We missed the timing. The rumor that we were fighting with the Precepts Sects Alliance would have already spread through the world. Still, if we were to say that the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Evil Faction made the agreement, it would only sound like nonsense. We lost it. We lost it all. Amitabha...”

The abbot couldn’t hide his despair. The more this happened, the more distorted the face of Byeop Gye became.

“I apologize, Abbot...”

“It wasn’t your fault.”

The abbot shook his head.

Everything had happened. What difference would blaming Byeop Gye make now? What they had to think about was how to somehow resolve what happened.

“To the sects who inquire, please confirm that the treaty is indeed true.”

“How do we...”

“It is better than getting into trouble and avoiding it. Just say that Shaolin will take responsibility for what happened.”

“... Yes. I will.”

“And!”

In an instant, the eyes of the abbot shone.

“It isn’t different because they are all sects. Tell them that we must keep in mind that since this is a treaty made in the name of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families, it must be followed.”

“A-Abbot!”

Byeop Gye’s eyes widened.

“Well, it happened because of our fault. But forcing the others...”

“Do as you are told!”

Byeop Gye stayed silent in response to the abbot’s remark.

The man’s face was so severe that he didn’t even dare to say any persuasive words.

“There will be a lot of backlash.”

“It cannot be helped.”

The abbot’s eyes had turned cold.

“Of course, it isn’t a good thing to lose public support and hear complaints. But it isn’t the worst. The worst thing is that Shaolin loses its control over the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families because of this incident.”

“...”

“Even if we are criticized, we must remain the star of Murim. Suppose they rebel against our decision and act independently. In that case, the entire world will say that Shaolin is no longer the leader of the Nine Great Sects. That must be prevented.”

Cold sweat began to form on Byeop Gye’s forehead.

At this point, he couldn’t help but realize the magnitude of his mistake.

“And...”

“Yes, abbot.”

“Emphasize that the person who decided all of this was Wudang’s Heo Do, and Shaolin will not receive visitors for a month from now.”

“Y-you mean we are closing the gates?”

“No. We just won’t be accepting visitors.”

The abbot sighed.

“You have to avoid the falling rain. Another solution is to remain still until the storm calms down. First, let’s see how public sentiment develops.”

After finishing that, the abbot chanted with an exhausted look.

Those who were angry would inevitably find someone to blame. It was unavoidable that the complaints of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families would be directed at Shaolin. Still, the public’s criticism of Shaolin needed to be prevented.

In that case, he had to come up with the right measure and the right scapegoat.

“Wudang... because of this, Wudang will lose its position. Heo Do. Heo Do... will also...”

The abbot couldn’t continue speaking and closed his eyes.

If one was in a position of leading a sect, one could not help but wonder why Venerable Heo Do made such a choice. Moreover, didn’t they know how the Mount Hua Sect had fallen?

It would also be difficult for the abbot to endure if Shaolin fell like Mount Hua did this time.

“But no matter how much it is, there are limits.”

He understood, but he didn’t understand.

It was then that the abbot sighed and shook his head.

“A-abbot!”

An urgent voice came from outside the door.

“What is happening?”

As the Abbot quickly answered, the door opened, and Byeop Gye hurried inside.

“We-we are in huge trouble.”

“Don’t rush into talking. What is happening?”

“W-Wudang...”

As soon as he heard the word Wudang, the abbot’s face went stiff.

“It is said that Wudang’s leader, Heo Do, has given up his position as sect leader and is going to step down.”

“What?”

The abbot jumped from his seat, his eyes wide, and asked again.

“Is that true?”

“Yes. This news comes directly from Wudang.”

“T-this...”

The abbot’s voice trembled.

He thought they would say something to deflect the anger, but he didn’t expect it would go this far.

‘Is he willing to take full responsibility for the situation?’

This was something that could not be accomplished without immense determination.

It was unprecedented for a sect leader, once they resigned, to take back that position. This meant that Venerable Heo Do was taking full responsibility for the incident and was stepping down from the sect leader position.

“The next sect leader?”

“Heo Sanja, a sajae to Venerable Heo Do, will serve as temporary sect leader for a while.”

The abbot’s face went blank.

“What insanity...”

The most foolish thing a guilty person could do was to accept their guilt. People didn’t know much about what happened there. They would be shocked and angry at the news they heard, but after their anger subsided, they might guess there must have been a good reason for it.

There were quite a few things that the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families had accumulated over the years, so there will definitely be people defending them.

But for Venerable Heo Do to take responsibility for the incident by stepping down from the position of sect leader was ultimately the same as admitting that the Nine Sects and Five Families did make a huge mistake.

“I need to meet Venerable Heo Do in person.”

“... It is difficult, abbot. It is said that Wudang will close its gates for 3 months from now.”

“This...!”

In the end, the abbot’s face had blue veins protruding.

“What is this! Woah. This fool!”

A thunderous shout erupted from the abbot’s mouth, who normally doesn’t get angry.

“Why did it end up like this! Why!”

His fingertips trembled with anger and shock.

It was collapsing.

The order he tried to protect, centered around the Nine Great Sects, was shattering and crumbling at this moment.

‘Are you saying that the difficult times have already arrived?’

The moment the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families lost control, the world would no longer be as peaceful as it was in the past.

It was as if he could see the Central Plains ablaze.

“I am heading to the Yangtze River. I need to meet Mount Hua.”

“... Abbot. We... we cannot go to the Yangtze River.”

At that moment, a look of despair and shock overtook the abbot’s face.

He looked at Byeop Gye in silence and then chuckled mockingly. It was a mockery of the situation.

“Mount Hua’s sect leader... no, the Alliance leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Request a meeting with them.”

“... Yes.”

“As soon as the matter is over, enter the Penance Hall. I order you to meditate there for half a year.”

“I will do as commanded.”

“Huhuhu.”

His carefully built tower had collapsed, as if everything he had worked so hard to build was just a sandcastle.

‘The Four Precepts Alliance and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.’

He closed his eyes and chanted a prayer.

‘It is difficult.’

For the first time, the abbot felt the name of Shaolin was diminishing.

The rumor that the Four Precepts Alliance had signed a non-aggression pact with the Nine Great Sects in the Southlands swept through the central plains like a storm. Even those who initially doubted it ended up believing, their mouths agape as the news spread.

“How can the Justice Faction collude with the Evil Faction?”

“Isn’t it too much to call it colluding?”

“This is colluding, or what else can this be called?”

“Yes, but...”

The news that angered those who heard the rumor the most was that they had abandoned the Yangtze River.

The pirates on the Yangtze River suffered damage due to the war. In such a situation, if the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were not going to set foot on the Yangtze River, wasn’t it obvious the place would turn into chaos?

This was no different from the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families abandoning the people of the Southlands.

People across the realm were angry about it, but the news that followed was even more shocking.

-The person who was at the root of this is Wudang’s Venerable Heo Do, the sect leader.

-Byeop Gye, the elder of Shaolin, Namgung Hwa of the Namgung family, and Byeok Hyun, the sect leader of Qingcheng, sympathized with elder Heo Do’s decision.

-The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families who didn’t go to the Yangtze River will also agree with the treaty.

At first, there was just shock; then it built up to anger, and finally, it turned into hatred. Those who exploded at the news they heard climbed Mount Wudang one after another.

Those who crossed the mountain reached the main sect of Wudang but couldn’t help but be astonished at the sight of the tightly shut gates.

“You caused things to end up like this and then close the gates!”

“There is a limit to shamelessness!”

“Come out! Right now!”

Actually, the situation would have been a little different if the Wudang had greeted the visitors without making them climb up. No matter how wrong things went, there wouldn’t be many people who would openly curse Wudang.

But the tightly shut gate set fire to the hearts of those who were trying to be polite.

“You damn bastards! Even after all this, you call yourselves the Justice faction?”

“You talked about being traitors, and now you abandon the innocent civilians!?”

“It was a waste to spend so many years trusting you!”

Those who flocked to the gate began to throw the items they were holding over the fence.

The first step was usually difficult. As those in front began to curse and throw things, those following them also raised their voices.

The curses went too far, and some people even kicked the closed gate with all their might and spat.

“...”

On the other side, inside the mountain gate, Jin Hyun, who was silently listening to the curses, closed his eyes.

“Jin Hyun.”

Mu Jin carefully placed his hand on his shoulder.

“Let us leave. It will only hurt to stay here.”

“... no, sasuk.”

But Jin Hyun shook his head and held on.

“I need to see clearly. Looking away will not change anything, right?”

“That could be true, but...”

Jin Hyun sighed and looked back at the gate. The sound of the gate slamming and the dirt flying broke his heart.

‘This much...’

He mumbled in a hollow voice.

“Actually, I didn’t expect them to be this cold-hearted. Still, there must have been quite a bit done in the name of Wudang.”

“They aren’t angry because we didn’t do anything; they are angry because of what we did. If there are no expectations, there can be no disappointment, right?”

“... true.”

But those words didn’t comfort Jin Hyun at all. In other words, his disappointment was at its extreme.

“Sasuk.”

“Yes.”

“I know that the choice of sect leader wasn’t wrong. And the fact that all those choices were for us. “

“...”

“But... looking at this now, I wonder if it would have been better for us to die there. It isn’t that much of a disgrace... if I had known that the name of Wudang would fall to the ground like this...”

Mu Jin sighed in frustration.

Why wouldn’t he know the feelings of Jin Hyun?

To them, being a Wudang disciple was an extremely honorable thing.

How could he forget the envious looks that poured in when they walked down the street in Wudang’s robes?

But they wouldn’t get that look anymore.

To them, the Wudang was someone who held hands with the Evil Faction just to live.

Mu Jin sighed and tapped Jin Hyun on the shoulder.

“Life is a waste for everyone.”

“...”

“What is wrong is wrong. But if there is something wrong, we can always make up for it. If the day comes when we establish the Wudang’s name again, then even those who are angry at us will look at us again.”

“... yes, sasuk.”

“Let’s go now.”

“Yes.”

When Mu Jin nodded and turned, Jin Hyun followed him and turned. But he couldn’t move his foot so easily.

Jin Hyun, looked at Mu Jin’s back with a bitter look thought.

‘But Sasuk. There were people there who didn’t regret it.’

Wouldn’t they be afraid of the situation too? No, that couldn’t be true.

There was just one difference between them and Wudang.

Could one carry out that will even in the face of fear?

Were they able to take a step forward even though they were afraid?

That small difference led to such drastic results.

‘This is tough.’

What was righteousness? What was justice?

Things they thought they knew had turned unclear.

The harsh curses from the people were still coming through.

The fact that there was nothing wrong with those curses made him sad.

‘I am sorry.’

He said it with all his heart. It was an apology that wasn’t listened to by anyone.

Although the rumor spread quickly, this time, thanks to the Lower District Clan’s active movement, the word traveled through the world even faster than before.

Everyone who heard it was angry, but among them were people who didn’t have time to be angry.

There were those who lived on the Yangtze River, relying on it as their only means of survival.

Kwaang!

A ship mercilessly rammed into another vessel crossing the Yangtze River. As the ship tilted, chains flew from the attacking ship and connected to one another. Stepping on the chains, the pirates boarded the ship.

“Please spare me!”

Those who saw the pirates fell to the ground in fear.

The pirates on board had such evil looks in their eyes.

“If anyone is hiding even a penny, I will cut the flesh from their bones right here! Give me everything you have! Go and bring out everything!”

“Yes!”

“Give everything? Isn’t that too much? I will prepare a toll fee for you, so please...”

As an old man approached and begged, the captain’s eyes narrowed.

“Who the hell are you?”

“I am the captain of this ship. If you take all of this, we will be ruined. And if we are ruined, those people will...”

Slash!

At that moment, the blade sliced through the captain’s chest.

“ACKKKK!”

The old captain, with his chest torn open, screamed and fell to the ground.

’ACK!“

The person who saw someone being cut by a blade trembled in fear.

“You arrogant bastard, how dare you try to teach me?”

The pirate licked the blood from the blade with his tongue and smiled.

“There are plenty of people who will pay even if you don’t. If you complain one more time, I will kill you and feed you to the fish.”

“If anyone is caught hiding anything, they die on the spot!”

“Move swiftly!”

One by one, people began to open their wallets, their pale faces ready to surrender. If all the money was taken, they would starve to death, but it was better than dying here.

‘How did something like this happen...’

The pirates were more venomous than before. In the past, they managed the Yangtze River in their own way by collecting a toll, but recently, they would only shout and take everything.

Thanks to the destruction of the pirates by the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, the injured pirates were killing people and stealing goods without any hesitation.

‘If this continues, all the merchants traveling up and down the Yangtze will go bankrupt!’

‘What will happen now...?’

‘If they hadn’t come to the Yangtze River, this wouldn’t have happened at all!’

Everyone gritted their teeth inwardly. In a way, this was no source of amusement. Wasn’t the anger towards the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who brought the situation to this point, greater than the anger towards the pirates for robbing and killing people right in front of their eyes?

“We took ’em all. What will you do with these people?”

“Send them away?”

The person who seemed like the captain spoke with a grin.

“The officials won’t come forward unless people die in huge numbers. Wealth is nice. We aren’t people who collect taxes but rob them.”

The faces of the civilians turned pale at those words. It was because there was no mistake in what he said.

“There is no need to scratch and make crumbs. Besides, if you let them go alive, won’t they need to have money to take the ship again? See you then. Next time, if you want to save your life, ride with a huge amount of money. Hahahaha!”

The bandits, who had entirely destroyed the ship, laughed and moved to their own ship and went away.

Those who were looking at the back of the ship began to speak in confusion and fear.

“... I am ruined. I spent all my money on this trip... if this happens, even if I return, I will have to starve to death!”

“Starve to death? I owe people money! Now I have a store that will be sold!”

“Damn it! Until recently, such a thing never happened!”

“What kind of situation are we in because of those Nine Sects!”

They boarded the ship carelessly, even though the Yangtze River was dangerous. They knew it was risky, but they needed to get on a ship to make a living.

Those who relied on the Yangtze River as their lifeline had no choice but to make a living from it. Even though they knew it was dangerous, it was difficult to find another way to survive.

“... How are we supposed to live?”

“Ahuk.”

This ship wasn’t the only one experiencing this. A similar situation was occurring across the Yangtze River.

Those who had suffered extensive damage were plundering the ships and filling their stomachs with the permission of the Black Dragon King.

Normally, when they felt that the atrocities were worsening, common people would run to the Justice faction and ask for help. Still, now they had no choice but to endure the tyranny.

“Captain, are you alright?”

“Here, get a clean cloth! Right now!”

The sailors were busy checking the condition of the captain, who had fallen to the ground. The warriors who saw the captain’s pale face were overcome with despair.

“I will rip that Heo Do bastard to death!”

“Is Wudang the only problem? Namgung and Shaolin were there too! They are all the same, be it Nine Sects or Five Families, they are all the same!”

“When have those self-indulgent bastards cared about us? What good will talking do? They look like people who will die in shit! Those people are as bad as the pirates!”

“Spit! Fucking dirt!”

The ship turned helplessly.

Since all of their belongings had been taken away, heading to the destination would only cost them more fare to return.

“... I guess we have to pray that we don’t run into any more pirates on our way back.”

“Everything has been stolen, so what difference does it make?”

“Don’t say that.... what if they get angry because we don’t have anything and try to harm us?”

“...”

One of the merchants sighed.

“Life is so hard. It is so fucking hard...”

Everyone went silent at the complaint. In a moment when everyone was immersed in their own sadness, someone pointed outside the ship and said,

“Look there... it seems like that ship will get plundered too.”

“Oh my...”

Sure enough, they saw a ship approaching from afar. The ship that discovered it turned the bow and began to head toward this new ship.

Everyone looked there with sad eyes. There was no choice but to watch this new ship’s misfortune.

“Look there, there.”

“What?”

“Another pirate ship is coming. They aren’t even alone; they are a large group.”

“What is happening on the Yangtze River?”

Everyone was speechless at this terrible sight.

At that time, someone mumbled curiously,

“...But why is it moving that way?”

“Uh?”

“In the first place, wasn’t the one who attacked first the one who plunders it? The pirate ship has already reached there, so why is this one also going there?”

“...Uh?”

The people blinked, looking at the scene again.

“It is going fast... Uh? Huh?”

The ship that appeared late rushed forward without even slowing down. Then, it rammed into the pirate ship attached to the merchant ship.

Kwaaang!

“...Oh my!”

The eyes of the people grew as big as lanterns as they witnessed this.

“Take it all away!”

Seo Hong, who had just cut down a resisting person, laughed without hesitation and shouted.

‘This is it! This is how it should be!’

Until now, he had never been able to run wild while hunting. This was because he knew very well that if the plundering was excessive, he would end up attracting the Justice Faction.

But now, the Justice Faction could not come to the Yangtze River to stop them. This was what he meant when he said it should be like this.

Of course, as time passed, they would have to make adjustments like before. If they caught all the fish in the sea, the lake would die and rot with nothing left.

But for a while, they would be able to freely release the evil feelings they had suppressed.

“Captain!”

“What?”

“A ship is arriving from behind.”

“What? Is it the government?”

“No, it looks like a pirate ship.”

“Pirate?”

Seo Hong frowned.

“It seems like they are coming here. Leave it alone.”

“Yes.”

Even though he told them to leave it alone, he clicked his tongue.

‘Still, don’t we have to follow the rules of pirates?’

The law of pirates was simple. Whoever touched it first won. It might be different elsewhere, but the area around here was unclaimed land, so many pirates might converge here.

And so, the authority of the ship belonged to those who came first.

“It is a pity, so we will leave a little for—”

Kwang!

At that moment, a loud noise erupted, and the ship they were on shook as if it would capsize.

“Uh!”

“Ahh!”

The sudden shock caused the pirates running on the dock to roll on the floor and scream.

“W-what was that?”

Seo Hong, flustered, ran to the ship. This unfamiliar ship was stuck behind their chained ship.

“T-those fucking morons!”

He cursed, thinking they must have hit something because they didn’t steer properly. Of course, pirates who spent most of their time on ships were usually good at steering, so this rarely happened... for now, he had no choice but to judge it that way.

But he quickly realized that thought was wrong.

“Let’s go!”

A group of warriors rose from the rammed ship.

‘What?’

Seeing that they were perfectly dressed in black uniforms, clearly different from the clothes pirates wore, they weren’t normal bandits.

The moment he saw the swords in their hands and the Plum Blossom Sword pattern engraved on their chests, the name of a sect flashed through his mind.

“M-Mount Hua? Why is Mount Hua coming here?”

His face had turned pale.

If it had been a year ago or even a month, his reaction would have been entirely different. But the status of the name of Mount Hua in the Yangtze River had changed drastically compared to a month back.

There was no one in the Yangtze River area who didn’t know the rumor that Mount Hua had destroyed pirates with a small number of people and even played a key role in the final battle.

In other words, the name of Mount Hua in the Yangtze River today was no different from the Grim Reaper.

“AhHh!“

“Spare me... argh!”

No, perhaps differently, the swordsmen of Mount Hua who jumped onto the ship began to cut down the pirates in an instant. It could be deduced that there were just a few people left on the main ship to even move it, but even taking that into account, the speed was too much.

“D-damn it!”

Seo Hong was confused by the completely unexpected situation and was struggling to find a way to handle it.

“Tsk.”

A man in a black murim robe jumped onto the merchant ship. Almost at the same time, five young warriors jumped behind and supported him.

“Hmmm.”

The person on the ship slowly looked around, and his face contorted.

“Have these pirate bastards lost their sense of fear? Are you talking about business in the river with such a rude nature?”

“...”

“Who is in charge here?“

Seo Hong looked at the man with a stern look on his face and said.

“It is me. So, are you a member of Mount Hua Sect?”

“Mount Hua sect? Mount Hua Sect memberrr? You bastard, you speak short words or what? How dare this bastard speak so informally? I am going to pull off your teeth and let you live off of just gruel!”

“...”

Seo Hong’s face became dumbfounded by the sudden outpouring of verbal abuse.

“Who are you?”

“Me? I am Chung Myung, you bastard. What should I say? What are you?”

But surprisingly the face of Seo Hong turned pale.

“C-Ch-chung Myung?”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung tilted his head in response and glanced back.

“Seems like you know me?”

“... I do.”

“It is even weirder if you don’t know now.”

Baek Cheon and Yoon Jong shook their heads. This guy sometimes underestimated his reputation too much.

“M-Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

Cold sweat poured down Seo Hong’s face as if he were standing in front of the devil’s judgment.

He wanted to deny it, but the expression of the person squatting in front of him was the same as what he had heard. Moreover, the bandages wrapped around the wrists, exposed under the open front and sleeves, proved that this man had just been involved in a fierce battle not long ago.

Chung Myung, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.

A warrior from Mount Hua that killed the Great Whale pirate’s leader and destroyed their entire force.

“That... he fought against the Hegemony Lord....”

Chung Myung’s face contorted at Seo Hong’s mumbling.

“Yah, bastard? Heh, I would have won if that bastard hadn’t interfered.”

Baek Cheon, who was standing behind him, spoke in a panic.

“... no, that is a bit.”

“What?”

“A person should have a conscience.”

“What? I won! If I fight till the end, I will win for sure! Anyone! Any bastard can come!”

“Ahh! Right. Right. See ahead now. Ahead.”

“I am telling you!”

“Yes. Yes. Look ahead.”

Seo Hong’s fingertips were trembling like thin trees.

Even in the face of ruthless pirates, he didn’t seem nervous at all. Depending on how you thought about it, it could be seen as either humiliating or realistic. Still, Seo Hong didn’t even feel humiliated.

It was natural.

Would a person who invaded, caught, and killed the Great Whale pirates, destroyed their pirate troops, and engaged in a fierce battle with the Four Precepts Alliance in the midst of the war between them and the Justice Faction, be concerned about normal pirates?

Resist?

He couldn’t even think about it. If the opponent was Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, someone who poured oil on the body and jumped into a fire pit would have a higher chance of surviving.

But even if he didn’t fight back with a weapon, he still had something to say.

“Well, the Justice Faction isn’t allowed to enter the Yangtze River! T-then how are you people here?”

“Why can’t the Justice Faction enter, you bastard! The Nine Great Sects cannot come here. We aren’t one of them. We got kicked out years ago.”

“No, you speak too much. Why are you saying all that! I cannot believe we got kicked out!”

“I am telling you!”

“You can say the same thing a little differently. It doesn’t apply because we are the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Eh. If we hadn’t been kicked out, we wouldn’t have been coming to this.”

“Euk.”

Baek Cheon showed displeasure and covered his face with the hand that wasn’t holding the sword.

Chung Myung pointed at Seo Hong with his chin.

“Haven’t you heard that if you get caught doing business in this area, you die?”

“W-what is that...”

“You don’t know? Well, there is nothing I can do now. Then, even if you lose something, you can go and blame what happened on you.”

Chung Myung shouted as if there was nothing more to say.

“Hahaha! Take away everything they have, cut down those who resist, and turn them into food for the fish!”

“Stop talking like a pirate, you moron!”

“He didn’t say anything weird, but the feeling is odd.”

The Five Swords mumbled and kicked the ground and moved like beams of light. At the same time, the Baek and Chung disciples began to drive away the pirates.

The ship was cleared in an instant.

The number of people who could ride on one ship was initially limited. If they faced each other in such situations, the pirates weren’t even an after-meal workout for the Mount Hua disciples.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who cut down the fiercely resisting pirates, surrounded the sole remaining Seo Hong.

Seo Hong, clinging to the railing, looked at them, sweat dripping down his face.

“Huh, the Black Dragon King will not sit idly and ignore this! If you kill me...”

Baek Cheon smiled softly at that and answered.

“Thank you for the advice, but I don’t think it is something you should worry about.”

“...”

Seo Hong gritted his teeth and glared at Mount Hua with poisonous eyes.

“How long can you last on the Yangtze River....”

“Shitt!”

At that moment, Chung Myung, who was behind him, flew in like a bird and kicked Seo Hong in the chin.

Baaaak!

Everyone closed their eyes tightly.

Seo Hong, with his head tilted back, twitched and fell over the railing.

Plop!

“Ahho! You speak too much, bastard!”

Chung Myung finally shook off his hands like he felt refreshed.

“I told you not to overdo it. The wounds haven’t healed yet.”

“How can it be difficult to catch that minnow? It was a light exercise.”

’Ugh.“

Baek Cheon shook his head, saying he couldn’t stop him.

Anyway, he never listened to him.

“Soso, who is injured?”

“No one died, sahyung. There are a few people with stab wounds, but they aren’t life-threatening.“

“You rotten bastards.”

Chung Myung gritted his teeth and glared at the pirates to one side. The pirates hurriedly lowered their pale faces.

“We will treat you and find the owners of the stolen items.”

“I get it!”

Mount Hua’s disciples began to move busily.

The civilians, who had been watching the scene trembling in the corner, finally gathered courage and approached cautiously.

“Right, thank you. Thank you.”

“Eh, what! Ha. Ha. Ha. Ha. As part of the Justice Faction, this is something we must do! Of. Course. Justice. Faction.”

“Ah...”

Those who had blinked asked with puzzled eyes.

“But this time, due to the agreement with the Evil Faction, the Nine Sects couldn’t step into the Yangtze River...”

“Nonsense. Indeed, the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families cannot come, but we can.”

“... then?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung smiled and answered.

“We don’t know about other places, but the Gugang region is what we will protect, so you can come through this way. I will make sure those bastards don’t even get close to you.”

“AHH!”

“Please let people know. This place is protected by the Heavenly Friends Alliance. There will never be anyone in Gugang attacked by pirates.”

Then, Chung Myung secretly whispered to the merchant standing in front.

“And we built a bridge to the island here? If you come, we will charge you a cheap price and help transport it.”

“Really?”

“Huh? Do you think you were deceived? This is Chung Myung! Chung Myung! Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

As Chung Myung spoke while pounding his chest, the faces of the merchants turned bright red.

“R-right! Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

“We can trust the famous Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

The Five Swords, who were listening quietly, closed their eyes.

‘How did things end up like this?’

‘When did this guy, who is the symbol of fraud and corruption, turn into a symbol of trust?’

In the Yangtze River, Chung Myung’s reputation grew incomparably higher than before.

If the story began to spread, it would not be surprising if Mount Hua gained the reputation of being the best sect in the world, at least along the Yangtze River.

The merchants were filled with emotion and repeatedly held Chung Myung’s hand, bowing their heads.

“T-thank you. Thank you. How do I repay the favor...”

“Hahaha. How do you repay? You can pay it back with money. You speak as if there is another way to repay the favor. A man with the name and profession of a merchant... eup! Eup!”

Baek Cheon covered Chung Myung’s mouth and dragged him away with a smile, saying,

“Hahaha. He hasn’t healed from the injury yet, so his head feels a bit unstable now.”

“....”

Yoon Jong quietly blocked the view of the dazed merchants.

“If you have any questions, you can ask me. I will explain everything.”

“Ah... so, can I ask one thing...”

“Please. Feel free to ask.”

“... that is an unfortunate question.....”

“It is alright. Please speak comfortably.”

“Then, without shame....”

The merchant, who was hesitating to speak, took a deep breath and then spoke with gathered courage.

“That... is that really Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

“...”

Yoon Jong couldn’t bring himself to answer ‘yes’.

As the pirates rampaged, the public sentiment around the Yangtze River turned extremely uneasy.

It wasn’t easy to grit one’s teeth and hold on as people were losing their lives and having all their belongings taken one after another everywhere.

Those who were exhausted went to the government to appeal. Still, all they received was a perfunctory response that they would request the Imperial Palace to subdue the pirates.

In the first place, the administrative power of the officials did not extend to the Yangtze River. If they could find and deal with the pirates hiding along the river, they would have done so immediately.

Moreover, since the pirates were devastating villages and reducing tax revenues, they must be caught somehow. However, since the pirates didn’t touch the grain or food, they weren’t considered as urgent a threat as bandits.

Since the pirates were also mindful of the goods transported by the government, those were left completely untouched.

Therefore, the officials were not planning to take any hasty action.

Those who were pounding the ground in frustration soon felt like they were grasping at straws and went to small and medium sects adjacent to the Yangtze River, rather than the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, to seek help.

But they also expressed their rejection.

“I have no excuses for this. If we step forward and handle it, it will be a distraction for no reason.”

“It is a nearby village, so we will do our best to protect it...”

“What can we do when the Nine Great Sects cannot do anything?”

In the end, the civilians had to just turn their backs.

More than anything else, the most despairing thing was pondering what they could do about a situation that even the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families had given up on.

It was said that the merchants along the Yangtze River were taking measures, but there was no way they could do anything special.

At best, it would involve hiring escorts and placing them around merchant ships. It made no sense to expect that an escort, who could be purchased for a few pennies, would be able to fend off the pirates, one of the Shenzhou Five clans.

Meanwhile, the damage was increasing, and while everyone was speaking out against the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families out of frustration, strange rumors began to spread.

-Pirates cannot approach the area around the mouth of the river.

-The Heavenly Friends Alliance, centered on Mount Hua, settled there. The pirates who were plundering were caught by Mount Hua and cut to pieces.

-The Heavenly Friends Alliance, led by Mount Hua, declared the mouth of the river as their land and said they would protect the ships traveling from there.

The merchants who heard the news were very upset.

People gathered at the dock in groups of twos and threes, each raising their voices to understand the situation.

“No, wasn’t the Justice Faction not allowed to come to the Yangtze River? They said they signed a treaty like that?”

“N-No! It isn’t that the Justice Faction cannot come; it is the Nine Sects and Five Families who cannot come! Aren’t the Heavenly Friends Alliance neither of the two?”

“I-Is that so? Why didn’t I think of it?”

Those who were shocked by the choices of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, which had forgotten the existence of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, began to engrave the name in people’s minds once again.

“But is this alright? Is the Heavenly Friends Alliance a greater force than the Nine Great Sects or Five Great Families? They won’t be able to handle the pirates, right?”

“We don’t necessarily have to think that. Isn’t Mount Hua the place which was first to subdue those hostile bastards in the first place?”

“R-Right!”

One of the merchants nodded.

What happened next was so significant that it was eventually forgotten. Still, if one considered it, it all began because Mount Hua struck the pirates first.

“If Mount Hua was afraid of the pirates, it wouldn’t have taken such action initially. Didn’t I mention that all pirates approaching the river’s mouth had already been defeated? Mount Hua is actively navigating a ship to bring them down.”

“And the pirates? Is there no response from them?”

“It seems that way.”

“Does that make sense?”

“If you think about it, it doesn’t make sense. Aren’t the pirates the ones who suffered the most damage in the war?”

“Right. Because countless pirates have collapsed.”

“Maybe the pirates lack the strength to confront Mount Hua right now. And wasn’t Mount Hua a sect that wasn’t afraid of pirates to begin with? What is there to fear from the pirates when they were originally confident of handling them?”

“Oh my... was Mount Hua such a strong sect? Strong enough to not fear the pirates?”

“Recently, the name of Mount Hua has been heard more often than Shaolin or Wudang. I guess we did underestimate Mount Hua.”

Someone nodded quickly.

It didn’t matter if the news was true or not.

The most important thing then was that a route had opened up. Merchants on the Yangtze River were suffering enormous losses each day because they could not keep their ships afloat.

“So, whatever, you’re saying that you can use that route while Mount Hua is holding it, right?”

“Yes.”

“W-wait... does that make sense? The pirates control the entire Yangtze River, so we cannot even move a ship. If we somehow move the goods there, there couldn’t possibly be a ship to transport them to the other side.”

“They said that wasn’t true. Mount Hua is said to have built a bridge across the Yangtze River.”

“B-bridge? On the Yangtze River? What nonsense is that?”

It was impossible to build a bridge across the wide Yangtze River.

“No, no, listen. It is said that long piers were built on the left and right around the middle of the river. They say that if you bring the goods, they will load them onto a ship and move them.”

“R-really?”

“Right. But I heard that there is a toll to pay. It takes a huge amount to protect such a path.”

“Yah, man, seriously! Are tolls really a problem now? The goods are all rotting in the warehouse, and they will be thrown away if we cannot ship them right now! D-did... did you say the mouth of the river? I need to report this to the merchant’s head!”

The rumor spread quickly, mainly among the merchants.

This news was like a ray of light for those who were stamping their feet because they couldn’t launch their ships due to the fear of pirates. Everyone who heard this rushed there.

“I-it is real!”

What those who arrived at the place saw was an estuary whose topography had changed from before, with dozens of merchant ships anchored around the river.

“L-look there. I want to ask something.”

The Green Forest person managing the ship turned his head and looked at the merchant who rushed in.

“What... no, the... uh, what is happening?”

The person, who was about to give a harsh answer, clasped his hands and answered politely, causing the merchants to react. At that time, one of the merchants said,

“That... Mount Hua is indeed moving the goods across the river?”

“It isn’t Mount Hua; it’s the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“Yes, yes! Heavenly Friends Alliance. Is that true?”

“Of course, it is true. So what do all these ships seem to be doing? Instead, if you drop off the goods on the island, they have to move them to the other side. Of course, we rent the ship, I get paid too.”

Sparks shone in the eyes of the merchant.

“I-is this really true?”

“W-wait a minute, doesn’t that mean we have to pay twice for the ferry fare and also pay to use the cart?”

“What’s wrong with that? Then, if you meet the pirates, they rob everything!”

“Who said it was wrong? Yah-yah! What should I do to use the ship?”

The Green Forest person smiled and said,

“I will move things or people, so come. We will rent out a ship if you want and even a porter. Please negotiate the detailed prices over there.”

“There?”

The eyes of the merchants turned.

Indeed, there was a small house built in front of the dock where the ships were lined up and anchored.

At that moment, the eyes of the merchants shone with madness.

“Get out of my way!”

“Come out, you bastard!”

“I came here first!”

Merchants began to rush to the reservation office on the dock. The Green Forest bandits burst into laughter, seeing the merchants fight each other.

“... I never thought this would happen.”

One could never guess what happens in the world.

Swish!

The ship approached the island through the current.

“H-here it comes!”

“Hurry up! Hurry!”

The pirates who saw it were frightened and ran to the water.

Tat!

A ship stopped at the dock, and when the person jumped out of the ship, the bandits lined up immediately, bending their backs at a right angle.

“They are here, great sahyung!”

“What?”

The grumpiest voice in the world came out. Everyone who heard the voice clenched their necks.

“Ha... Hahaha....”

One of the pirates opened his mouth awkwardly, sweating profusely.

“N-now that we have become one family...”

“One familyyy?”

“...”

Chung Myung rolled his eyes and said sarcastically,

“The world has gone insane, uh? Is this bastard really trying to bring Taoists into their family? Uh?”

‘This bastard.’

‘Even if we treat him well, it is still shit.’

But he couldn’t bear to say that with his mouth.

He knew right away that this person was terribly strong with the worst personality, but who would have imagined that he could be so incredibly strong and reckless?

‘I never thought he would be a human being who was capable of fighting against the Hegemony Lord.’

‘It was fortunate that I got saved.’

‘He gives me goosebumps, really.’

Chung Myung looked at the pirates with crazy eyes.

“Guys.”

“Yes!”

He seemed satisfied with the loud answer, so he nodded and spoke in a much softer voice.

“Being a person has its own value.”

“Yes!”

“But you are not a person. Bastards of pirates who are trying to be treated like a person. Does it work? It doesn’t, right?”

“Ah, no!”

“Right, I know. So break that arm and then work. If there is a bastard who isn’t working properly and playing around, I will tie the limbs and throw them into the water. If you are that good in the water, you can survive even with arms and legs tied. Right?”

“...”

“Why are you not answering?”

“I-I will do my best!”

“I will do my best!”

“Please spare me!”

“Nice.”

Chung Myung nodded with a satisfied look. Jo Gul, who was getting off the ship at that time, looked at that and laughed.

“... this suits you so well.”

“Pirates, pirates.”

“He was so excited when the ship hit earlier that I wasn’t sure if he should give them full-time work or not.”

“Bastard.”

The Five Swords shook their heads.

They took a quick glance around the island to see what was happening.

“There! Hold the pillar straight!”

“We don’t have enough materials! Is the transport ship too far?”

“I-I am doing my best to carry it, but since the transportation ship is...”

“Hurry a little more!”

“Yes!”

Large pavilions were being built in the middle of the island. And, of course, those leading the construction were members of the Tang Family.

“Eh. If I had known this would happen, I would have brought my higher-ups.”

“I thought we were coming to fight, so I couldn’t bring them along. It’s not like we’re creating something amazing here.”

“Still, it is a pity, isn’t it? There! No, you cannot do that! Get out of the way!”

Baek Cheon mumbled with a slightly exhausted expression as he watched the Tang family members running around.

“Did it really have to go this far?”

“Because the situation changed. Chung Myung mentioned it, right? Not just here, but all the nearby merchants will flock here. So, of course, we have to expand. We need to build more paths.”

Baek Cheon nodded hesitantly at Jo Gul.

‘It seems like things are getting bigger and bigger.’

Paradoxically, the chaos throughout the Yangtze River was more beneficial to Mount Hua. The more the pirates rampaged, the more merchants seeking safe trade routes would flock here.

Chung Myung asked, gesturing toward the pirates.

“Right. Have the guests arrived?”

“Now, there is an endless stream of people coming to the other side. The Green Forest King will soon start the business.”

“Where is he?”

“Receiving guests? That’s his job.”

“...”

Uh... in the end, a bandit’s job was to receive guests.

But it could hold a different meaning...

‘I don’t know. As long as you do your job well, things will be fine here.’

Chung Myung put away the complicated thoughts and smiled as he looked at the merchant ships anchored across the river.

“Money is money. Now, let’s get it all together! Kekeke!”

Hit the Nine Great Sects bastards, make money, and kill the pirates along the way. This was a ditch and...

Ah... there was nothing to even eat here, so it wasn’t a big deal.

Could they not even catch a carp here?

Ugh.

“Sir, do you think this is the place?”

“I-it does seem like it, doesn’t it?”

Those pulling the cart stopped, patting the horse on the back. Before them lay a large pier and a bustling crowd of people.

“... why are there so many?”

“R-right?”

Baggage was piled up like mountains, and the line of carts stretching behind seemed endless.

Of course, it wasn’t something they couldn’t comprehend.

Launching a ship into the Yangtze River was akin to suicide now. Yet, this was the only place where one could safely launch a ship. In other words, all merchants needing to use ships on the Yangtze River would converge here.

“... I never imagined there would be so many merchants on the Yangtze River.”

“You don’t usually see crowds like this.”

“Yes.”

The man nodded, gazing ahead with a nervous expression.

“Anyway, be cautious. This is no ordinary dock. Isn’t this place run by the Mount Hua and Heavenly Friends Alliance? One wrong word, and we could be in trouble.”

“They’re from the Justice Faction, so I really hope it’s alright.”

“You never know! Do you realize how high the standards are for those who claim to be from the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families?”

The noble-looking man shook his head thoughtfully.

“The people merchants find more challenging to deal with than officials are those high-headed sects. I’m sure it’s not the first time people have lost their minds trusting the words of the Justice faction. So don’t relax and think you’re dealing with a high-ranking official.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

“... let’s go.”

After swallowing hard, the nobleman led the troops toward the dock.

‘But who should I speak with?’

Since this was a system he had never encountered before, it was difficult to get a sense of it. The chaotic surroundings only added to the challenge.

The gaze of the person who was constantly looking around landed on someone in the center. The way he was holding a large booklet looked a little different from others.

‘Is it there?’

The noble-looking man approached the guy and asked.

“Excuse me... c-can I take a ship from here?”

Stroke!

The student-like person turned his head and smiled brightly at him.

“Welcome, sir!”

“...”

“Guys! Water! Bring refreshing, cool water and wet towels! Right now!”

“Yes!”

Those who were busy around the pier rushed to a chest and took out water that seemed to be soaked in ice and a cold towel.

“Thank you for your efforts in coming this long way!”

“Now, now! Here.”

The man suddenly accepted what was offered. As he held the cold gourd of water in his hand, he felt like all the hard work he had put into this journey had vanished.

“Is this the first time?”

“Yes, that is true... but our merchant leader said that I could take a ship from here...”

“Ah, is that so? Which merchant union are you from?”

“We are from the Cheering Merchant Union.”

“Cheering Merchant Union?”

This disciple-like kid flipped through the list in hand.

“Let’s see, Cheering, Cheering Merchant... ah! Here it is, book 2! If it is your first time, this is the ship! Guys! Accept this guest!”

“Yes!”

As soon as he finished speaking, a bunch of men rushed in and began to lift his baggage from the cart and put it onto the ship.

“Huh...? Is that heavy thing being lifted like that?”

At the merchant union, there were people who basically carried the luggage as their daily work. And yet, he couldn’t help but feel shocked at the sight of so much heavy luggage being carried like this. Right, one person was carrying two or three pieces of heavy load at the same time.

Just looking at it, it was easy to guess that these people were not ordinary ones.

“They are so strong...”

“Fly! Quickly!”

“R-Run, you bastards!”

“If you are late, you die! Fly!”

“...”

The merchants watched the people carrying the luggage with all their might.

‘Do we really need to do something like that?’

The shiphands appeared more desperate than the merchants transporting the cargo. It was quite strange, considering that the workers usually tried to make their work easier.

But the disciple/student in front of him didn’t seem to like this pace.

“Did these people not eat anything? Move quickly! If you cannot carry all these loads by today, I will die, and so will you! You know that guy’s personality, right? Don’t you?”

“Ugh.”

At those words, the workers groaned and began to speed up.

“Fly! Fly quickly!”

“No! This ship cannot take any more load! If you put more on, it will sink!”

“Then hurry up and take the next ship! What on earth are you bastards even doing? Hurry up, and don’t waste ships!”

“There! Don’t put it in there anymore!”

The nobleman wiped the cold sweat off his forehead as he watched the workers relentlessly carry the luggage.

‘What is even happening here?’

It was right then.

“Isn’t this Gwang Hong?”

“Uh?”

Hearing this familiar voice, the nobleman turned to the side to be greeted by a familiar face.

“Oh? Lee Myong hyung!”

“You came here too? After all, among those who make a living on the Yangtze River now, there is probably no one who doesn’t come here.”

“Yes, hyung. Hyung is also here.”

Lee Myong, a merchant, laughed at this.

“This is insane...”

“Truly insane...”

“But you will get used to it soon. I have finally gotten used to this.”

Lee Myong looked around and spoke in a voice of admiration.

“Isn’t this amazing?”

“Right. After all, Mount Hua is Mount Hua. When on earth did they even build a dock, and where did they get these workers? From the looks of it, they don’t seem like Taoists....”

“Uh? You don’t know?”

“What?”

“Look over there, on that side.”

Lee Myong carefully pointed to a large man carrying luggage. Hong tilted his head in curiosity.

“... What about him?”

“Look carefully. There’s no way you don’t recognize him.”

“Uh?”

He scrutinized the man for a moment, his eyes narrowing, then flinched in realization.

“Well... No way!”

“Right. That’s the Taiping Great Blade.”

“Why is that person here?!”

The Taiping Great Blade.

He was a notorious mountain bandit from the Green Forest bandits located in the Taiping Mountains. With his great strength and vicious nature, there wasn’t a merchant who hadn’t heard of him.

Hong had encountered him a couple of times. Due to the notoriety of the Taiping bandits, they had to pay more tolls there than anywhere else.

And this bandit was famous for his monstrous strength.

“Kuaak!”

As the Taiping Great Blade grunted while carrying the luggage, a passerby kicked him on the butt.

“Ah!”

“This bastard is done! Move quickly, you moron!”

“I-I am sorry!”

The unbelievable sight made both their faces turn pale.

“W-what is this...”

That Taiping Great Blade got hit on his butt and apologized in return? What was happening here?

“They all seem to be from Green Forest.”

“Uh, all the workers? All of them?”

“...”

Hong’s body went cold.

A bandit carrying merchant loads here—what kind of an absurd situation was this? It was like leaving the fish to the cat.

“A-are you alright with this?”

“If you think about it, it isn’t such a bad thing. Take a look. Who are the people targeting this place the most right now? Aren’t they the pirates?”

“Sure.”

“To fight against such pirates, one needs to have bandits too. How could pirates come when the bandits heavily crowd the place?”

“A-ah, no, they are still bandits...”

“Tsk tsk. You worry about everything. Look over there.”

“Uh?”

Hong turned his gaze to the place pointed out by Lee Myong. A huge flag was waving, adorned with some crooked handwriting.

“What is written there...”

Hong had to squint to read the words, and then his eyes widened.

There was no need to be surprised anymore.

“... What is that?”

“They say it was written by Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. No matter how strong the bandits are, can they really fight against Mount Hua?”

“...”

Hong felt dizzy with this.

Everything happening here was far beyond his common sense.

Bandits working at a dock built by Taoists. Even the Taoists of Mount Hua are renowned for being the best at defeating bandits.

‘I don’t care anymore.’

Let us not try to think; anyway, all you have to do is make money.

“It looks like your luggage is loaded.”

“A-already?”

Shipping usually took longer than expected. It would take an entire day to fill one ship and even get workers to do that.

But not here. One could see the load piling onto the ship. Even if a kid stacked wooden toy blocks, they wouldn’t be able to do it so fast.

“Then let us see the island.”

“You mean, not cross?”

“Tsk tsk. Did you really come here without knowing anything? Here, you need to travel through the island. I think they did it on purpose because it would be a pain if they went straight to the water and ran into pirates there.”

“But wouldn’t that mean repeating the loading and unloading again at the end?”

“Who cares? It isn’t something we have to do.”

After listening to it, he realized that it was true.

“Of course, one has to pay tax twice, but if they can just move, then the fare is nothing to me. Well, I have to go now, so see you later.”

“Yes, hyung.”

Hong clutched his head as he watched Lee Myong hurry to the ship. At that time, the disciple giving the instructions came rushing over.

“Cheering Merchant’s leader, right? The shipment is loaded. Get on the ship, and you can head to the island.”

“W-what do I do at the island?”

“Don’t worry. We do all the unloading. But the cart you brought cannot be loaded onto the ship, so please rent a cart on the other side to move the luggage.”

“Ah, the cart...”

“It is one coin per cart.”

“...”

The disciple smiled kindly.

“And it doesn’t matter when the time is early like today, but if you arrive at the island late in the day, I recommend sleeping at night and moving tomorrow. These days, the Evil Faction is so rampant that it is dangerous to travel at night. We already have accommodations ready for you to stay on the island.”

‘Ah...I am so thankful...’

“It is five coins per room.”

“...”

“Of course, meals are provided.”

“... and how much for that?”

“Hahaha. You can have as much as you want.”

“...”

Surely, that meal would not be cheap for sure.

“Well, can I ask one question?”

“Yes, go ahead.”

“It seems like they are building something there. What is that?”

“Ahh. There are more people than expected, so there are more people waiting. We are building accommodations for them to rest.”

“Ah. That would be easier...”

“There will be a diner where you can have a quick meal, a tea house to enjoy tea, a bar to drink, and even a gambling house to kill time.”

“...”

“Please visit us often! I hope you visit us!”

“... yes.”

“Then, please leave.”

The disciple who smiled kindly turned and shouted.

“Move those things quickly! The customers are waiting!”

He was filled with excitement and ran, but Hong shook his head.

‘I don’t know what is happening here.’

Even though he had lived and traveled around for a long time and had seen a lot of things in the world, a couple of things always went past his common sense.

If the workers asked for money or unnecessary compensation, he was asked to report immediately. All to separate the workers’ bones and flesh.

“ACKKKK!”

“P-please spare me!”

A village located right at the mouth of the Yangtze River was quickly engulfed in thick red flames. Amidst the chaos, people wielding menacing weapons were shouting.

“SET IT ALL ON FIRE! HAHAHAH!”

A man with a rough-looking beard surveyed the scene with his gleaming eyes.

People were screaming, running for their lives, and seeking refuge as houses burned brightly.

“Huhu.”

The man licked his lips once. How long had it been since he last saw this sight?

“Captain! I cleaned everything out of the warehouse. But I didn’t see anything of value.”

“Tsk. Everything is the same. It doesn’t matter, though, because this isn’t the only village we touch.”

The man had a triumphant smile.

“But is it alright?”

“What? Are you feeling scared now?”

“I am not scared, just that...”

“Don’t worry.”

Gwa Maeng, the leader of the Galho sect, chuckled and said,

“If it were normal times, the Justice Faction would have come rushing in, but now they cannot even get close, so what’s the problem?”

Implicitly, the area north of the Yangtze River was solidified as the lands of the Justice Faction. As a result, even the Evil Faction never dared step into the mouth of the Yangtze River because they never knew when the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families would come running.

But now, they could no longer approach the Yangtze River.

The other Justice Factions would not have the courage to approach the Yangtze River, where the pirates were located, without the protection of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

Because of this situation, the area around the Yangtze River was now a lawless place. The situation north of the Yangtze River was especially serious.

“Just don’t touch the merchants! Since our job is to rob merchant ships, they will not consider their area as being invaded if we mess with their farming lands. So, get rid of it all!”

“Yes, captain!”

Gwa Maeng laughed excitedly as he watched the village burn.

‘This is why difficult times are good times.’

In the days when the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were quietly keeping each other in check, even the Evil Faction couldn’t go on a rampage. Even small ripples appeared large in a calm lake. This was because if one carelessly stood out, there was a high chance they would be shattered into pieces like a sharp stone being crushed.

But in today’s world, there is no need to worry about that.

“What better world is there than this! Ehhahahaha! Take it down! Don’t leave a single grain of rice behind!”

Just as Gwa Maeng was shouting excitedly.

“L-leader.”

“What?”

“Something is coming from over there. It looks like there are a lot of them.”

“What? Officials?”

Gwa Maeng turned his head with a surprised face, then looked in the direction where the subordinate pointed and narrowed his eyes.

“... doesn’t seem like the officials to me.”

There were too few of them to be called officials. Besides, the officials wouldn’t be there at all. If those guys had moved so quickly in the first place, Gwa Maeng wouldn’t have even been able to rob the damn village.

“They are either insane Justice faction bastards who don’t know their situation, or they are bastards from another Evil Faction. Nothing to worry about, first, let us finish it here...”

But things didn’t go as smoothly as expected.

He couldn’t even finish his sentence and quietly went silent. This was because the speed of those approaching was completely beyond common sense.

The people who looked like dots in the distance suddenly grew larger, and soon the shape of the person became visible.

‘W-what?’

The figure of the person running in front was clearly visible to the eyes. A young man in a black murim robe pulled out a sword in one hand and roared like a tiger.

“You, you bastard!”

The person who rushed ahead in a flash closed the distance and charged at Hwa Maeng. Gwa Maeng raised the axe in his hand.

“How dare you?”

Gwa Maeng shouted loudly and ran toward the young man who was charging at him. The intense qi rose before the axe even met the sword.

Gwa Maeng smiled as if he had already won.

His axe was one of his most formidable weapons. It wasn’t something a sword could block. If one didn’t consider that and let the blood rush to their head, then this was just a kid who didn’t know his place.

Perhaps the Justice Faction members, who heard the rumors, rushed to the Yangtze River without knowing their place. Making the kid pay would be enough.

‘Such a moron. Die!’

But it was right then.

Swish!

The sword rushing towards his axe split into dozens of forms.

‘What?’

Numerous sword forms scattered like an illusion, and soon hundreds of sword qi emerged.

Gwa Maeng’s eyes widened at this unbelievable sight. The sword qi scattered around grazed his heavy axe, and was now flying toward his body.

‘T-this is?’

Red sword qi fluttered, just like... flower petals.

At that moment, Gwa Maeng realized just where the person who attacked him belonged.

“M-Mount Hua...”

Slash.

The flying flower petals sliced through his throat.

Slash. Slash. Slash!

One after another, various parts of his body were cut, and blood splashed around.

“Grrg...”

Gwa Maeng’s body, unable to bear the weight of the heavy qi, fell down on the spot. The man who passed Gwa Maeng before anyone realized, gritted his teeth and looked back.

“L-leader!”

“Leader, in just one slash...”

The faces of those who watched turned pale.

Of course, their leader couldn’t be called the greatest warrior compared to the wide world. But still, he was famous around this area. He definitely wasn’t weak enough to lose his life to a young warrior.

Swish!

The one who shook off the sword to shake off the blood, Jo Gul, bared his teeth at the flustered Evil Faction ruffians.

“You damn bastard! How dare you trouble the civilians!”

The disciples of the Mount Hua Sect, who arrived later, rushed in without question and began to wipe out the pirates.

“You damn bastard!”

“Who are you calling an Evil Sect!”

The eyes of Mount Hua’s disciples were filled with anger.

“Do not give them too much credit!”

“Oh!”

The panicked Evil Faction members fought back with their weapons, but there was no way some small backstreet thugs at the Yangtze River could be opponents of Mount Hua.

Unlike when the village was only theirs to occupy, they lost their force and will to fight, leading to an instant collapse.
