“Fire! Fire! No, it isn’t like we are going to beat them right now! Put out the flames now! We can do that later!”

“... No, I think it is already all burned.”

“Why are those bastards setting fire to a normal house? Just take the damn grains and go, damn it!”

Jo Gul cursed as much as he could, rushed ahead, and kicked the groaning Evil Faction guy in front of him.

“Ahhh!”

“Does it hurt? You bastard! Does it hurt even for the disgusting people of the Evil Faction?”

Swish!

The guy who got kicked in the face couldn’t even scream and passed out, drooling from his mouth. Even after seeing that, Jo Gul couldn’t stop his anger and yelled out.

“Put out the flames!”

“I got you, you moron!”

“Ah, damn it!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who quickly cleared out the Galho Clan, moved to the burning houses. Some people were running to the well, but it seemed like only a couple of houses could be saved even if they started using buckets.

“There is no end, no fucking end.”

What Chung Myung said was indeed right.

-What? When are we going back to Mount Hua? What kind of carefree talk are you telling me? This is where it all begins.

“Don’t underestimate the bastards of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families. It’s true that those bastards were coasting on their names without doing much, but reputation is what matters. Now that they are officially out of the Yangtze River, there will be more chaos.”

“I will probably have to run around with a broken leg for a while.”

“Okay.”

Jo Gul sighed deeply.

Not only Jo Gul but also the others dealing with the rampant Evil Faction warriors felt the same way.

Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, and Yoon Jong were each leading Mount Hua disciples to patrol the Yangtze River, and members of the Tang family were also assisting Mount Hua in stabilizing the Yangtze River.

Still, there weren’t enough people to help.

This was because the Yangtze River was too vast for them to manage alone. With this number of people, there was a limit to how much they could stabilize the river.

It was said that the Four Precepts Alliance would not be able to move for a while, but they weren’t the only Evil Faction around. The idiots of this sect, who were usually unable to act boldly because of the Justice Faction, were now emerging like rats fleeing a burning house.

“Damned bastards...”

“Um... Sir warrior.”

“Ah? Yes!”

Jo Gul quickly turned at the sudden voice. A village man was looking their way, hesitant. Jo Gul promptly walked over and took his hand.

“Do not be scared. We are disciples of Mount Hua. No harm will come to you.”

“Ah... Yes. Mount Hua Sect’s...”

“Yes, right.”

Jo Gul nodded with a bright look and continued to speak.

“We will return the grain that was stolen. We will crack down on those bastards so that they will never cross into the village again.”

“Thank you. Thank you, really...”

Tears of relief flowed from the man’s eyes. If everything had been stolen from them, he wouldn’t have had a life to live anyway. If there wasn’t anything to eat, most people wouldn’t survive the year.

“But the houses...”

“Don’t worry too much about that. Having the lives saved is enough. We can always rebuild the house.”

“... I apologize.”

Jo Gul sighed.

He regretted that if he had come a little sooner, those houses wouldn’t have burned down to this extent.

“Gul.”

“Yes, sasuk.”

“Among those who fell, several were seriously injured. I guess we have to take them to the island.”

“Is it that serious?”

“Treatment will be difficult. We need a physician.”

Jo Gul nodded.

“Yes, I understand.”

Then, he turned his head and looked at the village path again. Without letting go of the old man’s hand, which he was still holding with both hands, he spoke carefully, making sure not to let the anger seep into his voice.

“As you heard, I think we need to take a few people with us. There are physicians at the dock in the river, where Mount Hua is located, so we can treat them there.”

“Physician? That... thank you, and I apologize, but lowly people like us do not have money to give to the physicians...”

“Don’t worry. We don’t need to get paid.”

“Uh? R-really?”

“Yes. And, sir, please comfort those who are too shocked by the incident. If you need anything to rebuild your homes, send someone to us. We will help as much as we can.”

“... How do we repay the favor...”

It was then.

“Damn it, the grains are burned!”

“What?”

Jo Gul turned his head.

“What should I do with sahyung? These bastards put the cart next to the house, and half the grain is burned.”

“No, what... oh, for real!”

In the end, Jo Gul got angry again and rushed to kick the jaws of the Evil Faction guys who were kneeling.

“Why can’t they even plunder people properly, these morons!”

Jo Gul, who had kicked the Evil Faction people until his anger subsided, huffed and secretly looked back at the village chief. Seeing the clear despair on his face, he let out a deep sigh.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“Do you have any money? It’s a disaster, and you usually carry an emergency fund.”

“Yeah, I know how you feel, but we shouldn’t be doing this...”

“For now, let’s think about all this later.”

“Chung Myung told me not to use it.”

“Should we let them die?”

“...”

“Give it to me.”

“...”

“Quickly now.”

Baek Sang looked up at the sky once and then finally took out the pouch from his arms. He handed it with a tight fist to the old man.

“The price of grain in the Yangtze River area will probably rise soon, so use this money to buy grain again. Then there will be no problem surviving the winter.”

A look of despair formed on the old man’s face, who wasn’t sure what to say.

“Why go this far for...”

“It is nothing like that.”

Jo Gul pouted his lips.

“If we just go back, it’s obvious those devils will come back to run amok with revenge in their eyes, so don’t be so thankful.”

“Uh?”

“The regret I have is that Mount Hua came too late. It’s the sorrow of life. Ah, I should have been the sasuk. Then I could just move as I wished!”

“I can say the same thing back to you.”

“AH, SASUK! Don’t be like this!”

Baek Sang smiled a little but then looked back with a straight face.

“The injured must be moved quickly. We don’t have time for all this.”

“Ah, right.”

Jo Gul nodded and greeted the old man.

“Then we should go back now.”

“Already?”

“Because there could be problems elsewhere too. If you have any problems in the village, you can come to the new dock in the Gugang region.”

“Nowadays, it is also called Plum Blossom Island, so it won’t be so difficult to find it by asking around.”

“Uh?”

“Then.”

As Jo Gul turned around, Baek Sang gave the instructions loudly.

“Tie up all the Evil Faction bastards and hand them over to the officials! And those of you who are quick-footed, carry the injured people over there and move them to our island, quickly!”

“Yes!”

“Move!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who were organizing things and moving like the wind, quickly departed from the village.

The villagers, who were staring blankly at the scene, mumbled without realizing it.

“...where did you say they were from?”

“The Mount Hua Sect.”

“Mount... Mount Hua Sect?”

A groan-like voice came from the old man’s mouth.

“The agreement was there; I thought all the help was an old story....”

He spoke softly, stroking the head of the child next to him.

“Remember the name of the benefactor. There is only so much we can do.”

“Uh.”

He followed the backs of the Mount Hua disciples with his wrinkled eyes as they walked away after the hard times.

Until they were no longer visible for a long time.

“He....”

“....”

“Hehe....”

“...”

“Heheheheheheheh!”

“....”

“Uhahahahaha! Uhahaha! Gurgle!”

“...”

An Elder of the Green Forest whispered carefully into the ears of Im So-Byeong.

“Are you alright?”

“... Just let them enjoy it.”

“...”

Before he knew it, Chung Myung, dressed in silk clothes and adorned with expensive jewels, was embracing the concept of wealth. The corners of his lips were stretched so wide they seemed to reach his ears.

“Uhahahaha! Uhahahaha!”

It was all money.

The money he made in just two days of business!

He had earned so much money that he was almost sick of it, but it was entirely different to watch it accumulate each day before his eyes.

Wasn’t this the beauty of a cash business?

“Ahem! After all, business should be done with rich people! Aren’t the richest people in the world merchants? Hehehehe!”

Merchants on the Yangtze River generally didn’t have problems due to a lack of money; their problem was not having enough money.

The island’s toll was set somewhat excessively. Still, the merchants, willing to sell their souls just to cross the river, paid without hesitation. They paid so willingly that others felt they had set the toll price too low.

Thanks to that, Chung Myung was now in a situation where money was accumulating like a mountain just by literally sitting still.

“Hehehe! Hehehehe!”

The 2nd elder, looking at Chung Myung, shook his head.

Where would you see a Taoist looking like that? Originally, a Taoist was someone who should stay away from money and focus on leading people spiritually.

‘Even merchants, who are called the demons of money, probably don’t like money as much as he does.’

Where on earth did such a Taoist come from?

“Woah. Nice job. Nice job. After all, you are the King of Green Forest. One with the right name for sure!”

“Hehehe. This is what I am an expert in.”

“As expected, so trustworthy! As expected!”

“That is an overstatement. Isn’t this all possible because of Mount Hua and the Taoist’s reputation?”

“Yes, yes! Hehehe!”

“Hehehehe.”

A Taoist and a bandit were praising each other and smiling; anyone could see that these people with no conscience were plotting things.

“This is just the beginning. The merchants who flock to the docks now are the best, the merchants from around the mouth of the Yangtze. If the rumor spreads further, all the merchants on the Yangtze River will flock here.”

“Right?”

“At that time, even if the toll fee is low, we will be able to earn several times more than we are earning now.”

“We need to expand the pier! Bring more workers in!”

“Of course! That too!”

“Because more people will flock. Should we expand facilities not only on the island but also on the dockside?”

“You know it all, as expected!”

Nowadays, not only they but also numerous merchants lived on the island, which was now known as Plum Blossom Island. The merchants, who were anxious about the pirates plundering, couldn’t relax even at the dock, so they decided to spend the night on an island protected by Mount Hua.

Everyone applauded the majestic nature of Mount Hua as they looked at the Mount Hua disciples stationed on the island and the cannons deployed as if to guard the island.

It was good for merchants because they could transport their luggage with peace of mind, and Mount Hua was good for making money, so wasn’t it really good for work?

“It is still an island, but the surrounding area needs to be a bit more stabilized. Even if you let them cross the river, the island will be useless if it is plundered on the other side. The problem is that this is not something we can do with Mount Hua and our own power...”

“Is it alright?”

After hearing Chung Myung’s words, Im So-Byeong tilted his head.

“Of course... it might be alright for a while, but you don’t plan to stay on the island forever, do you? If Mount Hua falls in its place, there will be new problems.”

Chung Myung grinned.

“No, there’s no problem. I just don’t want the commercial area to be blocked. I will stabilize it on my own soon.”

“Ah...”

Im So-Byeong nodded.

There was no way Jang Ilso could stand by and see his money flow be blocked. Other Evil Faction bastards might be greedy for small gains and continue to plunder. Still, the marketplace would know better than anyone else that money circulates to make more money.

“Which is why we need to make just enough money. I thought I would come to the Yangtze River and go through a lot of hardships before returning, but it was worth it after all!”

Chung Myung picked up the silver jar and threw it over his head. It was at that moment when Chung Myung’s face softened as he saw the silver coins pour down like shining rain.

“Sect leader!”

“No, even if you say so...”

A loud noise was heard outside the door.

“How could there be no other way?”

“Isn’t it Shaanxi? No matter how fast we move, it will take several months.”

“I don’t have time to wait that long. The situation is very bad.”

“Ugh. Right, I know that... it isn’t like I brought a bundle of money. And we don’t have to...”

Slash.

Soon the door opened, and Hyun Jong, with an arrogant frown on his face, entered the room. The Five Swords followed behind him with an eager look on their faces. Hyun Jong sighed.

“So, if you beat me up, you will get more money...”

Hyun Jong, who was speaking weakly, suddenly focused on the pile of coins around Chung Myung.

“...any?”

“Yes.”

“It is all piled up.”

“...”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened at the sight of the sect leader and the Five Swords staring at his silver coins.

“Hahaha. Our Chung Myung has saved up money again. How did you understand we were in need of money?”

“Ugh...”

Chung Myung, with his hair standing on end, swept up the silver coins and shook his head in resistance, but Hyun Jong just smiled kindly and slowly approached him.

“Now, you are such a good kid. Take a step back.”

The Five Swords also noticed and slowly approached Chung Myung.

“No. I am not trying to use them for anything bad.”

“The area around the Yangtze River is a mess right now. The Evil Faction is running rampant, so the people around us cannot even eat properly.”

“People who used to survive by sailing fishing boats are suddenly starving now?”

“Give me the money now.”

“I will use just a little, just a little.”

“Haa!”

Chung Myung scolded him, but in the end, the Five Swords and the sect leader rushed at him at once.

“Hold him!”

“Let me go!”

“Take out the coins! Hurry!”

Chung Myung, whose arms and legs were caught, resisted by screaming loudly.

“Ahhh! You beings without blood and tears! Are you even calling yourselves Taoists?”

“I told you that I will spend a little and give it back!”

“You said it takes a while for money to arrive back from Mount Hua!”

“Now, now, enough!”

“AHHH! You daylight robbers! Ughhh!”

It was the exact moment when Chung Myung began to shed tears.

“Kuaak!”

Jo Gul, who was cheering and pushing Chung Myung down, was kicked by something, flew away, and landed against the wall.

Kwang!

He slithered down the wall and soon began to foam at the mouth and tremble.

“W-what?”

“Is it the enemy?”

“Who?”

The Five Swords turned their heads in surprise and saw a person standing in front of them, radiating a bright blue qi from their entire body.

“E-elder?”

Hyun Young gritted his teeth while exuding this intense qi and then spoke as if chewing his words.

“You brats...!”

“...”

The Five Swords, completely shocked by his killing intent, shrank back in surprise.

“I said we’d see, but now you are grouping up to rob Taoists of their money?”

“Ah, no! That isn’t what is...”

“Well, we were...!”

The Five Swords, who were trying to make excuses, quickly turned their gaze to someone else. When Hyun Young was angry, the only person who could stop him was Hyun Jong.

But all they could see was the back of Hyun Jong, who was standing in the distance facing the wall.

Wah...

Wahh....

“Today, I will rip your brains out and fix them right! Come here, you bastards!”

“E-elder!”

“You have got it wrong!”

“S-spare me!”

Hyun Jong, who was standing looking at the wall, closed his eyes without a word.

‘I am sorry, kids.’

But there was nothing he could do about this.

Hehehe. Infinite Buddha.

“...”

The Five Swords were kneeling down with their heads lowered, sullen looks on their faces, with just their eyes slightly raised.

They saw Hyun Young radiating this killing intent and Chung Myung sitting next to him, chomping down on a sweet potato. Seeing Chung Myung laughing loudly every time Hyun Young stroked his head made them all feel their stomachs twisting inside.

‘Disgusting.’

‘I know what we did was wrong, but it’s still so disgusting to watch it.’

‘I want to beat him just once.’

“Why are you not raising your arms straight!”

The Five Swords were pricked by the fiery words that fell like lightning and raised their arms.

“These mountain bandits! Now you’re trying to use other people’s money as if it’s your own! Still, these people are Taoists!”

The two mountain bandits, Im So-Byeong and the first elder, who were watching this from the corner, shrank their necks in annoyance. They were not people to be intimidated by, but how could they handle someone who beat up the Five Swords like wild dogs and silenced the sect leader of Mount Hua with just a couple of words?

“... that... that isn’t what we intended to use it for.”

“We are in dire need of money, but it will take time to bring grain and money from Mount Hua...”

“Still, you bastards!”

As Hyun Young shouted loudly, the Five Swords bowed their heads.

“A Taoist should never justify the means for the purpose! Where in the world was that money yours?”

“...”

“It is good to help those who are hungry! But how can it be fair to steal money from a decent person to provide for the underprivileged? If you are in need of money, you should go out and earn it or borrow it! You are trying to gain a good name by stealing someone else’s money!”

“W-we were in the wrong!”

The Five Swords received so much criticism that Hyun Jong, who was hurt inside, silently opened his mouth.

“Hyun Young... it wasn’t something done with bad intentions...”

“Sect leader, the same applies to you!”

“....”

“Is this the money of Mount Hua? What did Mount Hua do with the money? Chung Myung made all the plans, and the Green Forest did all the work! And the Tang Family built the buildings around us!”

“T-that is true, but the situation is still a bit...”

“A man who just came over after work and takes things around! Why do you want to use my disciples’ money as your own? Does all the money in Mount Hua belong to the sect leader? What? Then you will take away my money, too, right?”

“I-it wasn’t our intention. I am being honest!”

“Then what? What is the point of going that far? That is the same as robbery! Is there even a noble figure like the Sect leader who is trying to build good fortune by robbing his disciples? Is this a Taoist sect? Is this even a sect? This is just like the Evil Faction!”

Finally, when Hyun Young closed his eyes again, Hyun Jong quietly folded his hands together and bowed his head.

“They are pure trash who cannot even make a single penny! Even though you thought you were rich, you were upset to see us spending all the money we earned! What now? Are you trying to take money from someone who fixed the problems for you and now has to survive by eating grass? There is a level of ingratitude!”

“Hehe, elder, please don’t be so harsh. Still, it is a sahyung and sasuk relationship for us, and he is the sect leader.”

At one word from Chung Myung, Hyun Young’s frowning eyes suddenly took on a gentle glow. He patted Chung Myung on the head and admired him.

“Oh my! Oh my, my Chung Myung! He also has a kind heart, of all things! How can he be so nice! How?”

“Hehehe! Hehehe!”

Baek Cheon cursed under his breath at how Hyun Young said it without hesitation.

There were as many people as grains of sand in the Central Plains, but there was only one person in the world who could call that demon of a bastard a good person—Hyun Young.

“If you try to touch my kid’s money one more time, you will live on nothing but your blood for the rest of your life! Did you get it?”

“... Yes.”

The leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the leader of Mount Hua, answered politely. Then, he carefully raised one hand.

“Um, but... Hyun Young, no, Finance Hall leader.”

“What is it?”

“... I am really in a hurry for money right now, so can we not do it?”

“If you are in a hurry for money, borrow it! Borrow and pay a fair interest for it!”

“Is this it?”

Hyun Jong looked at Hyun Young with a shocked expression on his face, then secretly turned his gaze to Chung Myung.

“Uhm... Chung Myung. I am saying this because I need money desperately, but I can pay you back really soon. Before that, I’d like to borrow some money for a moment. How much will the interest be for it...”

“I-interest?”

“Ah, yes, interest.”

“Interest?”

Chung Myung gulped the sweet potato he was chewing and smiled.

“How much is the amount, and what is the duration?”

“...”

Hyun Jong realized once again that life wasn’t so easy.

Clank.

A mountain of coins was piled up.

Seeing the money stacked in front of him was something he could have smiled at and enjoyed. Still, in reality, Hyun Jong’s eyes as he looked at the coins were filled with an inexplicable sorrow.

“Ehehehe.”

“...”

“I am giving it to you cheaply because you are the sect leader. If it were anyone else, I would have charged twice as much. Woah, this really shouldn’t be done like this. Normally, the closer you are to each other, the more certain the money transactions between the two are supposed to be, but I have a weak heart when it comes to my people.”

Hyun Jong, who was listening to Chung Myung whispering, looked up at the ceiling in silence.

‘Ancestors’

He apologized.

Mount Hua had now become a place where the disciples committed usury to the Sect Leader.

Hyun Jong was in despair and held his face with both hands.

When it came to debt and usury, the person who woke up from sleep to worry was Hyun Jong. When he thought about what he once suffered, his head still ached.

But he never thought he would have to borrow money not from someone else but from his disciple and also pay interest.

“I-isn’t it compound interest?”

“You just need to pay the deposit within the agreed date. Why worry about the other things?”

That was something he had heard a lot...

“Then... what happens if I cannot meet that deadline?”

“Ehh. You will pay it back on time, so don’t bother asking. Hehe.”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes tightly.

They said that a tiger came in where a wolf had been defeated, and a devourer came in where a debtor had been resolved.

His disciple...

However, Hyun Young, who was with him for most of his life, seemed to have a slightly different opinion.

“Money transactions must be done with certainty! Ah! You seem like one amazing guy!”

As he watched Hyun Young nodding his head in satisfaction, Hyun Jong had the urge to turn his head back. He glared at Chung Myung, who was laughing again.

That bastard!

That guy who didn’t back down and was sure of his calculations!

... Uh? Was this turning out to be a compliment?

At that moment, Hyun Young’s eyes fell on the Five Swords.

“You people!

“Uh?”

“You are prohibited from spending any personal expenses money for 3 months from now. No more money or bonus for you!”

“Uh? W-why?”

Jo Gul asked with wide eyes, so surprised that the end of his voice went high.

Since the Mount Hua disciples spent most of their time training, there was no place for them to make money. Only those whose families were wealthy received money from home. Still, most of them hadn’t received support from their families for a long time since Mount Hua began to earn money and paid the disciples for their expenses.

But suddenly that money stopped! There was no stronger shock than this!

“Then? You tried to use other people’s money to give out donations and want to spend your money on yourself? Such condescending nature?”

“... uh, that...”

Hyun Young spoke firmly.

“They say that talent is for bears and money is for people! You are the right example of it! If you want to cooperate and make people benefit, then of course you will have to take losses. Why are you taking everyone you need and making yourself feel better? That too with other people’s money?”

“Ah, e-even so, you are taking away even this small amount of pocket money! I’d rather die! You have no blood or tears...”

“What, you bastard?”

The moment Yoon Jong was about to throw his fist and punch Jo Gul, something like lightning came and kicked Jo Gul.

“Gul!”

“...”

Yoon Jong gulped when he saw the leg stretched out in front of him.

“Your head is for nothing. You need to get hit.”

Right, hit.

“That’s right. But that doesn’t mean you can just kick people... even your thoughts have changed a lot.”

Yu Yiseol slowly folded her long legs, calmly knelt down again, and raised her arms.

Yoon Jong, seeing her eyes, flinched and quickly put his arms close to his ears.

“... We will do as you say, elder.”

Along with Baek Cheon’s soft reply, the other Five Swords nodded.

“We will do it.”

“Yes.”

“Ugh... if you were going to be like this, you could have answered before I got beaten...”

“You spoke out before I could answer.”

The Five Swords sighed deeply. However, he didn’t seem to be very dissatisfied because he was stung by the comment that he was trying to show off with other people’s money.

Giving meant giving away something of one’s own, not giving something away someone else’s?

At that time, Hyun Jong, with slightly complicated feelings, quietly moved the coins in front of him to the side. Then, he calmed down and looked around.

’Anyway, so...“

Before he knew it, Mount Hua’s leading people had gathered in the room.

‘Let us discuss the current situation... right, let’s discuss it.“

“Yes, sect leader.”

He had already lost the reputation of the sect leader. Still, it didn’t matter since his reputation wasn’t great to begin with.

“You may have all heard, but the situation around us isn’t so good. After the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families signed the non-aggression pact on the Yangtze, this area turned into the sect’s place.”

When Hyun Jong looked at Hyun Sang, he nodded and said.

“Currently, the area around the Yangtze River is literally teeming with Evil Faction clans. It is said that those who normally don’t appear near the mouth of the river are also running rampant.“

“Seriously, those shameless thieves!”

“You should throw them all into the river and drown them!”

“This is why they are called evil!”

In response to the reactions coming from all over, Hyun Jong glanced at a corner of the room with a slightly awkward look. He was concerned about one person who was becoming increasingly different.

Hyun Jong cleared his throat.

“... Be careful of what you say.”

“Ah...”

The people who finally noticed the Green Forest King crouching in the corner scratched the backs of their heads with slightly pitiful expressions.

“That wasn’t my intention, Green Forest King.”

“Yes, ahem. Not all thieves are the same. Bandits are a bit better.”

“They are all thieves; what is different about them?”

“Chung Myung, stay quiet, please.”

But Im So-Byeong continued to be explained to by Hyung Sang calmly.

“As a result, the damage to the civilian population is severe. Not only is it a problem to be plagued by thieves, but merchant ships cannot operate properly, so they cannot work, and those who drive fishing boats are also unable to get into the river. Of course, there is no other choice but to be in poverty...”

“Hmmm.”

Hyun Jong, who thought for a moment, quietly looked at Baek Cheon.

“What was it like seeing it in person?”

“The situation is serious. This is not a one-day or two-day thing; it has been going on since the war began on the Yangtze River... after a little while, deaths from starvation may begin to appear one after another.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

Of course, there were people who knew all of this because they saw it firsthand, but it was important to let everyone know what they knew from a person of higher position. This was more so for Mount Hua. Hyun Jong spoke after confirming that everyone understood the situation.

“The wealth of Mount Hua from the nearby Eunha merchants is being transferred to the Yangtze River. As soon as the grain and wealth arrive here, we will address the urgent needs first, so everyone please help us.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

At that time, Hyun Young, who had been listening quietly, opened his mouth with a slightly displeased look.

“There is a limit to mercy too.”

“I know.”

“Sect leader, I am also a Taoist. I am not dissatisfied with the wealth being used to save people. But that cannot be the entire solution to the problem. Our Mount Hua’s treasure trove isn’t eternal, right? I cannot take responsibility for others forever.”

Hyun Jong agreed with that and nodded. Worldly affairs could not be solved with the heart alone. Of course, realistic aspects must also be taken into consideration.

In this situation, there was one person who gave the most accurate opinion, and Hyun Jong’s eyes turned to that person.

“Chung Myung. Your thoughts?”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung tapped his cheek a couple of times with his finger and then said.

“The Evil Faction doesn’t have patience, nor are they warriors, so they cannot make a fuss for long. The situation is as such for now, but soon it will lose its momentum and become quiet. Then, most things around will turn normal. The problem is that the waterway is blocked, and the workers have no place to work right now...”

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders.

“We already have a shortage of manpower, so we hired workers. It would be nice to just bring in free manpower and do it for them, but... well, bandits are people who don’t know how to work right in the first place, so if we continue like this, problems will arise.”

Then, Chung Myung, who was irritated for no reason, glared at Im So-Byeong.

“If the King of Green Forest had some dignity, he could have taken the situation seriously.”

“... I am sorry for being so disrespectful.”

“If you know that, then it’s good enough.”

Hyun Jong sighed lightly and told Hyun Young.

“That being said, let’s spend our money for a while. Aren’t our Mount Hua disciples also responsible for the situation on the Yangtze River turning out like this?”

“Even if we aren’t responsible, you would have still used it.”

“Hehe, please understand.”

Hyun Young pouted but no longer objected to Hyun Jong’s words.

When the surrounding noise died down a little, Im So-Byeong cleared his throat and quietly spoke.

“The reason the sect leader left me here to hold the meeting was probably to hear my opinion, so I will speak.”

“I just started the meeting without much thought...”

“...”

“D-do speak.”

Im So-Byeong began his explanation in a slightly timid voice.

“Aren’t the merchants from the Yangtze River flocking to the mouth now? If we hire workers there at a good price, even the workers’ families will move in together.”

“Could be.”

“Then it would turn into a city.”

“Hm? City?”

Hyun Jong blinked at the sudden words.

“Isn’t that too far a concept to touch?”

But Im So-Byeong smiled and shook his head lightly.

“A city isn’t that big of a deal. Any place where many people live together is a city. Even if we don’t do anything, people who gather will build houses on their own, and if merchants see people gathering, they will come. A city will be created in no time.”

“Hmm. When I think about it, it seems fine.”

Shak!

Im So-Byeong spread out his fan and covered it under his eyes. Both eyes shone.

“Then what we need to do is securely take over that city and turn it into a land for the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

When everyone was nodding their heads as if fascinated by the mentor-like form, Chung Myung’s eyes suddenly widened.

“How can you spread out a fan in front of the sect leader? Will you not take it down?”

“...”

Im So-Byeong, who turned sullen, quietly lowered his fan and continued speaking.

“The city provides money and connections. Now, Mount Hua, the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and the Green Forest have seized an unprecedented opportunity. With this city as the center, we can exert influence throughout the Yangtze River and, furthermore, spread the majesty of our alliance across the Central Plains.”

“How dare you sneakily insert the Green Forest into the topic? Do you want to die?”

“Ah, don’t be so harsh. Just include it! I did join the Heavenly Friends Alliance, right?”

“What? Why don’t you just go and get an official post next?”

“Actually, I did try it...”

Hyun Jong sighed and covered his face.

‘Please, let us continue with the meeting, you people...’

Hyun Jong quickly opened his mouth before the conversation could progress further.

“So, the Green Forest King said that our Mount Hua... no, you think the Heavenly Friends Alliance should do so?”

“We can do it.”

“Uh?”

Im So-Byeong smiled.

“Sect leader, you just have to do what you have done so far. There is nothing more to do.”

“... Is that alright?”

“Yes.”

Im So-Byeong nodded slowly and spoke seriously.

“Those who have already gathered here remember the name of Mount Hua and consider you as the guardian. So they will naturally have a sense of belonging to Mount Hua... cough! Cough! Ah... one second.”

Im So-Byeong coughed several times and continued to speak with a red face.

“If they accept Mount Hua as their sect, they will consume what Mount Hua gives and then try to do what Mount Hua shows. They will push Mount Hua ahead, and Mount Hua will protect them. One exploits the other naturally... no, that... harmony? Ah, no... damn it, what is this called?”

The disciples of Mount Hua narrowed their eyes and looked at Im So-Byeong as if to say, ‘Ah, that is right.’

‘That thief...’

‘So this is why they are called bandits.’

‘Look at that thing going on in his head. He is the same as Chung Myung.’

As if to prove the silent accusation, Chung Myung stepped ahead to help Im So-Byeong.

“Virtuous cycle! It is a Virtuous Cycle!”

“Ah! Right! The virtuous cycle! A virtuous cycle will occur. Then all is well. Hahaha.”

Im So-Byeong smiled brightly. Then, he suddenly mumbled something with a confused look on his face.

“No, but this couldn’t be easy... it is ridiculous to even think of it. If this was going to happen as such, why go for bandits? We could have been a Justice faction too. If you just pretend to be nice, people will give you the money back... Woah, the more I think about it, the more unfair it seems?”

“Get your mind together.”

“Ah, I am sorry. One moment.”

“...”

Im So-Byeong, who noticed the distrust coming in, smiled awkwardly.

“So, in conclusion, what we need to do is seize the commercial potential of the Yangtze and protect those who come there.”

“... I didn’t start this with this much thought in mind.”

Im So-Byeong heard the complaints inside Hyun Jong’s mumbling.

If one thought about it, all of this was done by the Four Precepts Alliance and the Nine Great Sects. And now they could swallow this meal whole just by raising the chopsticks.

Building a city on the Yangtze River, pushing people into it, and making it into their own land? This was a difficult task for a nation to do. But something so ridiculous was happening now.

“The question is, how will those Evil Faction bastards react?”

It was at that moment that a little worry bloomed in Im So-Byeong’s eyes.

“Sect leader! Is the sect leader here?”

“Uh?”

Hyun Jong turned his head and looked at the door. This rushed voice came from outside.

“I am Hong Dae-Kwang of the Beggars Union! I stopped by because there was something I needed to share.”

“Oh, branch leader Hong. Please come in now.”

When the people at the entrance opened the door, Hong Dae-Kwang came inside.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had just paid his respects to Hyun Jong, opened his mouth with a dark face.

“Sect leader... the Four Precepts Alliance have done something.”

Those words were enough to darken Hyun Jong’s expression.

“Did you say the Four Precepts Alliance?”

Hong Dae-Kwang nodded at the slightly loud voice of Hyun Jong.

“Yes! Sect leader. The Four Precepts Alliance has declared Unification of the Evil Way.”

“Unification of the Evil Way...”

A groan escaped Hyun Jong’s mouth as he repeated the phrase.

Meanwhile, Jo Gul looked at Yoon Jong with a puzzled expression, clearly not understanding what had just been said.

“Uh... sahyung.”

“What?”

“What is this Unification of the Evil Way?”

Yoon Jong, shocked, turned to look at Jo Gul. Jo Gul, noticing the contempt on Yoon Jong’s face, protested in defense.

“Ah, no, I know what it means! Aren’t they trying to unite the Evil Sects?”

“Ugh... I am glad you know.”

“No, but hasn’t the Evil Faction already been unified? The sects did come together as one... of course, there is the Green Forest. Still, now half of Green Forest belongs to Heavenly Friends Alliance and has almost completely stepped back from banditry, so it is difficult to call it an Evil Faction....”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who heard those words, looked toward Jo Gul and shook his head.

“That’s half right and half wrong.”

“Uh?”

“It is true that Shenzhou Five Sects represents the Evil Faction. But it cannot be said that they are all the Evil Sects out there. Aren’t there many such sects in the world?”

“R-right.”

The Galho Clan, which Jo Gul took down, was one of the Evil Sects.

“Now the Four Precepts Alliance has declared that it will bring all the Evil Sects in the world under its control.”

“Uh? All?”

Only then did Jo Gul understand what the Unification of the Evil Way meant, and he felt flustered.

“Is that alright? The...”

“It is a bit tough. That is the habit of the Evil Faction in the first place.”

“Habit?”

When Jo Gul asked what he meant by that, Hong Dae-Kwang clicked his tongue.

“Think about it. They are the Evil Faction. Evil Faction! These are the people who absolutely hate rules and order. Would such people bow their heads to others? Why do you think the Evil Faction is pushed aside by the Justice Faction?”

“...because their martial arts are weak?”

“Wrong.”

“Then?”

“It is because they cannot create such a big force.”

Baek Cheon asked as if he didn’t understand.

“But isn’t the Shenzhou Five Sects comparable to the Nine Great Sects?”

“Right, right. But there is a limit. The Evil Faction couldn’t get any bigger than that. When the power was expanded beyond it, if the leader’s control didn’t reach the corners, there would be internal fights and the entire faction would split.”

“Ah...”

“The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families are all sects that are hundreds of years old. But, even if the Evil Faction is large, it cannot survive for a hundred years. Because over time, it collapses on its own.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face contorted.

“So, think about it. It is normal for the Evil Faction to be divided even within themselves, so does it make sense to truly bow down to another faction?”

Hyun Jong was deep in thought, letting out a sigh, and opened his mouth.

“Branch leader Hong.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“But as far as I know, the Evil Faction also gives in to the strong sects?”

“If you look at the inside story, it is a bit different. Some sects line up near Mount Hua, right?”

“...”

“...None?”

“That...uh...”

At that time, Chung Myung poked him on the side.

“Ah, we are in Xi’an now.”

“R-right! We are! There are many!”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang felt like he had asked for something he couldn’t say, so he continued to speak hesitantly, avoiding the gaze of Hyun Jong.

“Yes... that, uh... in any case, just because there are sects lined up, the higher sect cannot give instructions to the lower clans. Isn’t that something which cannot be done even to the sub-sects of a sect because of the principles of the Justice faction?”

“Yes.”

“The reason for lining up is simply to gain an advantage. The same goes for the Evil Faction. But what the Four Precepts Alliance is saying now is not just to bring the lower clans into line. The idea is to subjugate them and make them their slaves. The idea is to bring together the many sects in the world that are tagged as Evil under the name of the Evil Faction. In other words....”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lip and said.

“Only the Evil Faction will remain in the world; the Evil Faction will be the Four Precepts Alliance, and the Four Precepts Alliance will become the Evil Faction.”

“W-what is that?”

Hyun Jong’s face turned slightly pale. Only now did they understand how tremendous the declaration of unification was.

“Is that possible?”

“Of course, it isn’t. If it were possible, there would have been countless people who would have attempted such a thing by now. But...”

When Hong Dae-Kwang hesitated to speak, Chung Myung took over.

“That very impossible thing is possible now.”

“Hm?”

“With the emergence of the Four Precepts Alliance, the powerful sects are no longer able to keep each other in check, and the Justice faction, who step in at the right time and take advantage of the situation, are also unable to approach this land.”

“Hmm... that is...”

“But.”

Chung Myung smiled and laughed.

‘I thought they would be busy handling the internal aspects... so they are going to come out like this? The land where the knife was cut probably didn’t even heal yet, and yet Jang Ilso, that bastard....“

In Chung Myung’s opinion, the current Jang Ilso was no different from a greedy pig. No matter how great he was, there was no way he could swallow the entire pirates, Black Ghost Fort, and Lower District Sect at the same time.

So he thought he would have a hard time sorting things out, but it turned out to be the opposite of what Chung Myung expected.

“What do you think will happen?”

“Hmm.”

Im So-Byeong, receiving Chung Myung’s gaze, scratched his chin.

“Actually... although this is extreme, it is a reasonable number. When there is conflict internally, it is best to create enemies on the outside.”

“Right.”

“If the internal reorganization continues like this, there will eventually be a power struggle among the Four Sects Alliance. But if they turn their knives outside, whether they are strong or weak, they won’t be biting down on each other’s throats for a while. And that could be what Jang Ilso is aiming for.”

Chung Myung’s eyes sunk in.

“I don’t think that is all.”

“Uh?”

“Unification of the Evil Faction. This war is none other than a war carried out in the name of the Evil Faction.”

“... isn’t that obvious?”

“In other words, those who subjugate are also subjugated in the name of the Evil Faction.”

“Ah!”

Im So-Byeong flinched as if he had missed that.

“Right, then....”

Hyun Jong, unable to follow their conversation, asked with a stern look.

“Chung Myung. What does that mean?”

“When the Shenzhou Five Sects subjugate the Evil Faction sects in each land, the subjugated sects’ lands come under them. But if they subjugate in the name of the Four Precepts Alliance, they will follow the orders. And so...”

“... are you saying that all of that power will turn up under Jang Ilso, the Alliance leader? Everyone coming under his command from now on?”

“Precisely.”

Chung Myung smiled, showing his teeth.

“A crazy person is doing a crazy thing.”

In every aspect of the world, there were stages. The reason you needed to take that step was that the consequences of ignoring the rule were significant.

They had just achieved a form of union, yet they were enriching themselves while neglecting internal maintenance? And leading the other sects of the Shenzhou Five Sects, which could not be considered as much, to completely surrender to him?

This was no different from a blatant provocation directed at the other leaders of the Shenzhou sects.

Baek Cheon asked with a stern face.

“Of course, there will be backlash, right?”

“You must have the confidence to press that, right? If it succeeds, the benefits will surely be great.”

No matter how great the sects belonging to the Shenzhou Five Sects were, if it attracted all the other sects, its power could not be taken lightly. No, at least in terms of numbers, it would be more than enough to overwhelm the other sects.

If that happened, even the Black Dragon King, Thousand Faced Scholar, and Black Ghost Fort leaders wouldn’t dare touch Jang Ilso.

“And if they fail?”

“Death.”

Chung Myung giggled.

“He will die the most gruesome death without leaving a single piece of flesh behind. He probably knows that.”

All small and medium-sized sects that protest would be trampled and subjugated. In the meantime, he must keep in check the other leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects, who might try to stab him in the back at any time.

This was an incredibly dangerous tightrope Jang Ilso chose to walk on.

But now that the lingering effects of war hadn’t subsided yet, Jang Ilso had declared that he would continue on this tightrope.

“What do you think? The odds of success?”

Chung Myung frowned at Baek Cheon’s question.

“... can he? No, maybe... half and half?”

“Half?”

At that level, it was a close gamble and even he would not be sure of winning.

“That Jang Ilso?”

Baek Cheon unconsciously made a remark about Jang Ilso, but there was no need to change his words.

“I am giving him that much chance because it is Jang Ilso. If it were any other guy, he would have already died and turned into dog food by now.”

On the contrary, Chung Myung held Jang Ilso in high regard.

“Then...”

Baek Cheon gulped and opened his mouth. He was torn between asking immediately or waiting, but he couldn’t hold back.

“... What if Jang Ilso succeeds despite the slim odds? Then what happens?”

“What if he succeeds?”

“Yes, then what?”

Chung Myung twisted his lips into a smile.

“On the outside, it might look like the Four Precept Alliance has unified the Evil Way, but in reality, it will be like the Ten Thousand People Clan swallowing the Evil Faction. In other words...“

“...”

“An unprecedented Evil Sect, which has never appeared before, will be born.”

Chills ran down Baek Cheon’s spine.

“And Jang Ilso will become the only person to lead the greatest sects of all. This has never happened before in the history of Kangho.”

A dead silence fell in the hall.

It was such a huge statement that they had no idea how to react.

Chung Myung’s low laughter echoed through the silence.

People looked at Chung Myung with blank faces.

“An insane person just does insane things. If you’re not going to get everything, it doesn’t matter if you don’t get torn, right?”

For the moment, Jang Ilso would have endured again and again.

Now, Jang Ilso had started to move.

He would never stop until he devoured the entire world or burned it down. Even if he swallowed too much, his stomach exploded, and he died, or even if he burned himself from the fire he made!

Because that was the way people like Jang Ilso lived their lives.

Meanwhile, Baek Cheon’s face, looking at Chung Myung, was stiff.

Everyone was weighed down by the gravity of Jang Ilso’s actions, but only Chung Myung seemed happy.

Maybe it was an illusion...

“I am curious, too.”

At that moment, Chung Myung spoke with a bizarre smile.

“Is Jang Ilso really capable of keeping the entire Evil Faction under his command, or is he just an idiot who will explode and puke out everything he inhales?”

Chung Myung’s gaze turned to the door. To be exact, beyond the door.

Until now, the world seemed to be waiting for Chung Myung to make a move. But now, the world is starting to come upon Chung Myung.

‘This is how it stays interesting.’

Chung Myung clenched his fists.

“But... well, the result is the same. I will prove to him that the mistakes he makes are irreversible.”

He fixed his gaze to the south and smiled, showing his teeth.

And maybe.

Someone far away, out of sight, might be looking at them and smiling.

Step. Step. Step.

Splash.

Pure white silk shoes stepped into a puddle of blood.

The man frowned in displeasure as his footwear was stained by the blood that splashed up.

“...this is why I hate blood.”

The man, who shakily stepped out, raised his head and looked ahead. His face, once irritated, soon filled with joy.

“But... I like red.”

The whole world was turning red.

The ground was soaked with blood that flowed endlessly, and bloodied corpses were lined up. Moreover, as the flames burning the palace soared, even the black sky was turning red.

The only thing there that wasn’t red was the man.

“Ugh... uhh...”

Jang Ilso, a man whose entire body was wrapped in a long white cloth, turned his head towards the source of the groans.

A man with one arm and leg cut off was writhing and groaning in a puddle of blood.

Jang Ilso slowly walked in that direction. Then he quietly squatted in front of the man and lightly pushed his forehead with his index finger.

“Ja... Jang Il...”

The man’s body trembled like a thin tree.

Although nothing was waiting for someone who was already dying, when he made eye contact with Jang Ilso, he was filled with fear, as if he had encountered a demon from hell.

“Scared?”

“Uh... huh...”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Well, it would have been nice if you had listened when I was saying something nice. Then, you wouldn’t have to lose your strength for no reason and could continue to enjoy your glory.”

“...”

“What does it matter what happens to a small clan like this? It would have been just fine if you had come under me and lived happily till the end. Such a moron.”

Bloody tears flowed from the man’s eyes.

It was true that he flatly rejected the Four Precepts Alliance’s offer to give up everything and be their dog. But who would have imagined that just because the offer was refused, such a cruel act would be performed?

Everyone died.

Nothing in this palace, not a single ant, was alive.

The Ten Thousand People Clan’s demons came without notice, cutting, killing, and trampling everyone in this palace. Everyone who had been laughing and talking with him a moment ago had died at the hands of those people.

“Ugh...”

The leader of the Ghost Face Clan, Ghost Face Black Tiger, Mo Song’s face was contorted.

“Why... why so... why...”

“Ahh. I know, I know.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“I know what you want to say. You want to say that you didn’t intend to put up such a resistance?”

“...”

“If we had come and just threatened, you would have kneeled down at any time. No, no. If we had told you beforehand that we would give you preferential treatment, you would have licked my shoe at any time. I guess that is what you want to say?”

Mo Song’s trembling head nodded slowly.

The sight of the blood-soaked man trembling and nodding his head wasn’t pleasant. But Jang Ilso just smiled brightly.

“You pitiful friend. Think about it. If I listened to every word, I would end up spending my entire time meeting the heads of the Evil Sects. Isn’t that right?”

“...”

“There is a much simpler way than that. I will tell you. So listen carefully.”

Jang Ilso put his mouth to Mo Song’s ear and whispered softly.

“You just crush one suitable place. And the corpses are laid out in a row without being cleaned at all. Then wouldn’t the rumor spread quickly before the body even rots? The clan that rejected the offer of the vicious bastard, Jang Ilso, was massacred without a single rat left behind. The same thing will happen to the other sects that reject the proposal.”

“Uh... Uh...”

Mo Song’s body was visibly shaking.

Jang Ilso lightly patted his shoulder.

“Do you know why I am telling you this?”

“... w... why...?”

A pale smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s beautifully decorated face.

“It’s because I am grateful.”

“...”

“I don’t have any ill feelings towards you because you didn’t do anything particularly wrong. From my perspective, there was just a sect of the right size in just the right place.”

Bloody tears, mixed with hatred, pain, and sadness, flowed from Mo Song’s eyes, but Jang Ilso seemed to feel no emotion.

“Thanks to you, things will be twice as easy. So take it easy. My nature is generous... right. No, that would be a bit difficult. Your corpse has no meaning unless it rots here.”

“Uh... Uhhhhack!”

In the end, Mo Song couldn’t hold back the anger and rushed at Jang Ilso as if he were going to bite him. But, of course, it failed. Jang Ilso calmly grabbed Mo Song’s face as he crawled.

And he tightened his hand so hard that it felt like his skull would break at any moment.

“Ughh... ughhh....”

Mo Song grabbed Jang Ilso’s wrist with his remaining hand. But no matter how hard he tried, Jang Ilso wouldn’t budge and just slowly squeezed his face.

“Oh. You don’t get what I am saying. I tried to send you off in a good way.”

With a smile like a demon, Jang Ilso growled.

“It looks like you still don’t get why you are dying.”

Crack. Crack!

“Ku... ugh....”

The blood in Mo Song’s eyes began to rush out. He struggled and scratched Jang Ilso’s arm, but Jang Ilso didn’t move, as if it didn’t even bother him.

“It is a mortal sin to have no strength and no sense. Even a human being like me lives by paying attention to what everyone thinks, so what does a weak person believe in? If you believed that the world would work as you please for the weak, you would have nothing to say even if you died.”

“Ugh!”

Puak!

In the end, a short groan and a terrible sound rang out, and Mo Song’s body went limp.

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso got up from the place and shook off his dirty hand.

“Alliance lord.”

Ho Ga-Myung approached him and quietly greeted him.

“The arrangement is done.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso glanced around.

“It is still a bit lacking. Let us make more of a mess here and scatter the corpses everywhere.”

“I will.”

“Y-yes.”

Jang Ilso nodded with a gentle face.

“Spread the money elsewhere. I am sorry for surprising them, and tell them that when the Alliance takes over from now, it will be different from the days when they were running rampant.”

“... Is that really needed? Warriors will have no choice but to do what they are told anyway?”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Ga-Myung, you always take the right path, but then you say we aren’t good enough. War is about reducing enemies and increasing allies. There are already too many enemies, so there is no need to create more, even if they are the most insignificant enemies.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand and added quietly.

“If I can make them on my side now for a few pennies, that is a cheap price to pay.”

“I will do as you asked.”

“Y-yes.”

Jang Ilso seemed lost in thought for a moment while looking at the corpses scattered around.

“Aren’t the others moving yet?”

“Yes, it looks like they are taking a wait-and-see approach for now.”

“Those morons.”

The leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects were probably confused.

“They must be busy thinking their minds out to figure out what position they should ask for in the Alliance. I wonder how far they would push their luck.”

“Isn’t that the nature of humans?”

“Because they are doing such an obvious trick, they are still stuck here, eating dirt.”

Jang Ilso touched his lips. The smell of blood rising from his fingertips lifted the flesh.

‘They are just pigs who are well fed.’

Livestock locked in cages live with only the thought of what they will eat each day. So, they would never come out against them.

To become a wolf, you must not be satisfied with the prey you caught. From the moment the hunt ended, you had to think about the next prey, aim for it, and bite again.

“Alliance lord... this is how we handled the Ghost Face Clan, but even with such warnings, not everyone will follow you.”

“I know.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand dismissively.

“There is no need for everyone to follow. Those who do can be trampled, and those who don’t can be killed.”

“... You mean all those who don’t follow?”

“Why? Is there any problem?”

Ho Ga-Myung fell silent, looking at Jang Ilso, who asked calmly.

Only Jang Ilso would dare to do something like this.

He was the Alliance Lord of the Four Precepts Alliance and the man who tricked the Nine Great Sects into a humiliating treaty. All of the Evil Faction people in the world were paying attention to Jang Ilso.

But even if pushed by his achievements, at least half of the Evil Faction in the Southlands would refuse to join.

But killing all of them?

“Tsk tsk, Ga-Myung. I can see all your thoughts on your face.”

“... I am sorry. There are times when I cannot follow your thoughts.”

“It will happen sooner or later. What is the difference between killing him beforehand or later?”

“...”

“You have to push yourself even if it’s too hard. Every time the Evil Sects form an alliance, the results are always the same. We always get defeated by the Justice Faction.”

“... Right.”

“But right now, the Justice Faction cannot unite.”

Jang Ilso revealed this.

“The fire in the Southlands isn’t important. What is important is that distrust has been planted among them. It means that we blame each other, taunt each other, and end up hating each other. In that situation, you won’t be able to gather under someone’s flag.”

“A-Alliance lord.”

“This is a unique chance. If it weren’t for this one moment, I, too, would become a pig in the Southlands. Before their blood dries and their wounds scab over and heal, they clean up the Southlands and head north. If that happens...”

Crack.

Jang Ilso clenched his fists.

“I will have the world at my feet.”

Ho Ga-Myung trembled and prostrated himself on the spot.

“Of course, it will happen! Hegemony Lord!”

“Tsk! This guy flatters me so much.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand.

“It isn’t that big of a deal. That’s also a story from when things went well. If you deviate even a little, you will just become greedy and become a fool. Then, you, too, will eventually die a pitiful death.”

“It doesn’t matter.”

“Hmm?”

Ho Ga-Myung smiled.

“I will be able to die with a smile because I have stood at the side of the Alliance Lord.”

“...”

Jang Ilso, who was looking at him intently, smiled.

“Strange.”

Jang Ilso turned around and spoke again in a cold voice.

“Get it sorted. I will have to clean up a couple more places tonight.”

“Yes!”

Jang Ilso walked slowly.

‘The die is thrown.’

He also didn’t start with full confidence. This is a test of himself and a challenge to fate.

But he knew one thing for sure.

There were times when you had to organize and move everything, and other times when you had to step into a black fog where you could not see an inch ahead.

What he needed now was the will to take that one step.

‘Nothing can stop me.’

The same went for anything else in the world. A world filled with peace-loving idiots who only cared about their own well-being could not stop him. Never.

“But...”

If there was anything holding him back...

Jang Ilso’s gaze turned south. A distant place that couldn’t be seen from there.

He showed his teeth and grinned eerily.

“But so do you. The Sword Demon of Mount Hua.”

The small stream flowed and became a river, and now it had turned into a rough, turbulent river. No one in the world will be able to stop this flow anymore.

Not even Jang Ilso.

Over a dozen sects were set on fire in just a few days.

There were countless Evil Sects in the Central Plains. While destruction and fights were common in the world of Evil Sects, where the winners take it all, this was not an ordinary occurrence.

Those who heard the news could not dismiss it lightly.

First, the extinct sects did not just collapse; there were no survivors left.

Second, it was because of Jang Ilso that the sects were destroyed.

Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso, the clan leader of the Ten Thousand People clan and the symbol of the Four Precepts Alliance.

He was a person who immediately united the Evil Sects, which had been at odds with one another for a long time. He brought about a humiliating treaty upon the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who had dominated Kangho for the past 100 years.

He achieved what no one else dared to dream of turning into reality.

His fame soared to new heights, and the fear his name inspired increased significantly compared to the past.

The fact that Jang Ilso personally took the initiative to exterminate the Evil Sects was telling.

The Unification of the Evil Sects he declared did not end with words. The message that death awaited those who did not agree was clear.

Kangho was burning.

The Evil Sects were forced to make a choice.

Just as Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung had done before, they had to choose between a humiliating existence or extinction.

Of course, they tried to resist. They attempted to question and understand.

But after witnessing other clans and sects reduced to nothing but ashes, they had no choice but to keep their mouths shut and remain silent.

They also sensed it: the direction of Jang Ilso’s madness was by no means limited to the Justice faction. To him, everything was something to trample and burn.

The Justice Factions also seemed shocked.

If it had been before, they would have shouted and advanced south. Everyone would have said with one voice that a major war could be avoided if they stepped on them before they gathered more strength.

But they couldn’t do it now.

This was because those who were supposed to kill the Evil Sects by standing in the vanguard position couldn’t even set foot in the Southlands.

Even if they wanted to, they now knew their place.

Only when Jang Ilso came to the fore did people realize how much of a role the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families had played. And that reality soon turned to terrible hatred against the Wudang, Shaolin, and Namgung families, who decided something on their own.

It was the same for the Nine Great Sects.

Whenever something like this happened, they always came together and formed a coalition centered around Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung. It was like that when the Demonic Sect had invaded, and it was as such when more foreign forces stepped into the Central Plains. So, it had to be the same now.

But that couldn’t happen. This was because of the hatred towards those sects, which arbitrarily concluded this non-aggression pact with other sects in order to save their own disciples, and this fact burned strong for the Justice faction.

Because they hated it, they couldn’t join together, and because they couldn’t join, they couldn’t talk. Since they couldn’t talk, they couldn’t unite, and as they didn’t unite, they lacked a solution.

The situation would have been different if Jang Ilso had declared an advance to the north and crossed the Yangtze River. They could have gone, saying that they couldn’t recognize the treaty concluded by the independent sects. If that had happened, then the treaty would have been invalidated.

But Jang Ilso only solidified the land he had gained.

Breaking an unreasonable treaty to protect one’s home might be acceptable, but ignoring a treaty to attack someone else’s land wasn’t the right policy for the Justice Faction.

So unless Jang Ilso crossed into their land, the Justice Faction could only sit idly by and watch as the Gangnam region unified around them.

“Did he plan this from the beginning?”

“That’s ridiculous. How far ahead could he possibly see? If that Evil Faction scoundrel isn’t a member of the Zhuge family, how could he have predicted all this? I suppose the circumstances just fell into place for him.”

“Damn it, if only the Wudang hadn’t done something like that!”

Those who watched the situation helplessly were consumed by anxiety.

The world understood. The power that has been amassed will inevitably be wielded. Nowadays, that force was sweeping across the Southlands, and once the situation was resolved, the power up north would grow.

And yet, they couldn’t prevent the Four Precepts Alliance from sharpening their swords.

In the Southlands, the Evil Faction waged war under the guise of Unification. Along the Yangtze River, many people suffered due to these same scoundrels running rampant.

But those responsible for the entire situation remained silent, with their gates locked.

Only then did everyone realize it.

The fact that the peace of Kangho, which had lasted for over 100 years since the Demonic Sect’s defeat, had come to an end.

The world was entering difficult times.

And in the whirlwind of turbulent times, it wasn’t just the Nine Great Sects and the Evils Sect that captured the world’s attention.

“Damn it...”

Anger filled the faces of those gathered.

Bang!

The person who was drinking slammed the table.

“Can someone live with this anxiety? Should I just sit back while those evil scoundrels run rampant across the river?”

“... calm down. Isn’t this happening all over the land?”

“How can you speak so casually?”

The man couldn’t contain his anger.

“Would those evil scoundrels do something like that just to survive among us? Of course, they will surely come to attack us too!”

“...”

“As soon as they see signs of people gathering here, they will attack! If we just wait and look, we will end up in hell! Damn it...!”

This place was better.

When he thought about the hardships those living in the Southlands would face, he felt hopeless, even though it was someone else’s problem.

“It felt like hell just from seeing all the bastards rampaging around the Yangtze River... now even the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“Ah, about that. These days, the Yangtze River is quiet.”

“Uh? How? Did the pirates leave the Yangtze River?”

“It isn’t that. The Heavenly Friends Alliance on the Yangtze River is cutting down the Evil Sects rampaging around and has secured a commercial route to the Yangtze.”

“What? Commercial route?”

The man’s eyes widened.

It was understandable that the Yangtze was cleared. The Evil Sect running around were more like fools there. If they looked at it, they were no different from the rabble.

The problem was that no one could step forward and suppress them. However securing a path in the Yangtze River occupied by the pirates was the problem.

“Are the pirates staying still?”

“They say they don’t even dare.”

“Is that possible though? Couldn’t that be wrong information?”

“Yah! Rumors are the last things we get from the Yangtze River. The northern side of the Yangtze is gathering at the mouth of the river. They say that the Heavenly Friends Alliance has taken over the land, making it impossible for the Evil Sects to get closer.”

“... The Justice Faction shouldn’t go close to the Yangtze River, right?”

“They have nothing to do with the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, right?”

This word held a lot of meaning.

Until now, the Justice Faction was represented by the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families. If those who used the name of the Justice faction weren’t part of those 14 sects, even the medium-sized sects in the world were also bound by the name.

But now, the perception was changing.

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance is handling the pirates... no, the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“There is nothing they cannot do! Think about it. The Heavenly Friends Alliance includes the Tang family and Mount Hua. And aren’t the sects outside the wall also part of it?”

“... They won’t be helpful since they are far away, and it’s just two existing sects then?”

“No, from what I heard, the Green Forest isn’t part of the alliance but is helping them.”

“Why would the Green Forest help?”

“Tsk tsk! It’s obvious. Isn’t the Ten Thousand People clan on bad terms with the Green Forest? Since all those people created the Four Sects Alliance, it’s inevitable for the Green Forest to have bad ties with them. So, they should take the side against them!”

A unified faction against the Four Precepts Alliance?

“Was the Heavenly Friends Alliance such a strong force...”

“Didn’t you hear? Mount Hua is the place that fought against the Ten Thousand People the most in the Yangtze River. They say if it wasn’t for Mount Hua, forget the treaty; even the sects would have been wiped out.”

“... Were they that great?”

“If you include the Tang family and Green Forest with Mount Hua, then they are untouchable.”

“No, I think there are too many rumors going around?”

“Tsk, you know nothing. It’s not the Mount Hua of the past. Even though Mount Hua has taken over the river and declared an island there, the pirates haven’t gotten close to it.”

“...”

“When the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families took care of their sects, the Heavenly Friends Alliance stayed on the Yangtze, keeping the pirates in check and providing help and relief. These are the Justice factions!”

But someone whose doubts weren’t resolved tilted his head at those words.

“... No, but that is a bit... what kind of justice faction leads bandits into a fight?”

“Now, now, look at you. You moron! If they hadn’t accepted the help of bandits, could they have saved the civilians on the Yangtze River? I wonder if they could fight against the Evil Sect with righteousness!”

“...”

“These bastards and their reasons! What happened to those who always spoke of being on the right path? Didn’t they abandon the helpless people and save their own lives? And these people, didn’t care about being perceived wrongly and took help to protect people! Now speak! What kind of righteousness do you want?”

“Well, after hearing you, it sounds right.”

“If it wasn’t for the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the Yangtze River would be a hell now. Thanks to them, people can stay in Yangtze.”

“Yes...”

Everyone who listened to the man sighed.

“... I thought all those who called themselves the Justice Factions were the same.”

“Right. I never thought there would be people holding ground in the middle of the dangerous Yangtze.”

At that time, someone tilted their head again.

“But I don’t get it. Is it really the Heavenly Friends Alliance fighting the Precepts Sects Alliance? At best, they are just the small and medium sects.”

“You, don’t ever say that when you go to the Yangtze River.”

“Why?”

“You will die from being beaten.”

The person who gave the advice seriously clicked his tongue.

“For the people of Yangtze now, Mount Hua is the most important thing in the world. And... it doesn’t seem like they are grateful through just emotions alone.”

“This is news which came from the Southlands.”

“Venerable Heo Do... or maybe the Namgung family head.”

“Uh? Then maybe Qingcheng?”

“No! Didn’t I tell you it was Mount Hua!”

“Mount Hua? Who in Mount Hua?”

“It is Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon who fought against the Hegemony Lord.”

The person who heard this became angry, thinking it was all nonsense.

“What nonsense are you spouting! Isn’t Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon still a disciple? Is it possible for him to fight against the Hegemony Lord?”

“Who did I say vouched for these words?”

“That...”

The man went silent.

It was clearly from the mouths of the Evil Sects who fought there. The Four Precepts Alliance was a symbol of pride for them. They wouldn’t uselessly tarnish the reputation of their lord.

“These are the words from those who faced the enemy and survived. These are the words of those who crossed the river and settled around. All are saying the same damn thing.”

Those listening gulped and looked at each other. They were curious about what happened next.

“It is said that Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon went against Jang Ilso, and it was an even fight. It is said that the sword of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon covered the sky, and his courage shook the cliffs. But just as the winner was about to be decided... it was Venerable Heo Do who attacked Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon to prevent the result!”

“T-that... I will tear him to death!”

The man couldn’t refute it and just laughed in disbelief.

“Ahem!”

“Puah...!”

Mount Hua’s disciples all covered their mouths, trying to stifle their laughter.

Even Hyun Jong couldn’t help but cover his mouth, though his shoulders shook with suppressed mirth.

Only Chung Myung’s face twisted in annoyance at the reaction.

Hong Dae-Kwang, perhaps unsure if Hyun Jong hadn’t heard properly or simply wanted to repeat it, answered again.

With all eyes on him, he gestured dramatically, like a representative making an announcement.

At that, everyone’s bodies began to tremble.

“The world calls us exalted; exalt that name!”

The four words felt like nails on the ears of those who were shaking with laughter.

“Mount! Hua! Sword! Saint!”

“HAHAHAHAAHAH!”

’Kuak! AUHHH! I am dying! Oh my, this is hella funny!“

“Sword Saint! Huh, Sword Saint!”

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint! Kuak, right! For sure!”

Chung Myung’s face turned bright red, his fist trembling with irritation.

“This...”

He knew the moment he heard it who had given him the title and spread it around.

“This... this fucking bastard... never stops messing with people!”

Jang Ilso.

It was a name clearly given by that troublemaker!

There was no need to confirm it, as the blatant intention was obvious.

Usually, when praising the swordsmanship of someone from Mount Hua, ‘plum’ was added to the title. Hence, it should be the Plum Blossom Sword Saint, not Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

It might be because Jang Ilso didn’t know, but if Chung Myung’s technique was to be praised, it should have been Plum Blossom Sword Saint. But instead, Chung Myung’s new title had Mount Hua in it!

The intention was to make it known that Chung Myung belonged to the Mount Hua Sect.

Besides, wasn’t the word attached to it none other than ‘saint’?

In the current situation, there was a clear intention for the name with the word ‘saint’ to spread throughout the world.

To upset him further...

Tak.

At that moment, a hand was placed on Chung Myung’s shoulder.

“L-look after yourself, Sword Saint!”

“...”

“Kuak! Saint my ass! Ahhhh... ackk!”

Jo Gul was kicked by Chung Myung, flew away, and landed on the wall. But even as he writhed in pain, laughter kept coming out of his mouth.

“I never thought there would be a title that doesn’t suit you at all.”

Hyun Jong covered his face with both hands.

It was a normal occurrence, but the intention behind all this was different. Chung Myung looked at Hyun Jong’s shoulders moving.

There was even drool flowing down between his hands too.

“... is it that funny?”

“Kuak!”

“Euk!”

“Eup!”

Even Yu Yiseol was trembling slightly and turning her head to look into the distance. The solemn Tang Gunak was holding back something with his head bowed and fist clenched.

Sect leader sahyung.

He wasn’t feeling very good being treated like...

-Puah....

This man?

You shouldn’t be laughing, at least not on the topic of this, you Great Virtuous Sword!

It was definitely a good name, but those who knew Chung Myung couldn’t help but think about the fact that such a title was given to ‘Chung Myung’.

“... ahem!”

Hyun Jong cleared his throat loudly and tried to stop himself from laughing.

“That... ahem, fine... phew! Ahem...”

The soul escaped from Chung Myung at this.

This was the first time he realized that the Chung Myung of this world had such a sophisticated way of screwing over people.

‘Well.’

Chung Myung let out a chuckle.

The status of the Hegemony Lord was enormous.

Therefore, just by mentioning the names of the disciples of Mount Hua along with Hegemony Lord would bring down his name. But, Jang Ilso encouraged this situation.

The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families did something that went against their agreement. This fact was causing chaos in the world, and a Saint appeared like a God?

As their names shone, the reputations of the two others would turn more muddy.

Hong Dae-Kwang said that and scratched the back of his head.

“From his perspective, it is a bit unclear whether this is something I should do or not...”

Since the Beggars Union was part of the Nine Great Sects, the consideration would be complicated.

Things continued to grow and eventually reached a point. Who would have imagined that something like this would happen when they set off to the Yangtze River?

Swish!

At that moment, Im So-Byeong opened his fan wide, waved it around, and spoke.

“When people feel anxious, they look for something to relieve the anxiety. So, the more bad news comes from here, the more people will try to find a place to rely on. In such a situation, the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua’s Sword... ahem! The Sword Saint will resonate with them a bit.”

“What is the big ahem in the middle? Huh?”

Im So-Byeong ignored Chung Myung’s words and continued.

Swish!

Im So-Byeong hit the table with a folded fan.

The sects on the western side of the Central Plains, including Sichuan, were already contemplating whether to make an enemy of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. But now, the Heavenly Friends Alliance has proven its justification and strength. If we hold the reins a little longer, those who conform to the order of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will come rushing to knock on the door of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

What kind of bastard was he?

Please! Please spare him! Stop this!

He could not do this anymore. He could not look at the sajaes and sahyung who began to stare at him with their evil eyes; their eyes curved like moons and started to laugh.

‘Wait, is that embarrassing?’

This wasn’t something that could be solved by digging down a hole. He wanted to escape from the place, even if it meant throwing himself into the rapids.

“Mount Hua’s Righteous Swordddd?”

He especially didn’t want to stand out from that bastard. In the eyes of that little devil, who was just looking for a chance...!

It was when Hong Dae-Kwang opened his mouth again.

Plop!

Yu Yiseol stood up like a beam of light and rushed to the door. No, she tried to, at least attempted it, and Tang Soso held her hand tightly.

Yu Yiseol, whose ears were as red as her neck, fixed her eyes on the wall. Watching her shoulders rise and fall, it was clear she was truly embarrassed.

Hyun Young used the money that Hyun Jong had borrowed from Chung Myung at interest to purchase a large amount of grain. They were now being distributed to the poor people of the Yangtze River.

Not just there, but all of Mount Hua’s Disciples and members of the Tang family visited nearby villages to provide relief.

At that time, the man who came ahead was scooping up grains into the sack he held out.

As Yoon Jong rolled his eyes, Jo Gul began to scoop up more grain, even though he was mumbling dejectedly.

Since it was the time to distribute the grain, the public sentiment of the people in the Yangtze River was clearly leaning towards Mount Hua.
