Seeing Jo Gul quickly close his mouth and share the grains, Yoon Jong smiled and straightened his back.

He could see the Yangtze River flowing in the distance.

‘It seems so peaceful now.’

But this appearance was only on the surface; it was no longer a place that could be called peaceful.

“There are so many.”

Baek Cheon, who returned after rescuing the civilians, looked at the crowd gathered at the dock and mumbled.

Not long ago, there were a lot of merchants trying to cross the river, but it felt like the number had doubled in a couple of days.

The interesting thing was that normally when this many merchants gathered, there would be a fight or confusion, but no such thing happened.

Merchants whose turn was imminent waited in an orderly manner, while those who still had to wait a while pitched their tents and rested.

Merchants were known to be hot-tempered when it came to money, but here they were no different from gentle sheep.

The reason? Quite simple.

“Excuse me?”

“Uh?”

“Your foot is sticking out.”

“S-sorry.”

“No, I am not pointing it out, but be careful when a cart comes passing by; you might get hurt.”

“Uh? Yes! Thank you so much for the concern!”

This was because the people managing the merchants were all bandits who looked like they could rip open a tiger with their bare hands.

“T-that one... Mister Ho Geol?”

“No, that is a merchant. Right, what is it?”

“Well, we came before the person loading the luggage....”

“Uh? Wait a moment.”

The bandit who was checking the order scratched his face with a sad look.

“Ugh. I am sorry. What do I do...if I report this, I will be hit to death. Ugh...”

The merchant flinched as he saw the bandit’s face turn white. Even as his eyes began to turn red, the merchant spoke up.

“Ah, no. Well, I am just saying. We can always wait and load it.”

“Uh? Is that okay?”

“Oh my! Of course, it is okay! We have a lot of time!”

“Thank you! I will definitely repay the kindness! Please stand here. I will load yours right away!”

“Oh, thank you!”

This was something he would never be thankful for since it was an accidental delay in the order. But the merchant was bowing as if he were genuinely grateful.

“...”

Baek Cheon, who was staring at them, opened his mouth to say something but then turned his head away.

However, the sight he saw when he turned his head was also not pleasant.

“So how much money did you say came in yesterday?”

“I have it recorded in the ledger.”

“Hmmm.”

Hyun Young took the ledger and flipped through it familiarly.

If this was all there was, there was nothing special about it. Hyun Young was the person responsible for the Mount Hua records and handled the money.

... if it weren’t for the Green Forest King, who was turning over the ledgers, he would have to be on duty here too.

“Hmm. Have you ever studied accounting separately?”

“Haha. Isn’t that basic knowledge?”

“Quite accurate.”

“You won’t miss a single coin. The most important things for a lasting friendship are trust and honesty!”

“Right. True. For the first time in a while, I was able to entrust work to someone I could trust. But... from time to time, Eunha merchants will come to check the ledgers, so I would appreciate it if you could let it happen.”

“Of course! It is open to do so any time!”

Im So-Byeong held Hyun Young’s hands tightly.

“Please look after me!”

Baek Sang, who was watching this from the side, was startled and spoke to Baek Cheon.

“S-sahyung. I just... in that hand...”

“Sang.”

“Uh?”

“Be quiet.”

“...”

Baek Cheon also saw it clearly. Hyun Young held Im So-Byeong’s hand tightly and let go of it, and a heavy pouch appeared in his hand.

Baek Cheon, who saw Hyun Young clearing his throat and putting the pouch in his sleeve, closed his eyes tightly and turned his head.

‘I saw nothing.’

That was the way the world was. The history of the world was like this.

Well, Baek Cheon’s eyes darkened slightly as he looked at the bustling dock.

“Slowly...”

“Uh?”

“We should start getting ready.”

“Ready for what?”

Baek Sang asked back, but Baek Cheon only smiled.

Late at night.

Chung Myung left the room, holding a bottle of alcohol in his hand. The halls were brightly lit.

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung, who was looking around the bright surroundings, slowly turned away.

Because it was too bright to have a drink.

His steps headed to the water. After walking for a while through the thick field of reeds, he arrived at a riverside that was rarely visited by people.

Swish.

The river was turbulent, continuously rising and falling.

Chung Myung sat down in a suitable place and took a sip of the drink in his hand.

“Hmmm.”

After a while, Chung Myung’s face slightly contorted as he put down the bottle of alcohol a little roughly.

As he lay down, he could clearly see the moon rising in the middle of the night sky.

‘I don’t know.’

Things were going well. Mount Hua was gaining fame, benefits, and justification for its actions. Through what was happening on the river, the Heavenly Friends Alliance will solidify its place.

What would have taken at least 10 more years was accomplished in just a month.

So they were a bit relieved. Because it was something Chung Myung wanted.

But...

“It is stuffy.”

For some reason, it felt like there was this stone placed in a corner of his chest.

Was he afraid and burdened by the work Jang Ilso was doing?

No. It wasn’t that.

He couldn’t understand the cause himself. Ever since he fought with Jang Ilso, this feeling of awkwardness, which he didn’t know, wouldn’t leave him.

He couldn’t explain it...

“Sword?”

“Wopahhh!”

Chung Myung was startled and rolled over to get away from the place, then stood up.

“...”

“Oh, fuck! You scared me! What are you, a ghost? Uh? Ghost?”

“... sword?”

Before he knew it, Yu Yiseol had come closer, looking at him with an expressionless face.

No matter how much he was on guard, how could she get this close without a trace... ah, right. This woman was always like that.

“What sword?”

“Pull it out.”

“Uh?”

Srrng.

At that moment, Yu Yiseol slowly pulled out her plum sword from her waist and aimed it at Chung Myung.

Chung Myung frowned at the sudden action.

“What are you doing?”

“Spar.”

“... with me?”

“Yes.”

Yu Yiseol nodded. Chung Myung sighed at this.

“I am not in the mood right now. I will spar later. For today...”

It was right then.

Pat!

An eerie sword qi rose from the tip of Yu Yiseol’s sword. Without any hesitation, the pouring sword qi passed right next to Chung Myung’s face.

“....”

Surprised, Chung Myung wiped his cheek with a blank look. There was red blood on his fingertips.

“... sago.”

“I won’t say it again.”

“....”

Chung Myung laughed at that, and then his face twisted into a slight smile as he slowly pulled the sword from his waist.

“I don’t know what you want to do, but I am really not in a good mood right now.”

“....”

“Be prepared to get hurt more.”

“So cocky.”

The eyes of Yu Yiseol turned colder.

“Sajil, know your place.”

“Oh? Then let us check how great your thoughts are....”

Yu Yiseol kicked the ground and flew into the air before Chung Myung could even speak.

A frighteningly cold killing intent rose from her eyes.

The sword flew in quickly yet gracefully, like a beam of light. If the wind were to become a blade, it would be like this.

But what made the sword dangerous was neither its speed nor its sleekness. It was the dense killing intent on the sword.

Kwang!

The flying sword hit Chung Myung’s sword and bounced off. But the Plum Blossom Sword, which was pushed away, gently changed direction as if it knew this would happen, and soon dozens of sword qi were scattered in the air.

Chung Myung frowned.

A sword was something that was felt at the fingertips. But sword qi was something that left the fingers, which was why one could not use it too many times. This was because one could not control an arrow that left the bow.

Spreading the sword qi from the start was no different from expressing the will to win without fail, even if it meant hurting the opponent in the end.

“This...!”

Chung Myung gritted his teeth and swung his sword. He created dozens of sword rays in the air and shattered the flying red sword qi one by one.

“What are you doing?”

What made Chung Myung angry wasn’t the fierceness or her unwillingness to pay attention to the situation.

It was Yu Yiseol’s eyes glaring at him from behind the sword.

Those emotionless yet subtly reproachful eyes.

Pat!

Yu Yiseol’s body jumped up.

Chung Myung’s eyes darkened. He had said several times that it wasn’t a good habit to soar into the air. Unlike on the ground, movement was limited in the air. And there was no need to restrict such movement.

Swish!

Yu Yiseol’s sword swung in the air, making dozens of plum blossoms bloom. Not too many, just enough to cover the body.

But just because the number was small didn’t mean it wasn’t dangerous.

The flower petals fluttering in the air, one by one, were fluttering vividly, aiming for Chung Myung.

Pat!

Among the fluttering petals, a sword with a red tinge raised its head like a snake and rushed at Chung Myung as if it wanted to bite him. Like a poisonous snake hanging from a branch full of plum blossoms ambushing a passerby!

Without any hesitation, the sword flew straight for Chung Myung’s neck.

Kakaka!

Chung Myung sharpened his blade and deflected the sword to the side. At the same time, he moved back to avoid the flying flower petals.

But Yu Yiseol didn’t seem to have any intention of letting him go.

Tak!

She hit the ground and followed closely behind Chung Myung, who was trying to retreat. Then, she drew a line in the air as if she were making an orchid drawing.

“Hmm.”

This time, Chung Myung’s sword struck Yu Yiseol’s sword and slightly pushed it back. With just that small movement, the sword’s direction was easily changed.

Wheing!

Yu Yiseol’s sword passed right over Chung Myung’s head.

Thanks to that, Chung Myung’s foot hit the completely empty side.

Pak!

The shock, which shook her internal organs, came out, but the moment Yu Yiseol was hit on the side, she leaned forward to disperse the shock and lunged ahead again, lowering her sword.

Chung Myung’s body moved to the side.

Kakak!

A sword filled with tremendous qi left scars on the ground. This type of sword attack, where one targets the opponent at the risk of injury, was something that couldn’t be performed in a sword dance.

“Haah!”

Yu Yiseol let out a loud roar of qi, which was rarely heard. At the same time, her sword unleashed a storm of sword qi. The sword qi, filled with enhanced energy, poured out non-stop toward Chung Myung as if it were going to destroy everything it touched.

The red sword qi created by Yu Yiseol embroidered the black night.

Crack!

For an instant, Chung Myung’s hand holding the sword clenched. A ferocious qi, different from before, was overflowing in his eyes.

Kwaang!

The sword qi that flew in couldn’t pierce the powerful, strong sword and was destroyed. But at that moment, Yu Yiseol’s sword shook in the air again.

The sword, shaken to the extent that its tip blurred, bloomed red plum blossoms in the air. In an instant, they bloomed into dozens and began to grow.

Plum Blossom Gradual Point.

The plum blossoms created by Yu Yiseol were scattered with the wind. The flower petals flew up like an illusion, targeting Chung Myung’s entire body.

A most deadly flower.

It was the first time Chung Myung had seen the Plum Blossom Sword qi flying towards him with such deadly killing intent. The corners of his lips twitched.

Wheik.

He swung the sword once and held it, moving slowly.

Eik.

Pang!

His sword was drawn diagonally.

A red line formed among the flower petals, which flew in with utmost force. The petals that touched the point fell apart, creating a large gap.

Kwang!

At that moment, Chung Myung hit the ground with force and pierced through the gap of the beam of light.

Yu Yiseol also rushed to Chung Myung as if she knew he would do so. Then she swung the sword at his neck at a speed invisible to the eye.

But Chung Myung didn’t dodge or take the attack.

Kwaang!

Chung Myung’s sword collided head-on with Yu Yiseol’s sword. Red blood spurted from Yu Yiseol’s mouth.

Puak!

Chung Myung threw away his sword and hit her in the solar plexus with his shoulder. Yu Yiseol flew and fell like a kicked stone.

Kwang!

“...”

Looking at Yu Yiseol, who had collapsed, Chung Myung took a deep breath and spoke softly.

“So much fun.”

No matter what kind of ties sahyung-sajaes had, using killing intent to fight another was unacceptable. Of course, she probably did it knowing she wouldn’t be able to defeat Chung Myung even if she tried, but even so, the same held true.

At that time, Yu Yiseol raised herself up with her sword pushed to the ground.

Even so, her face was pale, and red blood was dripping from the corner of her lips.

“This is no...”

Yu Yiseol, who had already returned with an expressionless face, mumbled and looked straight ahead at Chung Myung. Then she asked.

“How is it?”

“How is what?”

“Me.”

“...

Chung Myung frowned, unable to understand the reason for the question. Sighing, he said,

“...The sword is light. But if you make up for that, it is good. At that age, there is no one who can be compared to you.”

“How is it?”

“...”

But what came back was the same question again.

Chung Myung slightly contorted his face and looked at Yu Yiseol. Since he didn’t answer, Yu Yiseol answered it.

“Weak.”

“....”

“Horribly.”

Chung Myung bit his lip at the calm voice.

“Sago isn’t weak.”

“I am weak.”

“No, Sago. I know what you want to say, but Sago is...”

“Weak.”

“....”

“I am weak. To the point of disgust.”

Chung Myung was speechless. Yu Yiseol’s eyes didn’t waver in the slightest. So he could tell that this wasn’t just something she was saying.

“It has been said. We are your swords.”

It was the same thing she said when fighting Jang Ilso.

“Really? We are?”

“...”

Yu Yiseol shook her head.

“You fought better. When we were around you, that was disgusting. At that time, we were in your way. Troublesome... You have to protect us even when you are fighting.”

“Sago.”

“Am I wrong?”

Yu Yiseol’s eyes were dark as she stared straight at Chung Myung.

“I didn’t do anything in the fight.”

“Ugh....”

Because it was a battle that didn’t yet fit their level of comprehension.

But Chung Myung couldn’t bear to say it out loud. This was because he knew how humiliating those words would be for a swordsman.

Furthermore...

“You all are getting stronger fast enough. To the point where it is too much.”

This was Chung Myung’s true intention without any lies or hiding.

They were already stronger than when he was the Plum Blossom Sword Saint at their age. Compared to him, who paved the way alone, they had built a great foundation.

It’s just that they couldn’t fully unleash what they’ve learned, so it isn’t obvious!

But Yu Yiseol wasn’t easily convinced.

“Lacking.”

“Excessive greed isn’t a good thing.”

At those words, Yu Yiseol stared at Chung Myung.

A short sigh escaped Chung Myung’s mouth as he looked at her. Even though they had spent quite a long time together, Chung Myung still sometimes found it difficult to guess what this expressionless thought was.

“Then when will I get strong?”

“...”

“In the distant future? Relaxing in the Mount Hua you create through bleeding and suffering?”

Chung Myung was speechless as if his mouth had gone stiff.

Srrng.

Yu Yiseol said as she retrieved her sword and pushed it into its sheath.

“It is all meaningless. All of it.”

“...”

“A treasured sword that cannot be used when needed is worse than a dull sword that can be used at the right time. It is just an ornament.”

Yu Yiseol looked at Chung Myung with an expressionless face and asked.

“Why are you so scared?”

“Scared?”

“Why do you not teach us?”

“I don’t know what you are saying. I am...”

“Not teaching us swordsmanship.”

Chung Myung frowned.

“What are you talking about? The Plum Blossom Sword Technique and the Plum Blossom Sword Movement...”

“Then.”

Yu Yiseol interrupted Chung Myung’s words. It was as if she didn’t want to hear the known things.

“Why is the sword you use different from what we use?”

“...”

Chung Myung closed his mouth.

This was because he also realized that Yu Yiseol wasn’t wrong.

“We learned to fight, how to not lose will, and how to fight back. But...”

She shook her head.

“That isn’t a sword.”

“...”

Chung Myung knew what those strange words meant.

Even though it was the same Plum Blossom Sword technique, the sword of Chung Myung and theirs was different. His sword was learned from real fights. Because of that, his killing intent felt dark and cruel.

However, although their sword techniques have been strengthened for practicality, they still align with the original technique of Mount Hua’s Plum Blossom Sword technique.

Therefore, even though Chung Myung urged them to learn it, he didn’t change the direction of the technique.

“You never once gave us what you have.”

“...It isn’t that I didn’t give it to you.”

“I want to make the plum blossoms bloom.”

“...”

“Not in a painting manner, but living plum blossoms.”

Yu Yiseol’s eyes turned to the distant sky. She looked at the night sky in silence, then gazed at Chung Myung again.

“I don’t know.”

“What?”

“Why are you so scared?”

The corners of Chung Myung’s eyes changed.

Seeing his reaction, Yu Yiseol sighed.

“We are who we are. I don’t know what you want from us.”

“....”

Chung Myung stayed silent for a moment and then opened his mouth as if he wanted to say something, but Yu Yiseol spoke first.

“Sorry.”

She bowed her head slightly, apologizing to Chung Myung.

“Each person has their own reasons. I know; I, too, have them.”

“....”

“...But if you wouldn’t help, I will do it myself. I have no intention of being a famous swordsman who shines alone after the entire war is done. It is a worthless name. What I want is a real sword, even if the blade is worn and cracked, it’s there when you need it.”

Her voice seemed to have her will etched into it.

“Next time, it won’t be like the last time. I will protect you. Because I am YOUR Sago.”

With those words, Yu Yiseol turned away. And without any regrets, she walked away from Chung Myung.

Chung Myung’s face held a mix of complex emotions as he stared at her.

He stared in the direction she had walked away, silent, and bit his lip.

“... scared?”

Him?

He laughed out loud. No, he tried to.

But no matter how hard he tried, he couldn’t laugh.

Even as he looked at the sky, he couldn’t hear his sahyung’s voice.

All he could hear was the sound of the river flowing and touching the riverbank, along with the low sound of insects in the grass.

Swish.

A pure white cloth passed over the sword.

Swish.

Baek Cheon’s eyes were deeply focused.

It was something he did habitually once a day, yet his form was as serious as the first time he cleaned the sword.

Swish.

The sword’s reflection illuminated his face. Baek Cheon looked at his own face in the reflection and began to clean the sword again in silence.

How long was it?

“Phew.”

After confirming it was clean, he sheathed the sword.

Tak.

He carefully set down the sword he had finished cleaning and looked toward the door.

“If you want to come in, come in. Why are you doing that?”

“... When did you realize?”

“It would be weirder if you made those weird looks and I didn’t notice.”

“Then tell me to come in right away!”

Baek Cheon chuckled when he saw Chung Myung opening the door and entering with a grunt.

“Since when did you need permission to enter? Even if it’s locked, you’d break through the door.”

Chung Myung grinned and sauntered into the room, casually sitting down in the middle. A faint smile appeared on Baek Cheon’s lips.

“...”

“...”

A moment of silence passed. Chung Myung narrowed his eyes and looked at Baek Cheon.

“You won’t ask?”

“What?”

“Why did I come in?”

“I guess you came here for a reason.”

“No...”

“I’m waiting for it. What you’ll say.”

Chung Myung pouted and spoke bluntly as if he didn’t like it at all.

“You speak like you knew I was coming.”

“Right.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung raised his head. Baek Cheon responded calmly to the reaction.

“I didn’t expect you to come to my room, but I thought you were going to say something soon. Something quite important.”

“... Why?”

“Why?”

Baek Cheon’s reaction was rather absurd.

“Because you weren’t the same as usual.”

“What does that mean?”

“You really don’t know?”

“Uh? I think I’m the same as usual.”

Baek Cheon sighed and placed one hand on his forehead.

Chung Myung flinched and resisted Baek Cheon’s extremely pitiful gaze.

“W-what! What!”

“Chung Myung.”

“What?”

“What did you do after you came back from the fight with Jang Ilso?”

“What did I do? Of course....”

Chung Myung, unable to finish his words, slowly closed his mouth.

‘Right. What did I do?’

He tilted his head slightly and looked back on the past few days.

He was constantly doing something, but when he thought about it, he didn’t do anything in particular. As Baek Cheon asked this question, he was at a loss for an answer.

Baek Cheon, who was looking at Chung Myung who was thinking, smiled and said.

“The Tang family was the main force in renovating Plum Blossom Island. And the Green Forest was responsible for building the wharf and setting up the merchant ship systems. Mount Hua’s disciples stabilized the surrounding area and provided relief.”

“... Right?”

“If it were your normal sense, you would have been talking back and forth, giving the instructions while building the palace, and then you would have run to the dock to kick the bandits. And you would have lost your mind more than anyone else, threatening to kill all the Evil Faction bastards.”

“....”

“A guy who was so strong was stuck on the stuffy island and was just playing around with bottles. Would you have thought it was normal?”

A laugh which came out of Chung Myung;s mouth.

“Maybe sasuk is the one thinking like that?”

“Just me? Everyone must be feeling like they are holding a bomb in their hands. If we don’t know that your condition is not normal, then you are not a disciple of Mount Hua.”

“...”

Chung Myung’s face changed numerous times in a short period. Chung Myung, who was moving his lips like he wanted to say something, finally sighed deeply.

“... then why didn’t you say anything?”

Baek Cheon shrugged his shoulders.

“Because I didn’t have to.”

“...”

“Anyway, you are not the type of person who listens to others, and you aren’t the kind to complain and let things fester alone. I thought that when the right moment came, you would end the worries and say something.”

Baek Cheon’s eyes turned to Chung Myung.

“Right, like now.”

“...”

“So, tell me. What is the problem?”

The look in his eyes was so serious that Chung Myung rolled his eyes awkwardly and eventually made eye contact. A sigh escaped from his mouth.

“No...”

“Uh.”

“No, that...”

“Go on.”

“Wait, that... before...”

“... Wh-what is it, you bastard? What!”

“Ah, why are you screaming now!”

Chung Myung screamed and scratched his head.

“It isn’t like there is any trouble...”

“Uh?”

“If I have to put it bluntly, I am not sure if there is a problem. All that while I was in the room, I only came to know of it from sasuk.”

“...”

“Ah, right, so, don’t look at me like, ‘Ah, this moron is back at it again.’”

“... Have you learned some mind-reading techniques during that time?”

“Ugh.”

Chung Myung opened his mouth with a confused look that was difficult to decipher.

“After hearing what sasuk said, I know that I was somehow screwed up in the process after the war, but I also have no idea why I did...”

“You don’t know?”

“Yes.”

“I get it.”

“Uh? Really?”

Chung Myung looked at Baek Cheon in surprise. How could Baek Cheon know something that he doesn’t know?

“Well, it is obvious. You are always so thoughtless, simple, obvious, and stupid, more than you can...”

“Enough.”

This bastard.

Baek Cheon laughed as Chung Myung was turning pale.

“You are just that kind of person. If there is something that needs to be done right away, you are someone who cannot look at anything else.”

“...”

“Maintaining Plum Blossom Island, breaking through trade routes, making money, increasing influence, kicking the ass of the Nine Great Sects, and taking over the space they held...”

Baek Cheon stopped reciting one thing after another and looked at Chung Myung.

“Right, but that is important stuff. But...”

He smiled and said,

“Maybe that isn’t the most important thing. At least for Chung Myung.”

“...”

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Do what you want to do.”

Chung Myung was quiet for a moment as if he had lost his voice. Baek Cheon continued,

“Do what you think you should do. Don’t do it because you have to.”

“No, sasuk...”

“You don’t think we can deal with both the Four Precepts Alliance and the Ten Thousand People Clan, right?”

“... what are you talking about?”

Chung Myung looked at Baek Cheon a little awkwardly and said,

“The influence grows, and the bond in the Heavenly Friends Alliance grows stronger....”

“I know you.”

But Baek Cheon cut off his words.

“You don’t know how to use other people to solve problems. If there is a problem, you attack it straight ahead and destroy it to relieve your anger.”

“...”

“To you, all of this is just incidental. Isn’t it right? What is important to you is...”

Baek Cheon looked straight at Chung Myung and said,

“Can Mount Hua defeat the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“...”

“Can Mount Hua go against them?”

Chung Myung closed his mouth and looked at Baek Cheon.

“Is that all?”

“...”

Baek Cheon grinned at Chung Myung’s blank look.

“Sometimes the people watching from the side can understand the situation more clearly than you. It seems to me like this is the case.”

“...”

“The point has been made. What needs to be done is clear. But this cannot be done. Then... if it were me, I would have acted like you.”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with a serious expression and nodded.

“So I have just one thing to say.”

“... what is it?”

“You are a greedy, stupid, and horrible person with the worst nature.”

“No, this is...”

“But what’s worse than that is that you cannot trust others.”

“...”

“You have done enough already. Even if you don’t do anything, what you have created will go with the flow. So, give up the desire to have everything under your control. When there are a lot of enemies, what is the easiest way to solve the problem?”

Chung Myung licked his lips.

This was what he had said to Baek Cheon countless times.

“The head...”

After a while, a groan-like voice came out of his mouth.

“... I will break it.”

Baek Cheon smiled.

“Right, I know that.”

This doesn’t just mean handling the strongest. It meant that the most important things should be done first.

Both Chung Myung and Baek Cheon knew its meaning.

“It isn’t like Chung Myung, the one that I know, to be swayed around. You like simple things the best. Leave everything else to others to figure out, and you do the best you can think of.”

“....”

“Taking responsibility doesn’t mean taking on everything. It is rather impossible.”

Chung Myung stared at Baek Cheon without a word. Then Baek Cheon smiled slightly and shrugged his shoulders.

“Why? Finally, feel like acting like a sasuk?”

“No, it isn’t that...”

“Then what?”

Chung Myung, who had this confused look, laughed.

“I thought there were times when Dong-Ryong was like a human. It seems like just yesterday that you were so pathetic and couldn’t even pay for your good-looking face, always relying on others.”

“... this bastard!”

Chung Myung giggled.

“I know one thing.”

“What?”

“If a young bird follows a stork, it will have its balls torn.”

“You are talking about me?”

“No. I am talking about me.”

Chung Myung laughed at himself.

He wanted to be like his sahyung.

In his opinion, the ideal sect leader was his sahyung. But deep down, he already knew.

He could never become a person like him, and he wasn’t worthy of becoming a sect leader. Neither of these two goals could be achieved by Chung Myung.

‘I knew it, and yet I forgot.’

He was definitely doing something unbecoming.

He hadn’t heard anything particularly great, but he felt something clearly before his eyes. It was as if the hazy fog covering the world had lifted at once.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh.”

“Thank you.”

At his calm words, Baek Cheon’s eyes grew wide.

“... did something upset your stomach?”

“....”

“Maybe you got hit by Jang Ilso so much that your head is all messed up... should I call for Soso?”

“....”

Chung Myung felt sad. It wasn’t because of what Baek Cheon said, but because of Baek Cheon’s sincere eyes, which showed he was seriously concerned.

Normally, Chung Myung would have stabbed him in the eyes, but he decided to endure it for today.

“Anyway....”

Seeing Yu Yiseol and Baek Cheon, it felt like they were such morons.

Chung Myung’s eyes turned clearer and clearer.

“So, do what I have to do?”

“Yes.”

“The most important thing?”

“Yes.”

“... you mean it?”

“...”

There was a moment of silence, and anxiety began to appear on Baek Cheon’s face.

This was because an evil smile began to form on Chung Myung’s lips. It was so clear that he felt reassured, but on the other hand, it felt so uneasy too.

“Sasuk is right. I was being such a moron.”

“....”

“It makes me angry to think about it. Such obvious things! Since when did I care about other things!”

“Ah, no, wait. Chung Myung, brat!”

Baek Cheon’s face began to contort now.

Something... something weird was happening, not what he expected. No, this wasn’t just a feeling; he was sure of it.

“Thanks to you, I feel refreshed! Woah, there are times when even Dong-Ryong can be of use! Then, let us talk later!”

Chung Myung stood up with a relieved face and turned around.

“No, this bastard! What are you talking about...?”

Baek Cheon extended his hand, but Chung Myung had already kicked down the door.

The hand, which was awkwardly wandering in the air, grasped at nothing a couple of times.

“....”

Did he make a mistake?

At that moment, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul poked their heads through the wide-open door. He wasn’t sure when they arrived, but it seemed they had been listening to the conversation from outside.

The two looked back and forth between Chung Myung, who was running away, and Baek Cheon, who appeared dumbfounded and said:

“Sasuk.”

“... Uh?”

“What have you done?”

“....”

Guys...

He was also worried because he didn’t know what he did.

“Uh?”

“What?”

“Eh?”

“UH?”

“What did you say?”

The reactions varied, but the emotions behind them were identical.

The disciples of Mount Hua gathered around Hyun Jong repeatedly blinked as if struggling to comprehend what they had just heard.

Those who had been staring at Hyun Jong with blank, almost dumbfounded expressions turned to one another. Judging by their uniformly blank faces, it seemed they hadn’t misheard Hyun Jong.

“S... sect leader?”

Baek Sang spoke cautiously, tilting his head.

“Did... I hear... it wrong or...?”

He spoke with difficulty, his eyes scanning the surroundings. He wanted to confirm whether he had understood the situation correctly.

He hoped he had misheard, but the sahyungs who met his eyes all nodded slightly. This meant his thoughts were correct.

“Did... you just... say... closed their gates...?”

Baek Sang laughed at the absurdity of the situation.

Closed their gates?

What nonsense was this...

“... yes.”

“Ehk?”

“Uh?”

“What?”

“Us?”

Upon confirmation, the disciples of Mount Hua began to tremble. Naturally, the most affected was Jo Gul.

“No, sect leader! What crime did we commit to deserve Closed Gates? Did we do something wrong without knowing... euhhhh!”

“Wrong? This bastard! You moron!”

Yoon Jong grabbed Jo Gul by the collar with lightning speed and repeatedly knocked his chin, rendering him unconscious.

“Ahem.”

Holding Jo Gul with one hand, who hung limp, Yoon Jong covered his mouth with the other and coughed politely. The left and right sides were in perfect harmony, but no one cared to ask about it.

“... I am flustered, sect leader. Of course, you meant what you said, but why are we suddenly opting for this?”

Everyone nodded in agreement.

As the eyes demanding explanation poured in, Hyun Jong slowly turned his head. After a movement that showed no strength, his gaze fell on Chung Myung.

In the midst of all that, Chung Myung smiled brightly.

At that moment, Jo Gul, who had been half-shocked, let out a terrible scream, shook off Yoon Jong, ran to Baek Cheon, and grabbed him by the collar.

“What did you do! Yah, this moron! What happened?”

Even though the outrageous situation of a sajil grabbing a sasuk by the collar occurred, Baek Cheon didn’t get angry. No, it would be more accurate to say that he couldn’t respond. He just awkwardly looked away from Jo Gul’s fiercely burning gaze.

“No... that bastard did it; why are you doing this to me?”

“If there is a fire, is it the fire’s fault? Or the one who set it? What is wrong with this bastard! It was sasuk who turned him like this!”

It might sound like nonsense, but all the disciples of Mount Hua agreed with Jo Gul, fiercely.

Even Baek Cheon felt sorry for his own actions.

“How could I have known that this...”

How could an ordinary person judge the thoughts of a madman?

A Closed Gate wasn’t a common thing; it was like a bolt out of the blue.

“... sect leader. No, just what did you hear to suddenly go for...”

Yoon Jong blankly looked back and forth between Chung Myung and Hyun Jong, clearing his throat as he spoke.

“Of course... that... whatever the reason, if the sect leader orders it, we should follow it as disciples, but I want to at least know the reason.”

“That is...”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“That...”

Hyun Jong’s eyes turned increasingly wet.

Seeing that, Yoon Jong couldn’t bring himself to ask.

Yoon Jong turned his head fiercely.

“You explain, you moron! Why did you suddenly decide to go for a Closed Gate? We did nothing wrong!”

“Those words are weird.”

Chung Myung poked his ears with his finger and blew on it.

“A Closed Gate isn’t something I decide. I am just a powerless third-class disciple.”

“Powerless third-class disciple? Powerless?”

This detestable moron.

“And what does Closed Gates have to do with bringing in trouble? Those Nine Great Sects bastards just lock their gates to hide when they do something wrong and don’t want to be cursed to their faces. Normally, it is something we do when we want to.”

“So why are we doing it now?”

Baek Sang quickly asked instead of Yoon Jong.

“Right, brat! What time are we in now! The entire world is praising Mount Hua! And the businesses have wings on them now! It is a time when Mount Hua is being talked about more than the Nine Great Sects! When the water is coming, we need to row to move! You don’t want to capsize the ship now, right? You... fucking insane bastard!”

Baek Sang pounded his chest as if he were angry.

“Don’t just stand there and say something, finance hall leader!”

And then he urged Hyun Young for support, but Hyun Young only said,

“Chung Myung must have a reason.”

“Ugh!”

This man doesn’t care about the time or place!

At that moment, Jo Gul let go of Baek Cheon’s collar and rolled his eyes, this time rushing for Chung Myung.

“Yah, you moron...! Kuak!”

And he bounced back at the same speed, rolling back. The sad thing was that no one stopped Jo Gul.

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung slowly folded his stretched-out legs, clicked his tongue, and looked at everyone.

“It seems like everyone is misunderstanding.”

“Uh?”

“This isn’t my decision.”

“Uh? Then who?”

Chung Myung nodded with a solemn look, closing his eyes.

“I am just doing what Baek Cheon sasuk orders me to do. Isn’t it the duty of a sajil to simply follow what a sasuk says?”

Everyone’s eyes turned to Baek Cheon.

Baek Cheon simply smiled gently upon receiving those eyes.

“... just hit me to death. Do not torment me like this. Give me a clean kill.”

“When did I ever torment you? You are being too harsh.”

Baek Cheon covered his face with both hands and shook his shoulders weakly.

“Sect leader! Are you really going to listen to this nonsense?”

“Even if it is Chung Myung, isn’t this too much?”

“I don’t understand. Closed Gates? Why are we doing it? The sins were committed by Shaolin and Wudang, and if we end up doing the same, what will the world think of us?”

As the disciples all stood up and questioned, Hyun Jong looked at everyone with a serious expression.

But at that moment, a soft voice came.

“Closed Gate.”

The disciples all turned to one place. Yu Yiseol, who was seated in the center receiving their gaze, nodded.

“Head back to Mount Hua and close the gates.”

“....”

“I like it.”

The disciples exchanged glances.

‘Why is samae doing this now?’

‘Who knows what is on her mind! She is insane too!’

‘I am going insane to the point of dying!’

If Chung Myung was a typhoon that could be seen from afar, Yu Yiseol was an earthquake that suddenly hit you. You could prepare for a typhoon, but you never know about an earthquake.

“Well, since it is as such, everyone should get ready. We head back to Mount Hua.”

“No, you bastard!”

“What the hell, brat!”

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who was silent, opened his mouth.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

His face was frozen.

“Explain it so that they can understand. This isn’t something that can be passed off like this.”

“Hmmm.”

Chung Myung nodded as if he understood.

“The reason is simple.”

His finger pointed at Yu Yiseol.

Yu Yiseol nodded.

And then he pointed at Baek Cheon, Jo Gul, and Yoon Jong. Then he turned to all the disciples of Mount Hua who had gathered.

Chung Myung pointed at each disciple, including the last one of them, and said,

“Are weak.”

“....”

There was silence for a moment, and everyone’s face hardened.

The atmosphere changed in an instant as if a sore spot had been hit.

Baek Sang, who had been silent, spoke first.

“... I understand.”

He stared at Chung Myung and opened his mouth as if he didn’t comprehend.

“We are certainly lacking. We weren’t that great in the battle, either. All we could do was hold back the Ten Thousand People Clan and endure.”

“Right.”

“But Chung Myung, there is a time for everything. It is only natural that a warrior devotes himself to training, but isn’t it time to look at something bigger?”

Baek Sang sighed.

“I understand the impatience, but in one year... no, half a year, the position of Mount Hua will be solidified. The Four Precepts Alliance signed a three-year treaty, and there is no reason we cannot wait for half a year, right?”

“Half a year?”

“Yes, half a year!”

Baek Sang looked at Chung Myung with hope.

As a member of the Finance Hall, he had learned about Mount Hua’s finances, became familiar with how the world worked, and observed the flow of merchants’ money with Mount Hua.

Which was why he knew. More clearly than anyone else.

This was how big of a chance Mount Hua had now.

If it were another sect, all of the disciples would rush in and work to seize the opportunity. But instead of kicking it away, he was about to throw the entire thing into a pile of dung and trample it.

He couldn’t understand the situation at all.

“What good will it do if we wait half a year?”

“Are you asking me because you don’t realize? Money first? And fame! And then there are countless sects that want to unite with Mount Hua and Chung Myung. And then the fame and status!”

“...”

“This is a chance to double what Mount Hua has in one go! Triple it! Maybe even five times bigger! Isn’t this what we have been hoping for? And now that we have made such sacrifices and are finally bearing the fruits, you want to enter Closed Gates!”

Chung Myung looked at Baek Sang without a word. Baek Sang, who was feeling anxious for no reason, suddenly raised his voice.

“And this isn’t just about the present! If we wait a little longer for our fame to mature and then start accepting the Myung disciples, talented kids from all directions will come in! That is what we need to secure the future of Mount Hua!”

“...”

“But why are you rejecting such opportunities? Why!”

Everyone agreed with Baek Sang’s words.

Anyone could see that Mount Hua was now riding an unprecedented wave. If they rode this wave well, they would be on par with the Nine Great Sects.

“Tell me, are you saying that a sect doesn’t need anything other than martial arts? That isn’t something you can say, right?”

Everyone looked at Chung Myung’s mouth. Their eyes were filled with firm determination that they wouldn’t back down this time.

Chung Myung stared at everyone with slightly dark eyes and slowly said,

“We have a lot of money. We have a good name. We have a good reputation. As you all know, I like those things, right?”

“Then why are you doing this?”

“Why are you asking me something you already know? I told you. Because the sahyungs are weak and the sasuks are weak.”

“This...”

Baek Sang, who was angry at the unsatisfactory answer, spoke with a grim look.

“Give me a proper answer!”

“This is the answer. Sasuk is weak.”

“Yah, brat!”

“Do you not understand?”

Baek Sang closed his mouth at the cold words from Chung Myung. The weight in the voice was unmistakable.

Chung Myung spoke softly but clearly.

“Until now, it was enduring with luck.”

His cold eyes scanned everyone.

“Next time...”

Those who met his eyes trembled.

“Someone here will definitely die.”

At that moment, everyone’s mouths went shut.

Death.

Actually, it wasn’t an unfamiliar word. No, maybe they were very familiar with it.

But the words that came out of Chung Myung’s mouth weighed on everyone’s hearts like a thousand-pound rock.

“... no....”

Baek Sang tried to say something but then closed his mouth again.

He didn’t know what to say.

Chung Myung chuckled.

“We were lucky, right?”

“....”

“If Venerable Heo Do hadn’t stepped in and stopped the war...”

“....”

“No, if only the timing of stopping the war had been a little later...”

Everyone turned their heads away, unable to meet Chung Myung’s gaze.

“If the Ten Thousand People hadn’t just stood there and stalled for time but had really rushed in with the determination to go to war...”

“...”

“Would everyone really have been able to sit here like this and speak of profit and fame?”

Baek Sang bit his lip.

“But that is...”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders, cutting off Baek Sang’s words.

“As a warrior, you should be prepared to die at any time. If someone who lives by swinging a sword thinks that he won’t die from being hit by the sword, that is arrogance and disgusting.”

“....”

“But that doesn’t mean it is alright to die.”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“The world has changed. The battlefield we will stand on from now won’t be as easy as it was before. The days of fighting like we trained or experienced are over. And they won’t come again. From now on, we really have to kill or be killed.”

Because the Evil Sects changed the world.

And everyone sitting here knew that fact.

“But so...”

But Baek Sang still looked unconvinced.

“So aren’t they uniting the sects and growing their power? If it’s the power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance....”

“Sasuk.”

Chung Myung stared at Baek Sang and asked.

“Do you really think so?”

“....”

Baek Sang bit down on his lip and sighed.

Chung Myung said.

“I like money too. I like fame and honor, you know?”

“... then?”

“But if it costs someone’s life in return, I don’t need it.”

Chung Myung’s voice was calmer and firmer than ever.

“Do you know what really sends a person to hell?”

“... seeing the death of their own sahyung-sajae with their own eyes?”

“No, it isn’t that.”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“The real hell is surviving it. And I, who survived, belatedly realize that I could have prevented the sahyungs-sajaes from dying.”

“...”

“Regret is what truly drags people to hell.”

It was a calm voice.

But those who heard it felt their hearts sink with an inexplicable sadness.

“Someone will die. This is unavoidable. We act as if nothing happened, but everyone knows it. Someday, one of us here will die. And it won’t be long before that happens.”

“....”

“At that time...”

Chung Myung cast his gaze up.

“The splendid fame on our shoulders.”

“....”

“The great wealth in our hands.”

“...”

“The great honor of saving the world.”

Chung Myung, who had been looking up for a moment as everyone stayed silent, slowly opened his mouth again.

“Do you think all of that is worth even a penny?”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung in silence.

Baek Cheon knew.

That wasn’t what Chung Myung said to his sahyung sajaes. That was what he was saying to himself.

“I don’t want to regret it.”

“...”

“If I cannot avoid seeing someone die, I should at least make sure it isn’t a death caused by my own complacency. It shouldn’t be a death caused by my wrong choices; it shouldn’t be a death that could have been avoided.”

Yu Yiseol nodded slowly.

Calmly, but firmly.

“Mount Hua needs to get stronger. It cannot be done now. It isn’t just because of the Ten Thousand People Clan. It isn’t because of the Four Precepts Alliance, and it’s not because of Jang Ilso.”

Because he was scared.

Because he was so scared of losing.

If there had been even one casualty in the battle, Mount Hua would never have been the same. And everyone knew it. This wasn’t the result of skill but sheer luck.

‘It should have been this way from the start.’

Chung Myung bit his lip.

He told Jang Ilso that he would regret giving Mount Hua time. But that was just an angry outburst.

Chung Myung was that scared.

Defeating the Ten Thousand People Clan?

What meaning did a victory have if it was won by sacrificing countless disciples?

He had experienced such victories countless times. Even if you searched the entire world, there wasn’t a single person who had experienced more victories, more glorious victories than Chung Myung.

So what was left for Chung Myung?

Nothing.

Seriously, nothing.

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who had been staring at Chung Myung, opened his mouth.

“So...”

The disciples all turned their gazes to him. At this moment, no matter what anyone said, the person who could represent the disciples of Mount Hua was Baek Cheon.

“If we close the gates as you said and practice to death, no one will die?”

“How can that happen? But...”

Chung Myung closed his eyes once and then opened them. And then he said,

“At least, the living ones won’t be ashamed in front of the dead.”

Baek Cheon chuckled.

“It isn’t that great, but.”

“....”

“I agree.”

“Sasuk!”

“Sahyung!”

The sahyung-sajes shouted in surprise, but Baek Cheon didn’t even look at them. He just looked at Chung Myung and laughed in disbelief.

“I told that greedy bastard to stop being greedy, but he’s doing something worse. I guess it is just typical of him.”

Meanwhile, Baek Sang’s eyes were filled with confusion. Having spent a long time with Baek Cheon, he knew better than anyone else that once the sahyung made up his mind, he would never change it.

He turned his gaze to Yoon Jong as if asking for help.

“Say something.”

“You mean me?”

“Yes.”

Yoon Jong took a moment to collect his thoughts and then said calmly.

“I don’t think there is anything wrong with that either.”

“... what?”

“I don’t know much about business, honor, or fame. I just learned the sword. Mount Hua is a place where you can achieve the Tao Way through a sword. Isn’t that enough?”

He shrugged slightly and continued.

“I know that what sasuk said makes sense. But I think that if I can just do what I have to do, no matter which side it is, that is enough.”

Before Baek Sang could say anything, Jo Gul clicked his tongue at Yoon Jong.

“This fucking man speaking like the great Byuk Chang-ho...”

“Why? Do you think differently?”

“If I say it is different, you will hit me, right? What can I say>”

“I won’t hit you.”

“How can I believe that!”

“I said I won’t!”

“Right, right. It is the words of heaven by a sahyung, so I need to follow... ack! You said you won’t hit me!”

Baek Sang sighed, thinking that he had done it.

Finally, he looked at Yu Yiseol, but since she had already agreed to the Closed Gates, there was no point in asking her. Furthermore, seeing the elders sitting there, he couldn’t even bring himself to open his mouth.

’Just give up, sasuk.“

Kwak Ho laughed bitterly.

“Honestly, what would you say after hearing that? You already know it, sasuk!”

“... right, I know...”

Baek Sang scratched his head.

“No matter how much I think about it, it is such a waste. Too much! Chung Myung. Is it really impossible to do both?”

“I am not that clever.”

“....”

“We are closing the gate to push through without anyone leaving the place. If it was possible, why would we have chosen that option in the first place?”

A deep sigh escaped Baek Sang’s mouth.

When he turned around, everyone’s eyes had changed.

‘These guys, seriously.’

They had been resisting so much, but they had already agreed without even hearing a word. No, it wasn’t an agreement; they were the ones who had to take the step ahead and shout that they wanted the Closed Gates soon enough and start training.

It was frustrating inside, but... still, Baek Sang didn’t say anything.

He couldn’t help but understand. Baek Sang was the same.

Maybe he could pressure them. He could make them change their minds by getting angry and yelling.

But that wouldn’t happen.

What if, as a result, Baek Sang came face to face with Baek Cheon’s fallen body?

Baek Sang would never forgive himself.

Knowing that he couldn’t bring himself to oppose.

‘It seems... sorted then.’

Hyun Jong, who had been quietly listening to their talks, opened his mouth.

“When Chung Myung came to see me last night and brought this up, I also had many concerns. What is right and not?”

“... Sect Leader.”

“There are countless chances in Mount Hua. And Mount Hua has a heavy responsibility on its shoulders.”

Hyun Jong, of all, would have even more so.

Others only have to think about Mount Hua losing. But Hyun Jong had to shoulder the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Even if it was limited to Mount Hua, he had more to consider than the disciples.

“I couldn’t come up with an answer in my head. So for the first time, I asked my heart. What path do I take?”

Hyun Jong smiled brightly.

“My heart said, a thousand gold coins, a world-renowned reputation, and the honor to make Mount Hua’s name shine....”

His eyes shone brighter than ever.

“It isn’t worth exchanging for even a single disciple.”

“... Sect Leader.”

Hyun Jong smiled.

Perhaps it was something that a sect leader, who led a sect, shouldn’t say. But at the same time, it was the right mindset that a sect leader should have.

Hyun Jong’s mind was clearly saying it now.

The chance that Mount Hua had now was something he had longed for in the past.

But now Hyun Jong knew.

“I don’t care if Mount Hua doesn’t become the best sect in the world.”

“...”

“Even without that name, Mount Hua is Mount Hua. It isn’t Mount Hua because of strength and greatness. But...”

A warm look filled the eyes of Hyun Jong.

“Mount Hua without you isn’t Mount Hua.”

Everyone clenched their fists.

There were many words in the world, but none could touch their hearts more than this.

“As the sect leader of Mount Hua, I will protect Mount Hua.”

“...”

“Is there anyone who opposes it?”

It was quiet.

Everyone looked at Hyun Jong in silence. After a moment, someone answered first.

“None.”

Baek Sang.

When he answered, everyone agreed.

“None.”

“There is none, sect leader!”

“I will follow the words of the sect leader!”

Hyun Jong slowly nodded.

“All disciples of Mount Hua, listen up.”

“Yes, sect leader!”

“In the name of the sect leader of Mount Hua, I at this moment order you to seal the gates from this moment on. With no deadline set.”

“Yes!”

Hyun Jong looked at everyone with gentle eyes.

It might be an unreasonable request. However, no one stood up to object, and no one gave him any discontented looks.

No matter what was inside, they were trying to follow his words.

How could they not protect such people?

“Let’s go back to Mount Hua.”

“Yes! Sect leader!”

That is how Mount Hua’s trip to the island ended.

Just then, as everyone was smiling at Hyun Jong, a dreary voice came.

“So, has it been decided?”

Crack. Crack.

Chung Myung twisted his neck from the side. The sound of bones in his neck somehow felt eerie.

“Ah, I didn’t mention one thing.”

“... Uh?”

“Someone will die soon, and it won’t necessarily be due to the war.”

“....”

“I am going to give it my all this time. If I die, it is better to die by my own hands than someone else’s. Right?”

“....”

“That’s just how it is. Just know it.”

The hope drained from Mount Hua’s disciples’ eyes as they all looked at Hyun Jong with frightened, deer-like eyes. Hyun Jong averted their gaze with a friendly smile.

“Let’s hurry up and go back without wasting time. We are going to get busy now.”

“....”

The gates to hell were beautifully decorated.

It was only then that the disciples of Mount Hua realized they had walked into their own hell.

“That...”

It was good until everyone unanimously decided to make the decision to close the gates.

Of course, seeing Chung Myung grind his teeth made them a little creeped out, but everyone was a bit prepared for this.

But what the disciples of Mount Hua, who had made the decision, didn’t expect at all was... that they had to explain the decision to this person in front of them.

“That...”

Baek Cheon scratched the back of his head with an awkward look, which was unusual for him.

“And so it happened, monk.”

It was still vivid in his eyes.

That moment when Hae Yeon declared to his elder that he wouldn’t return to Shaolin.

“... Closed Gate?”

“....”

“Closed Gate?”

“....”

Tears quickly welled up in Hae Yeon’s deer-like eyes.

“Then... me?”

“Uh....”

Baek Cheon answered awkwardly and hesitantly.

“That... what should I do about this?”

“....”

“Ha... hahaha...”

Cold sweat began to run down his back as he laughed awkwardly.

‘Say something now!’

‘He is crying!’

‘No. What do you want me to do?’

Who would have thought about Hae Yeon’s future in what happened?

At that moment, Hae Yeon opened his mouth.

“That... I am asking just in case.”

“... Yes.”

“If the Gates are closed... then the outsiders?”

“That...”

Baek Cheon smiled and answered kindly.

“If you close the gate, outsiders cannot come in. Isn’t that what Closed Gate means? You are asking something odd. This is something you know.”

“Yah, huh!”

“No, what can I do if you answer like that?”

Baek Cheon’s eyes widened slightly as if asking why protests poured in from behind.

“That is true, so I cannot lie.”

“Wah....”

“Did you leave your humanity on the other side of the Yangtze River?”

Hae Yeon closed his eyes tightly and turned his head away. Clear tears flowed down from his eyes.

‘These Mount Hua bastards who aren’t even human!’

The Evil Faction would be better. The Evil Faction was better.

No, it wasn’t their fault; it was Hae Yeon’s fault.

He knew from the start that Mount Hua was like this, so what was he expecting when he declared that he would stay with Mount Hua?

Jo Gul, who had been watching Hae Yeon’s expression, let out an awkward laugh.

“Hahahah! M-monk! This isn’t something to be so sad about.”

“... Uh?”

“Don’t do that. Instead, take this chance to become a full monk. If it were someone else, I wouldn’t know, but if you end up fully trained, the sect leader would welcome you with open arms.”

“....”

“Think carefully. Rather than living your whole life bald, wouldn’t you rather be a monk, grow hair, eat meat, and...?”

Yoon Jong suddenly grabbed Jo Gul by the hair and pulled him back, hitting him in the mouth.

“Yah, this non-stop mouth! Shut up!”

“Ah! Ack! Ugh! Why! Why are you hitting me? Why! Ack!”

Baek Cheon shook his head as he watched Yoon Jong quickly subdue Jo Gul.

“... I will have a good talk with the sect leader. Don’t worry too much, monk.”

“....”

When Hae Yeon didn’t answer, Baek Cheon laughed awkwardly.

“Haha. As the monks say, isn’t life like this?”

Hearing that, Hae Yeon smiled.

“... Please, sir.”

“Uh? What did you say?”

“... Go.”

“Uh?”

“Go out, please.”

“....”

“Did you say Closed Gate?”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes were so wide that they almost popped out.

“Closed Gate?”

“Um.”

“Closed Gate?”

“Right.”

“You said Closed Gate?”

“...”

“This might be a rude question, but...”

“Uh?”

“Did you do something wrong?”

Oh...

Amazing. One could not tell if he was rude or not. As expected, he was the leader of bandits.

At that moment, Im So-Byeong suddenly shouted.

“Are you in your right mind?”

“You are yelling at me!”

Chung Myung kicked Im So-Byeong away. However, Im So-Byeong, who would have normally joked about dying, got up as soon as he fell and returned to the original position like water.

“Oh my!”

Chung Myung flinched at the almost-flying momentum. Im So-Byeong shouted with his eyes wide open.

“No, are you saying this because you don’t know what the situation is right now? Money! Money! I mean money! Money!”

Im So-Byeong, who had been sick until now, continued to curse as if his head would burst.

“No! It isn’t just about money! It isn’t like someone who is a Taoist doesn’t know how much is at stake here! Closed Gates! What kind of nonsense is this?”

Im So-Byeong’s attitude, who had been yelling, changed again.

He grabbed Chung Myung’s hand and begged with earnest eyes.

“Are you kidding? Are you teasing me? Tell me it is a joke. Uh?”

“It is true.”

Thud.

Im So-Byeong, who had collapsed on the spot, mumbled with a face devoid of soul, legs hanging loosely.

“... is that something a bandit says to a Taoist?”

At that moment, Tang Gunak, who had been quietly listening from the side, looked at Chung Myung with a face unable to understand.

“I don’t know what you are thinking either. You are suddenly entering Closed Gates. The things you were trying to do are finally bearing fruit.”

“Well, that is true...”

When Chung Myung showed a slightly difficult look, Tang Gunak stared at him and nodded.

“So you are saying that it isn’t time yet?”

“... you understand it.”

“Jang Ilso is a scary person. He must be even scarier now that he has obtained the entire Evil Faction.”

The most important thing in leading a sect wasn’t how much you make now. It was whether you could handle a crisis.

As someone who led a sect, he could fully understand Chung Myung’s feelings.

Because he had seen countless times what happens to those who are distracted by the small things in front of them and fail to prepare for the important things.

“No, I understand....”

But Im So-Byeong still seemed to have something to say.

“That is why there is the Heavenly Friends Alliance! Mount Hua doesn’t have to do it alone!”

“That has its limits.”

“Limits? If my strange strategies come together...”

“Even Zhuge Liang couldn’t stop all of Wei, so how can you?”

“No, that is...”

“If you are outnumbered, strategy is just secondary,”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“Right.”

He added.

“In any case, it is true that we have gained time. I don’t think Jang Ilso will be silent for three years. There must be a reason why he changed the mutual non-aggression here. So the given time is at most two years.”

“Right.”

“To be frank, the only one who can strengthen their power in those two years is Mount Hua. Mount Hua is full of young and talented individuals. They will be able to grow the most dramatically during this time.”

If the one leading them was Chung Myung, they would definitely show results beyond what Tang Gunak imagined.

But Im So-Byeong still seemed dissatisfied.

“Stabilizing the Yangtze River right now will be difficult without Mount Hua.”

“Do not worry about that. That is why the Heavenly Friends Alliance exists. The Tang family will step forward. If that isn’t enough, we can ask for support from the Beast Palace.”

“Ugh...”

Im So-Byeong groaned and poked his head with his fan.

“Furthermore, the small and medium sects in Sichuan, Shanxi, and the Yangtze River are hoping to establish good ties with our Alliance. If we accept them and receive help, we can fill the void left behind by Mount Hua to an extent.”

“It is true, but...”

If the Four Precepts Alliance didn’t take action directly, most other Evil Factions could be dealt with by the Tang Family. The problem wasn’t that the Evil Faction was strong, but that others could not come to the Yangtze River.

“And the Evil Faction, which has decided not to touch Plum Blossom Island... actually, it’s true that Mount Hua doesn’t have much to do. It isn’t possible to leave the island empty forever.”

“Well, you are right.”

When Im So-Byeong spoke with a sullen face, Tang Gunak looked at him expressionlessly.

“Anxious?”

“...”

Im So-Byeong’s face hardened a bit.

“I know that Green Forest isn’t trying to join us after seeing the Alliance alone. Green Forest is only looking for Mount Hua and Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon... no, it’s Mount Hua’s Sword Saint that you look at with the Heavenly Friends Alliance as a potential enemy.”

Im So-Byeong didn’t speak.

“But whether we become friends or colleagues, it isn’t right to benefit just one side. If you want to be a friend of Mount Hua, prove that you can be the sword of Mount Hua.”

“...”

“First, stabilize the Alliance and solidify the Yangtze River while Mount Hua is away. Isn’t that it?”

Im So-Byeong, who was moving his lips like he wanted to speak, glared at Tang Gunak with a cold gaze.

It wasn’t just his usual easy-going manner, but rather the aura of the King of the Green Forest.

“I think you need to understand one thing.”

“Do speak.”

“Green Forest hasn’t made the Heavenly Friends Alliance its enemy. And even if you were the enemy, no party other than Mount Hua, where the leader is, can give us any orders.”

“Of course.”

The two people looked at each other.

“Kuk.”

However, Im So-Byeong, who soon lost his energy, dropped his shoulders and sighed deeply.

“I thought I was getting some benefit, but I ended up running until my feet hurt. Oh, my fate.”

Im So-Byeong, who had been mumbling, looked back at Chung Myung.

“It is worth investing time, right?”

“What an obvious statement.”

“Ugh. Long-term investment was never my thing. Life is so short.”

Im So-Byeong, who had been calculating in his head, nodded.

“No matter how much I say, you are not the type of person to change something you have decided.”

“I know it well.”

“When you decide something, you are not the person who just accepts without calculating.”

In the end, it meant that the conclusion was decided.

Im So-Byeong sighed and said.

“I will do whatever I can to take care of the Yangtze River and the commercial sides. The Tang Family will handle the people.”

“No.”

“... Uh?”

“You need to do that too. We will do our best to handle the Evil Faction people who fight and provide relief to the Green Forest at the same time.”

“....”

Im So-Byeong, who had been staring at Chung Myung, scratched his head.

“You really expect too much. Are you asking me to protect the commercial district and also get rid of the people’s perception of the Green Forest being part of the Evil Faction?”

“That is a good chance.”

“Ugh.”

Well, this person is always harsh. There is no way he doesn’t know how hard it is.

“... I understand. I will try my best. But!”

Im So-Byeong stared at Chung Myung with determined eyes.

“I will use the influence you and Mount Hua have.”

“....”

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance without Mount Hua would never last 3 years. You should know how unnatural it is for the Tang family of the Justice Faction and the Green Forest of the Evil Faction to work together. What was nothing special where there was Mount Hua, would become a cause for war with no Mount Hua.”

Tang Gunak slowly nodded his head as if agreeing with those words. Im So-Byeong continued.

“There is a limit to what I and the Tang Family Lord can do to stop it. If the return of the Taoist is too late, there is a risk of both me and the Tang Family Lord losing power before the Alliance gathers.”

“You cannot even control one of the brats...”

“Who is to blame for it!”

“How dare you yell! Fucking idiot!”

When Chung Myung glared, Im So-Byeong felt a sting and shrugged his shoulders.

“Anyway...”

Tang Gunak stepped forward to support Im So-Byeong.

“I also agree with the Green Forest King. Even if the Tang Family and Green Forest are like that, if you leave now, we will have to completely cut off all communication with the North Sea Ice Palace. Their hostility towards the Central Plains is beyond imagination.”

“Hmm.”

“No matter how much you try to ask for cooperation, there is a high probability that they will not extend a hand to the Central Plains while Mount Hua is in a Closed Gate state. They are not helping the Heavenly Friends Alliance, but Mount Hua.”

Tang Gunak said in a slightly more subdued tone.

“And the same goes for the Beast Palace. They might reach out right away, but if the period is too long, it will be the same.”

“Uh? You two are close, though.”

“Our friendship is maintained because of you. And the Beast Palace Lord liking me and the Tang family are different issues.”

“....”

“There is only one reason I am saying this. The Heavenly Friends Alliance was possible because of Mount Hua. Without Mount Hua, the Alliance would not survive. So....”

Tang Gunak paused for a moment and looked at Chung Myung with stern eyes.

“Come back as soon as possible.”

“....”

“Of course, you will also achieve the best result and come back.”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“That is too much to ask.”

“....”

“But...”

The corners of Chung Myung’s lips curled up.

“You can look forward to it.”

His eyes shone.

“I will show you some surprising results.”

“Hahaha. That is why you are called Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. No, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. I can’t get used to it.”

“Ugh. That rotten name.”

Tang Gunak smiled broadly.

He knew there was a good reason for what Chung Myung did. So what he had to do was support Chung Myung and Mount Hua as much as he could.

“Leave the rest to me.”

“Ugh, I will lend a hand.”

Chung Myung smiled broadly.

In the past, this would have been hard to imagine. But now, there are people he could entrust with his confidence.

If he had to pick one thing that made the current Mount Hua better than the past, it would be the presence of these people.

Chung Myung held out his hand. Then, Im So-Byeong and Tang Gunak grasped each other’s hands.

Their tightly intertwined hands conveyed warm body heat.

“Please.”

Tang Gunak accepted Chung Myung’s brief words with a gentle smile, and Im So-Byeong smiled as well.

“Uh...”

Hyun Jong looked at the person sitting in front of him with a complicated feeling that was hard to explain.

“That...”

Actually, this was something that made no sense.

But, looking at those big eyes like a deer’s, he couldn’t open his mouth.

“No...”

Every time he tried to speak, those bright eyes filled with tears, and he couldn’t continue.

Hyun Jong, who couldn’t meet those eyes, slowly averted his gaze and mumbled.

“That... I want to do that, but...”

He felt like his mouth wasn’t opening properly.

‘The Closed Gate... there are rules, but bringing in an outsider...’

The gloomy face sank. When he lowered his head, the round shiny head was so clearly visible that Hyun Jong flinched once more.

When Hae Yeon’s hope died down, the disciples behind him quickly gave Baek Cheon a look.

Baek Cheon, being pushed by them, coughed slightly and opened his mouth.

“I... sect leader. Of course, it is true that outsiders are not allowed to enter the Closed Gate in principle...”

He glanced at Hae Yeon, who was sitting gloomily, and continued.

“Hae Yeon, you cannot be considered an outsider in Mount Hua, right?”

“Yes!”

“Right! Sect leader! We never thought of monk Hae Yeon as an outsider!”

“Wouldn’t it be helpful if we trained together? It is hard to find someone as strong as monk Hae Yeon.”

The disciples shouted together in unison.

Hae Yeon looked back, moved by the voices of Mount Hua disciples who were thinking of her.

“It is better to get beaten together!”

“If that guy is a savage, he will get one less punch.”

“I cannot stand him living comfortably outside by himself.”

... right, right. You devil bastards.

In any case, whether it was good or bad, it was true that the disciples of Mount Hua were helping him.

“... sect leader.”

Baek Cheon opened his mouth again after receiving support.

“As you know, monk Hae Yeon has declared that he will not return to Shaolin. Of course, that period will not last forever, but don’t you know that it would be difficult for him to return to this situation?”

“... right.”

“Hae Yeon has lived in Shaolin all his life. If he cannot stay in Shaolin or Mount Hua, he has nowhere to go. Considering that situation, I think it would be a good idea to give him some leeway this time.”

It was excellent logic and a straightforward stance.

‘As expected, it’s sasuk.’

‘He really comes in handy at times like this.’

The expression of the sect leader, who seemed to be suffering, the proud back of Baek Cheon, and the poor back of Hae Yeon, who looked like a puppy in the rain. The disciples had no doubt that these words would work.

Until a man who didn’t know what compassion was appeared.

“Very....”

A crooked voice rang out.

The disciples all shrank their necks at the voice, which made the listeners instinctively cringe.

“The sect is going to be ruined.”

“....”

What made the disciples sad was that the person who said those words wasn’t Chung Myung. If Chung Myung had said something, they would have tried to make a big deal out of it, but someone completely incapable of making a big deal out of it was glaring at them.

“E-elder...”

Hyun Young burst into laughter.

“A decision on a Closed Gate is the most important decision that can be made by the head of the sect.”

“Right.”

“Then how can the second and third-class disciples keep telling the sect leader to make exceptions here and there for this Closed Gate? The sect leader says yes, it is yes; you people are crossing the line! How dare you come in and tell the sect leader what to do?”

The heads of the Mount Hua disciples dropped.

Actually, they knew very well that what he said wasn’t so wrong. If it was another sect, it would be impossible for the disciples to dare demand something in an important matter like a Closed Gate.

“I thought you would take it for granted if you treated them well!”

When Hyun Young opened his eyes wide, the disciples all crouched down, almost prostrating.

‘Who told us to come to the sect leader in a group?’

‘I think it was Jo Gul sahyung.’

‘Tell that bastard to stay after he’s done. What? If we come in a group, they won’t be able to ignore it? I will kill them!’

They came in a group and got scolded in a group.

At that moment when everyone was sighing in resignation.

An unbelievable statement followed.

“That isn’t for you to say; it is for me!”

“....”

“Sect leader.”

Unbelievably, Hyun Young sighed.

“There are rules in a sect, but there are exceptions too. Monk Hae Yeon always fought alongside Mount Hua, risking his life numerous times. It wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that he is Mount Hua’s closest friend and guest.”

“Hmmm.”

“So I wonder if we can make an exception this time and enter the Closed Gate together,”

Mount Hua’s disciples’ eyes widened.

“Oh my... elder?”

“I thought you would say it is better to reduce a mouth to feed?”

“Of course, it doesn’t cost much to eat grass, but...”

“No. Monk Hae Yeon eats meat, right?”

“Be quiet, brat!”

The disciples were surprised and mumbled, then slowly turned their attention to Hyun Jong.

Hyun Jong looked at Hyun Young with a bit of unease on his face.

“If that is the case, why didn’t you just come forward?”

“Procedure is important. One should follow the rules.”

“... Ugh.”

Hyun Jong let out a deep sigh and spoke with Hyun Sang.

“Martial Arts hall leader.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“Has Mount Hua ever brought guests or outsiders to a Closed Gate in the past?”

“... Mount Hua had never attempted such a thing, sect leader.”

“....”

Nice. That was how their past should be. It was different from those Shaolin guys who just cursed when they were bored!

Ah, no. That isn’t important right now.

“Then what about the other sects?”

“I am good...”

“... is that so?”

It was a difficult question to decide on right away.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

When they had a headache, Chung Myung was the best.

“What do you think?”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung hesitated for a moment and then clicked his tongue, looking at Hae Yeon.

“If it is according to the rules, it won’t be allowed...”

“....”

“Tsk, it’s obvious that if you leave him outside, he will starve to death without being able to beg, so let’s just take him. If we leave him alone, a beggar will come running and feed him, but then he will die there and turn into a fucking do... no, we cannot let the beggars take him.”

“....”

“And if he goes back because of hunger to Shaolin, it will hurt my stomach. I cannot stand seeing the others do well. Oh.”

Hyun Jong smiled.

How could he be so consistently ill-tempered? He always was...

“But it is still Closed Gate...”

“It isn’t like we did anything wrong to do so; we are just going to lock ourselves up in the mountain and train, so who would find fault with that?”

“... now that I hear it, it seems to be the case.”

“And!”

At that moment, Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

“If you are going to find fault, they are probably those Nine Great Sects bastards. If those bastards even have a shred of conscience left, they shouldn’t find fault with Mount Hua. Just try it. I will run over there and pour oil on them and set it on fire! Thinking about it makes me angry! These bastards should feel that they are all equal in front of flame...”

“Woah, Chung Myung, calm down now.”

“Now, be good.”

“Good thoughts. Good thoughts alone.“

“Phew! Euk! Euk!”

Chung Myung took deep inhales several times.

“Phew, now I am calming down a bit.”

“Right. That is fortunate....”

“If anyone is nagging about the closed gates, I will open them and handle it first, Sect Leader. It would be a good idea to harass them.”

Right, Chung Myung.

That was the real thing; this Sect Leader was so happy.

Maybe this closed gate would be a blessing for the Murim world. That unbridled colt... no, it was like the Asura himself was looking up.

“Anyway, if that is the case....”

Hyun Jong sighed and looked around at everyone.

“Since everyone seems to agree, let’s go to Mount Hua together, Monk Hae Yeon.”

“T-thank you, Sect Leader!”

“Goodness, Monk! Let us go together!”

“... Is that something you should be happy about....”

“Eh. Of course it is!”

“... Amitabha.”

Hyun Jong gave a short laugh and urged.

“Then, since all the important matters have been decided, everyone, get ready. We will go.”

“Uh?”

“Right now?”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“It is said to do things in one go. There is no good in wasting time. Let us go while we are at it. Every day is important to us now.”

“Yes, Sect Leader!”

The disciples of Mount Hua got up without hesitation and rushed out. They had to hurry to pack up their things.

A small smile appeared on the lips of Hyun Jong, who was watching them.

“Then let us go.”

“Please take care of the place.”

“See you next time!”

The disciples of Mount Hua greeted the Tang family and Green Forest bandits who gathered, as well as the swarms of people who came to see them off from afar.

“Tang Family Lord.”

Hyun Jong looked at Tang Gunak with a sad look.

“I am sorry things have turned out like this.”

“Do not apologize. Alliance Leader, I know that this is not a choice for Mount Hua, but a choice for the sake of everyone.”

“....”

“You can leave the rest to us. Aren’t close friends meant to share the burdens of each other?”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes slightly at those words.

In the past, even when Mount Hua was on the verge of collapse, no one offered a helping hand. But now it was different. There were those who readily stepped forward to share the burden. They had a close friend.

Next to him, Hyun Young asked Im So-Byeong to handle the island.

“Please take good care of this place.”

“Don’t worry, elder. I will send the ledger to Mount Hua once every three months.”

“There’s no need to go that far.”

“Anything certain is good. How can someone who entrusts their money to a bandit be at ease? Do not worry. Not a single coin will go missing.”

“...thank you for the concern.”

Im So-Byeong approached Hyun Young and whispered.

“...if I make a single coin go missing, there is someone who will chase after me to kill me.”

“True. Hehehe.”

Those who smiled took a step back one by one.

“Then, I will see you again.”

When Hyun Jong bowed in front of everyone, everyone behind did the same.

“We will meet you again!”

“Please take good care of the river!”

“I will become so strong that you won’t recognize me!”

“Cheer up!”

When Mount Hua’s disciples gave a hearty greeting, the remaining members responded loudly.

“Then!”

The disciples of Mount Hua bowed their heads once again and turned.

As they slowly began to move away from the Yangtze River, Tang Gunak lightly stomped his feet.

“Uh?”

When Mount Hua disciples looked back, Tang Gunak extended his hand ahead. Everyone, in unison, simultaneously clapped their hands.

“Win and come back!”

The loud shout was filled with enthusiasm. The disciples of Mount Hua sealed their lips tight.

There was no need to answer. They had already said their goodbyes.

They simply turned their heads and hurried their steps.

“...they left.”

“Right.”

Im So-Byeong nodded and glanced at Tang Gunak as he asked.

“Why are you smiling?”

Tang Gunak, who had been twitching his lips, coughed briefly and changed his expression.

“I am already looking forward to it.”

“Uh?”

His gaze fixed on the disciples of Mount Hua, who were moving away.

“When I think about how much they will grow when they break through the Closed Gate, I get excited.”

Im So-Byeong nodded as well. He certainly agreed with those words.

“I have to work hard. I don’t want to see that devil breaking through the gate come rushing at me. I cannot handle him now, then how will I handle him then....”

“... that is a bit scary for me too.”

Tang Gunak smiled and watched Mount Hua move away until the end.

‘I will be waiting.’

The day they returned, having grown even more.

The world would truly be engraved with it.

The names of Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“... it is Mount Hua.”

“Ah, Mount Hua....”

“... we have arrived.”

The disciples of Mount Hua looked up at the peaks of Mount Hua, which rose above the clouds, with dark faces.

In the past, when they left Shanxi and returned, looking at the high peak, an inexplicable emotion welled up from deep within their hearts. In the end, they realized that their roots could only be on this barren peak.

But now, the faces of the Mount Hua disciples looking at the high cliffs of Mount Hua were nowhere to be found.

Only endless despair settled down.

“... You want me to climb that?”

“With this body?”

“... just kill me. Just... kill.”

The eyes of the Mount Hua disciples turned watery.

When they first set out from the Yangtze River, their hearts were filled with enthusiasm.

What Chung Myung told them was definitely not wrong. While fighting the Ten Thousand People Clan, the disciples of Mount Hua felt their own shortcomings. The sense of crisis that they might have to see the death of their sahyungs right next to them if things continued this way definitely stimulated them.

Thanks to that, their motivation was overflowing, and their determination was firm.
