The sword didn’t even move an inch. And the emotionless eyes beneath it.

It was surely he who was pressing down, but it was also he who felt burdened.

“Eu-euuuuuu!”

So Jang-Gye pressed down on the sword with all his might.

Kakakang!

The sword and blade continued to grind against one another, sparks flying. But that was all. There was a slight feeling of being pushed back, but the sword was still blocking his path.

And at that moment.

The lips of the damned Mount Hua disciple curled up, and at the same time, the sword facing the blade was pulled back with a bang.

The moment their tense body balance was broken, So Jang-Gye, who was pressing down on the sword with all his might, staggered forward as if being sucked in.

Kwaang!

And then, the Mount Hua swordsman, who had pulled the sword back, struck So Jang-Gye’s blade with a force incomparable to that of before.

“Kuak!”

So Jang-Gye, who had been thrusting forward, was pushed back without much thought.

The qi transmitted through the blade twisted not just his wrist but his shoulder, too. It felt as if he had been struck by lightning, and his entire arm went numb.

‘F-fuck!’

Was he being pushed? By strength?

No, it was more technique than strength. But in any case, the fact that he got pushed remained unchanged.

The incomprehensible situation made his head spin. Furthermore, the eeriness he felt from that guy made his angry blood run cold.

At that moment, a very small sound passed by So Jang-Gye’s ear as he absentmindedly retreated.

The faint ticking sound, like snow falling, pierced his ears clearly, even amidst the chaotic battlefield.

Swish!

So Jang-Gye opened his eyes wide.

The sword of the Mount Hua disciple seemed to shake lightly in the air, and at its tip, flowers like an illusion began to bloom.

‘N-no...’

He instinctively felt danger, but he wasn’t given the chance to rush forward as he stepped back to relieve the shock from the blade.

Swish!

The flowers that had bloomed in the air swayed as if hit by a wind and soon began to scatter petals in all directions.

‘T-this is...’

He had heard of it.

The sword that bloomed flowers.

A sword that once commanded the world but was now forgotten and a legend. Yet, at some point, the poisoned sword of Mount Hua began to make a name for itself again.

“P-plum blossom sword....”

Swish!

The blooming flower petals suddenly began to fly toward him.

“... fuck.”

Feeling the sensation of the thin plum blossoms digging into his body, So Jang-Gye let out a helpless sigh.

“Come together! Damn it, these guys are no joke!”

“Do not fight alone! Gather together!”

Becoming known within the Evil Sect was not as easy as it sounded. The more notorious you became, the more frequent the fights and the more work you had to do to maintain your reputation.

Those here had survived a dozen battles without ever having fought a battle of such a scale. They might have the eyes to see the grand battlefield, but their survival instincts were the best.

Those who saw the front people collapse made an instant judgment. Those who ignored the enemies as kids and fought alone were all bleeding.

Considering the fame they had, it was ridiculously fast and brutal.

‘One-on-one is a sure defeat now!’

It was absurd to have to make such a judgment, but they could not deny what they saw with their own eyes; at any given time, survival was important, not pride.

“Fight in groups of two or three!”

So, their conclusion was simple. Their opponents were definitely strong. But they had never experienced a melee like this. They thought that if they gathered together and pressured the opponents, the battlefield would become entangled. At this point, they would be at a greater advantage since they could swing blades behind their backs.

It was a great conclusion for them to come to in such a short time.

The only mistake they made was that they didn’t know who they were dealing with.

Kaang! Kaang!

Sword and sword. Sword and blade. Sword and spear.

After confirming that their opponents were formidable, the Evil Sect warriors tried to focus on blocking the sword of their opponent. They had already confirmed that if they made a fool of themselves against those swords, which dazzled people’s eyes, their necks would get cut off.

‘How is this!’

But at that moment, things that had never been imagined before began to happen.

Kakang!

The moment the sword and the blade met, this incredible force gushed out from the sword and twisted their weapons.

It wasn’t some simple strike. The sword, which had been glued to the blade as if stuck, instantly pulled their weapons to the side.

‘How?’

Just as they were about to wonder how this was being done.

Paaat!

Right under the swordsman’s arm, which twisted the blade, a tremendous amount of sword qi flew in.

“Kuak!”

The frightened ones tried to pull their bodies back, but the sword attached to the blade refused to let them withdraw their weapons.

“Ack!”

“ACkk!”

The color drained from the face of Sima Ho.

“T-this is insane... bastards...”

It was an absurd act that no one could have imagined.

The fact that the sword qi flew from behind as soon as the guys in front twisted their arms meant that the ones who stabbed their blades from behind had moved before those who twisted the sword.

The guys in the back thought that the ones ahead would naturally handle the attacks and swung their weapons.

If they had been even a little off, the sword qi wouldn’t have cut their arms but their comrades’ arms!

‘This is insane.’

The people who swung their swords without hesitation or the ones who encouraged others to swing the sword qi at themselves without hesitation.

These kids were out of their minds.

But the effect was incredible. First, those who tried to endure and change the situation fell back, leaking blood.

At that moment, the swordsmen behind them jumped over the heads of those in front and scattered sword qi.

“S-stop!”

“Damn it! What the fuck is happening!”

Those who were wounded here and there swung their weapons in a hurry without even thinking about tending to their wounds. Wasn’t it obvious what would happen if they couldn’t block the sword qi?

But the connection didn’t end there.

“Ackkk!”

“My feet!”

Those in front began to drop low and hold their legs.

A blow above the head and another low to the ground.

It was definitely not easy to block sword qi flying from two places at the same time. A combination of high and low, rather than left and right, and this was deadly with their sword qi.

‘H-how?’

They didn’t even look at one another. Not a single talk between the kids, not a single signal either.

But those insane brats were perfectly coordinated as if they had already done this before and had been performing together for years. As if they were one.

“T-this makes no sense! You damned bastard!”

It was at that moment that Sima Ho, who let out this explosive roar, saw something strange.

The ones who had been chasing them suddenly changed direction and moved to the left and right.

‘What?’

This absurd act managed to snatch away the victory the guy had secured.

Sima Ho just stared at the sight.

Swish!

And then he saw it.

At the tip of the swordsman’s sword, flowers bloomed beautifully, just like the pattern on his chest.

Those who had no choice but to retreat due to the momentum simply stared blankly at the plum blossoms blooming again. Now, there was nothing they could do. Even though they knew full well how terrifying the blooming flowers could be.

“... Mount Hua.”

The vivid flower petals were flying everywhere they could see. Sima Ho realized that each and every leaf and petal was made of sword qi and let out a hollow laugh.

‘You can deal with Mount Hua at any time? That insane bastard Gal Cheon-Rip...’

If there were a hundred people who used such an insane sword technique, that place would be no different from hell beyond the underworld. Their fates were determined from the moment they stepped into this hell.

The blooming flower petals flew in at once.

‘How beautiful.’

The Plum Blossom Rain, which filled the sky, began to pour down mercilessly, digging into the bodies of the wounded men.

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes widened.

Pain throbbed from the wounds all over his body, but at that moment, he didn’t even feel it.

“... what the hell.”

Mount Hua’s disciples were one-sidedly attacking the Evil Sect members?

They were striking the enemies with their sword qi like lightning, using perfect coordination to overwhelm them. Mount Hua’s special qi, the Plum Blossom Sword qi, was blooming in all directions like beautiful, gorgeous flowers.

“Oh my...”

Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t help but be shocked as he watched the Evil Sect members helplessly fall, dripping blood.

He was prepared to die, pinning all his hopes on Mount Hua. But wasn’t it usually difficult to turn an expectation into reality?

In truth, what he really expected was for Mount Hua to arrive early and join hands with them to fight on equal terms. He had never imagined that Mount Hua would attack them on their own and single-handedly dominate like this.

For Hong Dae-Kwang, who knew every detail about who and what kind of people those Evil Sect members were, even having expectations felt absurd.

But right there, right before his eyes, something he had never expected had turned into reality.

‘What on earth have they been doing for the past 3 years, these crazy bastards...’

The faces of those who had been young and innocent had now transformed into the faces of seasoned swordsmen.

Flutter!

They pushed away their opponents with incredibly quick swords and deflected their opponents’ counterattacks with skillful, soft sword movements. And when the distance was just right, the Mount Hua Sect’s illusion bloomed splendidly.

“This is Mount Hua...”

Mount Hua, who once pushed away even the Wudang and took the title of the best sword sect in the world, was now roaring at the world from there.

Clench.

Hong Dae-Kwang clenched his fists. His face was hot, and something surged through his chest.

Unable to hold back the overwhelming emotions, he shouted loudly.

“You are doing good! Beat them! Those damned bastards!”

“...”

Blood drained from the face of Gal Cheon-rip due to this unexpected situation.

“Big bro!”

“....”

“Big bro! Shouldn’t we do something?”

“...”

“Big bro!”

Gal Cheon-rip flinched for a moment and looked around. Dam Hae was watching him, waiting. Not just him, but his other younger brothers who surrounded and protected him looked anxious since Mount Hua arrived.

“If we leave, we will fall apart!”

Gal Cheon-rip bit his lip.

‘Damn it.’

With red eyes, he scanned the battlefield. That kid... no, now the Mount Hua swordsmen, who couldn’t even be called kids anymore, were one-sidedly slaughtering the best warriors he had brought.

The Evil Sect, overwhelmed by the momentum and unable to hold their ground, got pushed back further and further, retreating.

It was a sight that couldn’t be understood.

‘How could this happen...’

The situation should have been the opposite.

No matter how much they were the disciples of a known sect, those here were not ones to be crushed by such fame. Rather, those young kids who had never experienced a battle properly should have been crushed by the weight of the battlefield, where people’s heads were blown off, and arms were cut off right in front of them.

If one couldn’t withstand the weight, wasn’t a battlefield a place you wouldn’t be able to even display half your abilities?

But...

“Eik!”

“G-get back! Those damn petals are moving again.”

“ACK!”

The ones who were lost in thought and unable to display their abilities were the Evil Sect.

No matter how much they thought about it, this made no sense.

Those who gained fame and skill through countless battles in the world of murim for decades were being defeated so miserably by young people who hadn’t even lived half their lives.

On top of that, those evil-looking Mount Hua people didn’t let their guard down at all while watching the scene; they continued to release their strange sword qi.

‘Is this a prestigious sect?’

No, it couldn’t be.

If all the Justice faction sects were capable of this, there would have been no such fierce division between the Justice and Evil factions. This wasn’t the power of a Justice faction but the power of Mount Hua.

‘We lost too much momentum at first. Those damned bastards!’

At the first moment of the clash, several people were beheaded before they could even raise a hand.

It was this cruel hand that went beyond boldness.

Even though it was a temporary alliance, those who saw their comrades being brutally treated right in front of them were naturally terrified...

Gal Cheon-rip, who had been anxiously lost in thought, suddenly was wide-eyed.

‘Isn’t this what we were going to do?’

“This..!”

He bit his lips tightly to suppress the anger.

“Big bro!”

Another anxious voice came.

Gal Cheon-rip gathered his mind. This wasn’t the time to analyze things. Once they lose the momentum, things would become really uncomfortable if they didn’t recover.

He shouted loudly, using his experience to help.

“Iron Ghost! Blood Blade Killer! Mount He Spear Ghost! Soul Breaking Killer! How long will you be watching this!”

There was only one way to revive this broken momentum: to crush them with more force. The force of the battlefield always changed with the actions of a few skilled warriors.

“You people join in! Before this gets out of hand, let those little bastards know how high the sky is!”

“Yes! Big bro!”

Kang Seo-Chil, who was guarding him, quickly rushed ahead.

“Damn it...”

Sweat dripped down Gal Cheon-rip’s palms.

This wasn’t going to happen. All he had to do was clean up Xi’an, empty the vaults of Eunha merchants, and head to Qinghai.

Then why did things get so messed up?

‘No! Not yet!’

But it’s not that bad yet. If he just took care of those damned people, everything would go according to plan.

‘Absolutely!’

The sight of those flying in front of him was clear in the eyes of Gal Cheon-rip.

Paat!

The tip of a sword dug into a shoulder like lightning.

‘Ack!’

A terrible scream burst from the mouth of the person whose shoulder was pierced in an instant. At that moment, Mount Hua’s swordsmen twisted and pulled out the sword that was stabbed in the shoulder, then stabbed him again without a moment’s hesitation, slamming it into the opponent’s dantian.

“Kuaak...”

The person clutching his lower stomach had despair in his eyes. If his dantian was destroyed, he could never live as a warrior again. This was a punishment worse than death for him.

Thud.

The one who was kneeling fell forward.

The foot of Jin Tae, the third-class disciple of Mount Hua who subdued the opponent, dug into the opponent’s chest.

Kwang!

He kicked the man backward and caught his breath.

The smell of blood rushed to his nose. In addition, the heat from the bodies struggling to live, the screams, the cracking sounds of weapons—he felt everything vividly with his body.

But his eyes only grew calmer with each moment.

Swish!

At that moment, a blue-green qi of a blade flew toward his head. Jin Tae, who confirmed the blade qi rushing, looked at the ground.

‘Nope.’

This was all he had to handle. His back was supported by the sahyungs.

He grasped the situation clearly and instead jumped ahead. The blade was originally a weapon that crushed the opponent with strength.

But,

Kaang!

The tip of the plum blossom sword that Jin Tae swung hit right near the handle of the blade, causing it to fly. The moment the blade bounced back, the opponent’s face was filled with shock.

So easy.

There was no reason to engage with someone relying solely on brute strength. If you targeted the point where their strength was misapplied, both the blade and the person could be deflected even with a slender sword.

As soon as a weapon lost its strength, a series of incredible stabs followed, utilizing qi.

‘This isn’t it!’

A battlefield where blood splashed and flesh was torn?

This was hardly worth noticing.

The training they had undergone was a hundred times more intense and brutal than this.

Killing intent?

For someone who had faced that bastard’s killing intent head-on, this was barely a tickle to their instincts. He didn’t even sense any hostility from those they considered opponents.

So why be afraid?

He was a survivor who lived even after dealing with that damned bastard. To him, their momentum was so gentle it made him yawn, and their attacks were easily identifiable.

Jin Tae tried to calmly sever the opponent’s tendons, but then.

At that moment.

Paat!

A sword flew towards his face.

“Euk!”

Jin Tae’s waist snapped back.

‘When?’

He confirmed there were no other enemies around. The sahyungs wouldn’t have missed their opponent, so where on earth did this sword come from?

The sword, which passed right in front of his face, spun in the air and then descended towards his neck like a poisonous snake pursuing its prey.

A desperate situation.

But Jin Tae didn’t panic. He swung his sword upwards, even though he was off balance. The tremendous impact of the sword meeting the sword made his shoulders tremble.

“Kuak!”

Even though he had used only half his strength, it wasn’t easy to handle the impact. So he had to acknowledge it. This was different from those he faced so far. Jin Tae quickly spun his body and tried to escape the opponent’s range.

But.

Swish!

Once the opponent’s sword gained momentum, it wouldn’t release him so easily. It persistently pursued him, trying to prevent him from adjusting his form.

“This...!”

Jin Tae gritted his teeth.

He instinctively felt that this could be dangerous if he moved clumsily, so he prepared to sacrifice one side and steadied his sword.

It was then.

Kwang!

Something flashed before his eyes, and the sword pursuing him was knocked back. Jin Tae glanced at the person who stood in front of him. For a moment, relief spread across his face.

“I will handle this guy, sahyung.”

Jin Tae nodded without a word.

In Mount Hua, what was important wasn’t the ranks, but skill. It was natural that the stronger one fought the stronger opponent.

And the one standing in front of him was definitely stronger, even though they joined later.

“Hmm.”

The evil sect person flicked the sword as if his wrist was tingling and looked at the Mount Hua swordsman in front of him with a slightly tense expression.

The force of the sword and a stable form without disturbance.

She was the epitome of a swordsman.

“As someone who uses the same sword as a weapon, let us at least exchange names. I am the Nine Hold Swordsman, Hwang Seong. What is your name?”

“I don’t have a name to tell you.”

“...”

At that moment, a bright light shone in Hwang Seong’s eyes.

“This damned woman...”

The Mount Hua swordsman raised her sword and aimed it at his neck. Hwang Seong clenched his teeth at the cold and sharp force.

“Someone who uses the same sword?”

A cold voice, like the wind of the North Sea, escaped her lips.

“That is an insult to my sword. The sword you use is not the same as the sword I use.”

“....”

“Know this and die. This is the sword of Mount Hua.”

A proud swordsman of Mount Hua, with the spirit of Mount Hua raging from the tip of her sword.

Tang Soso, who had grown stronger over the three years, charged at the swordsman with her sharp gaze.

Kakakang!

Hwang Seong’s face twisted in pain.

Paat!

“Kuak!”

The sword qi, like lightning, swiftly swept past his face. The skin on his chin split open, creating a long, red gash from chin to cheek.

‘Damn it!’

This was not a sword technique learned half-heartedly. It was a technique mastered by a swordsman who had swung the sword so often that they couldn’t tell if the sword was in their hand or an extension of it.

How could he accept that a woman, who appeared much younger than him, could wield sword qi that made his own sword seem insignificant?

“This..!”

As more wounds appeared on his body, Hwang Seong’s face showed signs of exhaustion.

Whoever this damned woman was, the fact remained that she was cornering him and inflicting slashes.

He let out a roar and swept his sword horizontally. The fiercely rising sword qi surged forward like a torrent of water pouring from a broken embankment.

“Take this...”

Prrr!

But then his eyes widened.

Suddenly, red flower petals bloomed before him like clouds, covering the entire area where his sword qi extended. The sword qi, not reaching its full length, collided with the flowers and was pushed back a long way.

“Kuak!”

Unable to move forward or backward, his internal qi turned his insides upside down. Blood gushed from his throat.

‘Damn it!’

This couldn’t be happening.

He was a famous swordsman, renowned throughout the world since before the young woman in front of him was born. Was he being pushed back in a sword fight and spitting blood?

This was absolutely impossible. Absolutely not.

“This fucking bitch!”

Hwang Seong pulled his sword back slightly. Soon, seven rays of black sword qi began to spread out.

An incredibly quick sword strike that sent victims to the afterlife without them even realizing their own death.

It was the moment when the poisonous sword technique, Deadly Twelve Thunders, was unleashed.

But.

Tat!

As soon as the sword was drawn, Tang Soso kicked the ground and stepped back. The sword of Hwang Seong, which had been rushing ahead at incredible speed, was withdrawn just an inch before Tang Soso’s neck.

‘What...’

The Nine Hold Swordsman glared.

Coincidence? Or not?

‘This is ridiculous.’

That young woman had never seen his sword technique before. So how could she predict instantly how far the sword she was seeing for the first time would extend and retreat exactly that distance?

Was that possible? That too in the middle of such a fierce battle?

No, even if it was possible, wouldn’t her neck be pierced if her calculations were even a bit off? What kind of guts did she have to stop it?

‘Is she even in her right mind?’

At that moment.

The eyes of the swordsman who was pulling back the sword and the emotionless eyes of Tang Soso met for a moment.

Creep!

He felt goosebumps rise through his body for a moment.

The eyes, which didn’t contain a shred of emotion, were watching him like a snake hunting its prey. As if they wouldn’t miss even the slightest movement.

‘This... this...!’

Hwang Seong gritted his teeth and shouted.

He couldn’t admit that momentary chill down the spine came from a child’s eyes, and he couldn’t dare admit that a young swordsman had read his moves.

“DIEEEEE!”

As an evil scream burst from his mouth, 12 flashes came through from his sword.

Stronger, faster.

The sword attacks, which he mustered with all his might, turned into 12 lightning bolts and rushed to Tang Soso.

‘There is no use stepping back.’

He wasn’t going to suffer the same result again. If she decided to do the same thing, her throat would...

It was then.

Tat!

Tang Soso spun around on the spot and jumped up lightly. The sword qi pouring down towards her body collided with her sword, which she had briefly swung.

At that moment, Tang Soso used the recoil to fly higher.

‘Idiot!’

That was the worst move because they couldn’t move their body freely in the air.

‘I will pierce you like a skewer!’

He secretly cheered and tried to deploy the same sword technique on Tang Soso, who was rising into the air.

But.

Prrrr!

With a sound like a dragonfly flapping its wings, the red sword qi bloomed from the tip of her sword.

One flower, another flower, and soon a dozen flowers.

Hwang Seong forgot that he was about to unfold a technique and just stared at that sight.

The red flowers, which had bloomed in profusion, covered even the body of Tang Soso. The vast sky that he could see was covered by the flowers.

And.

Wheik!

The blooming flowers suddenly scattered and began to rain down on the world like rain.

Rain of red flowers.

“Sky...”

At that moment, a thought struck through his mind.

A symbol of the Sichuan Tang family.

A legendary martial arts technique, which was handed down in the Tang family, had unfolded here and looked like the sky was raining flowers.

“Rain of Thousand... Flowers.”

Amidst the flower rain, which poured down with nowhere to run, the vivid green threat on the tip of Tang Soso’s sword was briefly visible. A voice, as if groaning, came from his mouth.

“Tang family...”

The flower rain swept through his entire body mercilessly and dazzlingly. The entire sky was covered with flower petals.

“... damn it.”

Those were the last words he said in the world.

“Ackkkk!”

A desperate scream echoed through the air.

“That...”

Wei Lishan’s mouth was open.

The swordsmen of Mount Hua were fiercely attacking the enemies, who seemed to be three times stronger. He could clearly see those who got pushed back by Mount Hua’s sword were getting cut down.

“... oh my.”

This was the kind of time when the word one-sided would get used.

And the one-sided nature of the battlefield brought Wei Lishan more shock than joy.

He knew their skills best, having experienced them firsthand.

How could those who were fearsome enough to be called the Gods of death when he faced them directly get pushed back so helplessly?

Wei Lishan suddenly looked around.

As expected, the other sect leaders in Xi’an were also looking with the same bewildered expressions.

“M-Mount Hua...”

“Like that...”

It had to be so.

Even Wei Lishan, who was proud to be one of the people who knew Mount Hua’s strength best, couldn’t help but be shocked at the current appearance of Mount Hua. How much more so for the others?

Paat!

The red qi of Mount Hua’s sword technique filled the sky.

“That...”

Someone’s half-hearted voice trailed off.

“We should help too...”

Laughter erupted around them.

“Help that?”

“...n-no, even so...”

“With our skills, it would be more of a hindrance than help. Helping has to be at the right level, too.”

“...right.”

Everyone stared blankly at Mount Hua’s swordsmen. Some gulped. They couldn’t help but bite their tongues, watching the swords draw flowers in the air.

‘No matter what, I thought they would be difficult to compare with Southern Edge.’

The fundamental limitations of Mount Hua.

The fact that the disciples of this sect were young and inexperienced.

That was why they thought so. Even if they didn’t know what would happen in a few decades, they believed they wouldn’t be able to keep up with Southern Edge, who was fiercely fighting back with their great disciples and elders.

But the scene happening here was enough to shatter their perception.

And.

“Ackkkk!”

The long-suffering men of Xi’an clenched their fists, watching the sight of Hwang Seong being enveloped in red petals and screaming.

‘It seems pointless to discuss the future. They can face the Southern Edge right now.’

‘I didn’t know Mount Hua would become this strong...’

An indescribable sense of excitement and pride surged through their hearts. Those who had once been looked down upon had grown dazzlingly.

At that moment, Wei Lishan limped forward.

“Sect leader...”

Then Hyun Jong, who had been carrying a pouch on his back, observed the situation and turned his head to Wei Lishan.

“Clan leader Wei. Are you alright?”

“...Yes. I am alright, sect leader.”

“We were too late. Forgive us.”

“How can you say that, sect leader? Just the fact that you came here is enough to move my heart as a disciple.”

Hyun Jong gently patted Wei Lishan on the shoulder with warm eyes.

“You have had a hard time.”

Wei Lishan felt emotional for a moment and then glanced around the battlefield. It felt strange to be having such a conversation amidst such a fierce battle.

However, it wasn’t his fault. Hyun Jong’s relaxed attitude, which seemed to show no concern even though his disciples were fighting, made him relax.

Wei Lishan hesitated for a moment and then asked.

“Are you alright?”

“What?”

“They have gained so much momentum. If there is anything we can do to help them...”

“It is alright.”

“But...”

“It is alright.”

Hyun Jong shook his head with a faint smile.

“They are not the type of kids to cause problems to that extent. So just trust them and keep watch.”

“Ah...”

Wei Lishan hesitated as if he had more to say but soon nodded after seeing Hyun Jong’s calm face.

This was because he felt Hyun Jong’s firm trust in the disciples.

In the past, Hyun Jong was a person who couldn’t hide his anxiety even when the disciples were facing evil sect members who were stronger. But now, he didn’t show anxiety even though the disciples were facing an extremely powerful enemy.

Has Hyun Jong changed? Or...

“Is it strange?”

“Ah...”

Hyun Jong asked with a slight smile, perhaps noticing Wei Lishan’s feelings. Wei Lishan flinched and lowered his head.

“I am sorry. It is difficult for a disciple to guess the sect leader’s intentions...”

“It just naturally happened that way.”

“... Uh?”

Instead of answering right away, Hyun Jong stared blankly at the battlefield.

The Plum Blossom Sword beautifully colored the battlefield, but what they saw on the outside could not be everything. Inside, it must be a dangerous and fearful place where lives are at stake.

But.

“Anyone who has watched the time these kids have spent would feel the same. To be so anxious about this would be to ignore those kids and the time they have spent.”

Upon hearing those words, Wei Lishan looked around the battlefield with a blank face.

‘What...’

Hyun Jong’s words were filled with utmost certainty. So, he couldn’t help but wonder what kind of training they had been doing all this time.

“You don’t need to worry.”

At that moment, Hyun Jong’s calm voice pierced his ears.

“Because Mount Hua is strong.”

Wei Lishan couldn’t say anything for a moment. Indescribable emotions swirled in his chest and surged up.

How long had he waited to hear these words?

Words he thought he might never hear in his life finally came out of Hyun Jong’s mouth.

‘Sect leader...’

The disciples of Mount Hua were incredibly strong.

And the sect leader of Mount Hua, who looked extremely serious and dependable.

Anyone who used the name Mount Hua, whether they were a disciple of the main sect or a disciple of the sub-sects, would feel the same emotions.

“And.”

“... Uh?”

At that moment, Hyun Jong gestured to someone with his chin.

“If he had felt even a little bit of danger, he would have already gone wild. But since he is holding back, it is still far from dangerous.”

Where he pointed, there was the back of a person. The figure had arms crossed behind.

Just by looking at that waving hair tied up, you couldn’t help but recognize who that person was.

‘Taoist Chung Myung.’

Looking at that firm back, flawless and strong, Wei Lishan bit his lips without realizing it.
